WARDEN: Endangered Indigenous Language Transcription and Translation with 6 Hours of Training Data
Authors: Ziheng Zhang, Yunzhong Hou, Naijing Liu, Liang Zheng
First: 2026-05-13T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: https://github.com/Ziheng-Zhang-AUS/WARDEN
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper introduces WARDEN, an early language model system capable of transcribing and translating Wardaman, an endangered Australian indigenous language into English. The significant challenge we face is the lack of large-scale training data: in fact, we only have 6 hours of annotated audio. Therefore, while it is common practice to train a single model for transcription and translation using large datasets (like English to French), this practice is no longer viable in the Wardaman to English context. To tackle the low-resource challenge, we design WARDEN to have separate transcription and translation models: WARDEN first turns a Wardaman audio input into phonemic transcription, and then the transcription into English translation. Further, we propose two useful techniques to enhance performance. For transcription, we initialize the Wardaman token from Sundanese, a language that shares similar phonemes with Wardaman, to accelerate fine-tuning of the transcription model. For translation, we compile a Wardaman-English dictionary from expert annotations, and provide this domain-specific knowledge to a large language model (LLM) to reason and decide the final output. We empirically demonstrate that this two-stage design works better than data-hungry unified approaches in extremely low data settings. Using a mere 6 hours of annotated data, WARDEN outperforms larger open-source and proprietary models and establishes a strong baseline. Data and code are available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WARDEN：基于6小时训练数据的濒危澳大利亚原住民语言转录与翻译
本文介绍了WARDEN，一个能够将濒危的澳大利亚原住民语言瓦尔达曼语转录并翻译为英语的早期语言模型系统。我们面临的重大挑战是缺乏大规模训练数据：实际上，我们仅有6小时的标注音频。因此，虽然在英语到法语等大语料场景中训练单一模型进行转录和翻译是常见做法，但在瓦尔达曼语到英语的场景中已不可行。为应对低资源挑战，我们设计了WARDEN，使其拥有独立的转录和翻译模型：WARDEN首先将瓦尔达曼语音频输入转录为音素转录，再将转录结果翻译为英语。此外，我们提出了两种提升性能的技术：转录阶段，我们以与瓦尔达曼语音素相似的巽他语初始化瓦尔达曼语标记，以加速转录模型的微调；翻译阶段，我们从专家标注中编译瓦尔达曼语-英语词典，并将该领域知识提供给大型语言模型（LLM）以推理并决定最终输出。实验证明，在极端低数据环境下，这种两阶段设计优于数据密集型的统一方法。仅用6小时标注数据，WARDEN便超越了现有开源和专有模型，建立了坚实基线。数据与代码已公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of training data for endangered languages like Wardaman, this work introduces WARDEN, a two-stage system that first transcribes audio into phonemic text and then translates it into English. To overcome the extreme data limitation—only 6 hours of annotated audio—the system decouples transcription and translation, initializing the transcription model with Sundanese due to phonetic similarity and enhancing translation with a custom Wardaman-English dictionary fed into a large language model. Experimental results show that this architecture significantly outperforms unified models trained on large datasets, establishing a strong baseline with minimal data.
为应对濒危语言如瓦尔达曼语训练数据稀缺的问题，本研究提出WARDEN系统，采用分两阶段处理：先将音频转录为音素文本，再将其翻译为英语。为解决仅有6小时标注数据的限制，系统以语音相近的巽他语初始化转录模型，并通过专家编纂的瓦尔达曼-英语词典增强翻译模型推理能力。实验表明，该模块化方法在极低数据量下优于传统统一模型，性能达到当前最优水平。
EVA-Bench: A New End-to-end Framework for Evaluating Voice Agents
Authors: Tara Bogavelli, Gabrielle Gauthier Melançon, Katrina Stankiewicz, Oluwanifemi Bamgbose, Fanny Riols, Hoang H. Nguyen, Raghav Mehndiratta, Lindsay Devon Brin, Joseph Marinier, Hari Subramani, Anil Madamala, Sridhar Krishna Nemala, Srinivas Sunkara
First: 2026-05-13T17:58:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:58:52+00:00
Comments: Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Voice agents, artificial intelligence systems that conduct spoken conversations to complete tasks, are increasingly deployed across enterprise applications. However, no existing benchmark jointly addresses two core evaluation challenges: generating realistic simulated conversations, and measuring quality across the full scope of voice-specific failure modes. We present EVA-Bench, an end-to-end evaluation framework that addresses both. On the simulation side, EVA-Bench orchestrates bot-to-bot audio conversations over dynamic multi-turn dialogues, with automatic simulation validation that detects user simulator error and appropriately regenerates conversations before scoring. On the measurement side, EVA-Bench introduces two composite metrics: EVA-A (Accuracy), capturing task completion, faithfulness, and audio-level speech fidelity; and EVA-X (Experience), capturing conversation progression, spoken conciseness, and turn-taking timing. Both metrics apply to different agent architectures, enabling direct cross-architecture comparison. EVA-Bench includes 213 scenarios across three enterprise domains, a controlled perturbation suite for accent and noise robustness, and pass@1, pass@k, pass^k measurements that distinguish peak from reliable capability. Across 12 systems spanning all three architectures, we find: (1) no system simultaneously exceeds 0.5 on both EVA-A pass@1 and EVA-X pass@1; (2) peak and reliable performance diverge substantially (median pass@k - pass^k gap of 0.44 on EVA-A); and (3) accent and noise perturbations expose substantial robustness gaps, with effects varying across architectures, systems, and metrics (mean up to 0.314). We release the full framework, evaluation suite, and benchmark data under an open-source license.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EVA-Bench：一种用于评估语音代理的全新端到端框架
语音代理是通过口语对话完成任务的人工智能系统，正越来越多地部署于企业应用中。然而，目前尚无任何基准测试能同时解决两大核心评估挑战：生成逼真的模拟对话，以及衡量语音特有失败模式的全面质量。我们提出了EVA-Bench，一种端到端评估框架，同时应对这两方面问题。在模拟方面，EVA-Bench通过动态多轮对话协调机器人之间的音频对话，并采用自动模拟验证机制检测用户模拟器错误，必要时重新生成对话后再评分。在测量方面，EVA-Bench引入两个综合指标：EVA-A（准确性），涵盖任务完成度、忠实度和音频级语音保真度；EVA-X（体验），涵盖对话进展、口语简洁性和轮次切换时机。两个指标均适用于不同代理架构，支持跨架构直接比较。EVA-Bench包含三个企业领域共213个场景，一套用于评估口音和噪声鲁棒性的受控扰动套件，以及pass@1、pass@k、pass^k测量方法，以区分峰值与可靠能力。在涵盖三种架构的12个系统中，我们发现：（1）无系统在EVA-A pass@1和EVA-X pass@1两项指标上同时超过0.5；（2）峰值与可靠性能差异显著（EVA-A的median pass@k - pass^k差距达0.44）；（3）口音和噪声扰动暴露了显著的鲁棒性差距，其影响因架构、系统和指标而异（平均达0.314）。我们将在开源许可下发布完整框架、评估套件和基准数据。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for comprehensive evaluation of voice agents in enterprise settings, EVA-Bench introduces an end-to-end framework that simulates realistic multi-turn conversations and measures performance across voice-specific failure modes. It uses bot-to-bot audio dialogues with automated validation and regeneration to ensure fidelity, and defines two composite metrics—EVA-A for task accuracy and speech quality, and EVA-X for conversational fluency and timing. Evaluated across 12 systems in three architectures and 213 enterprise scenarios, results show no system achieves over 0.5 on both EVA-A and EVA-X pass@1, significant gaps between peak and reliable performance (median 0.44 on EVA-A), and substantial robustness differences under accent and noise perturbations (mean impact up to 0.314).
为解决现有评估基准无法同时模拟真实对话并衡量语音代理多种失败模式的问题，EVA-Bench提出了一种端到端评估框架，通过机器人间音频对话模拟多轮真实对话，并引入自动验证与重生成机制。该框架定义两个综合指标：EVA-A衡量任务完成度、忠实度与语音保真度，EVA-X衡量对话进展、简洁性与轮次时机。在12个跨三种架构的系统上测试213个企业场景后发现：无系统同时在EVA-A与EVA-X的pass@1上超过0.5；峰值性能与稳定性能差异显著（EVA-A中位数差距达0.44）；且对口音与噪声的鲁棒性差异大，系统间、架构间、指标间平均性能下降达0.314。
What is Learnable in Valiant's Theory of the Learnable?
Authors: Steve Hanneke, Anay Mehrotra, Grigoris Velegkas, Manolis Zampetakis
First: 2026-05-13T17:58:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:58:46+00:00
Comments: Abstract shortened for arXiv
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Valiant's 1984 paper is widely credited with introducing the PAC learning model, but it, in fact, introduced a different model: unlike PAC learning, the learner receives only positives, may issue membership queries, and must output a hypothesis with no false positives. Prior work characterized variants, including the case without queries. We revisit Valiant's original model and ask: *Which classes are learnable in it?* For every finite domain, including Valiant's Boolean-hypercube setting, we show that a class is learnable if and only if every realizable positive sample can be certified by a poly-size adaptive query-compression scheme. This is a new variant of sample compression where the learner certifies samples via a short interaction with the membership oracle. Our characterization shows that learnability in Valiant's model is strictly sandwiched between learnability in the PAC model and the variant of Valiant's model without membership queries. This is one of the rare cases where introducing membership queries changes the set of learnable classes, and not just the sample or computational complexity. Next, we study the natural extension of the model to arbitrary domains. While we do not obtain an exact characterization, our techniques readily generalize and show that the same strict sandwiching persists. Finally, we show that $d$-dimensional halfspaces, which are not learnable without queries, are learnable with queries: we give a $\mathrm{poly}(d) \tilde{O}(1/ε)$ sample and $\mathrm{poly}(d) \mathrm{polylog}(1/ε)$ query algorithm, and prove that at least $Ω(d)$ samples or queries are necessary. To our knowledge, this is the first algorithm for halfspaces in Valiant's model. Together, these results uncover a surprisingly rich theory behind Valiant's original notion of learnability and introduce ideas that may be of independent interest in learning theory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Valiant学习理论中可学习的内容是什么？
Valiant1984年的论文被广泛认为引入了PAC学习模型，但实际上引入的是另一种模型：与PAC学习不同，学习者仅接收正例，可发起成员查询，且必须输出无假正例的假设。先前工作已刻画了若干变体，包括无查询的情形。我们重新审视Valiant的原始模型并提出问题：*哪些类在该模型中可学习？* 对于每个有限域（包括Valiant的布尔超立方体设定），我们证明：一个类可学习当且仅当每个可实现的正例样本均可通过多项式大小的自适应查询压缩方案予以认证。这是一种新的样本压缩变体，其中学习者通过与成员查询器的短交互来认证样本。我们的刻画表明，Valiant模型中的可学习性严格介于PAC模型与无成员查询的Valiant模型变体之间。这是罕见的引入成员查询会改变可学习类集合的情形，而不仅影响样本或计算复杂度。 接下来，我们研究该模型在任意域上的自然扩展。虽然我们未获得精确刻画，但我们的技术可直接推广，表明相同的严格夹层关系依然存在。最后，我们证明：在有查询情况下，d维半空间（无查询时不可学习）是可学习的：我们给出一个样本复杂度为poly(d)·˜O(1/ε)、查询复杂度为poly(d)·polylog(1/ε)的算法，并证明至少需要Ω(d)个样本或查询。据我们所知，这是首个在Valiant模型中针对半空间的算法。 综上，这些结果揭示了Valiant原始可学习性概念背后惊人丰富的理论，并引入了可能对学习理论具有独立价值的思想。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by Valiant's original 1984 learning model, which differs from PAC by allowing only positive examples and membership queries while requiring zero false positives, this paper investigates which concept classes are learnable under this framework. The authors introduce a novel sample compression scheme using adaptive queries to certify positive samples, showing that a class is learnable if and only if such a poly-size compression exists. Their characterization reveals that Valiant’s model lies strictly between PAC and its query-free variant, demonstrating that membership queries can alter the set of learnable classes. They further extend the model to arbitrary domains, preserving this sandwiching, and provide the first algorithm for learning d-dimensional halfspaces in Valiant’s model, achieving poly(d) polylog(1/ε) queries and samples, with matching lower bounds showing Ω(d) queries are necessary.
受Valiant1984年原始学习模型（与PAC不同，仅接收正例并允许查询）启发，本文刻画了可学习类为：每个可实现正例均可通过多项式大小的自适应查询压缩方案认证。该刻画表明，该模型严格介于PAC模型与无查询变体之间，证明查询扩展了可学习类集合。进一步，作者证明d维半空间在有查询时可学习，给出poly(d)样本与polylog(1/ε)查询的算法，并证明至少需Ω(d)样本或查询，这是首次在该模型下解决半空间学习问题。
Mechanism Design for Decentralized Risk Detection: Strict Propriety, Network Coalitions, and the Backfiring Mandat
Authors: Jian Ni, Lecheng Zheng, John R Birge
First: 2026-04-23T15:46:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:58:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Competing firms that share a population of risky customers face a decentralized risk detection problem in which each firm holds fragmentary information whose aggregation would generate social value, but private incentives impede truthful sharing. We develop a dynamic mechanism design framework for this setting and identify three strategic frictions that distinguish it from classical mechanism design with decentralized information: compliance moral hazard, adversarial adaptation, and information destruction through intervention. A temporal value assignment (TVA) mechanism credits firms using a strictly proper scoring rule applied to discounted verified outcomes; under stated assumptions, TVA implements truthful posterior reporting as a Bayes--Nash equilibrium (uniquely optimal at each edge in large federations, with $O(1/m)$ shading in finite systems). A network Shapley characterization shows that under edge-additive coalition value, each firm's marginal contribution is proportional to its weighted cross-firm interaction degree, yielding a sharp prescription for coalition design that prioritizes inter-firm volume over firm size. Embedding TVA in a model of competition among firms, we establish a welfare ordering across four regulatory regimes (autarky, voluntary federation, mandated full sharing, TVA) and identify conditions under which information-sharing mandates without compatible incentive design reduce welfare below autarky: a ``backfiring mandate.'' We illustrate the framework on a 1.4M-transaction synthetic anti-money-laundering benchmark; the same machinery extends to platform fraud, cybersecurity threat intelligence, and supply chain risk detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：去中心化风险检测的机制设计：严格恰当性、网络联盟与适得其反的强制令
共享高风险客户群体的竞争企业面临去中心化风险检测问题：每家企业掌握部分信息，若加以整合将产生社会价值，但私人激励阻碍了真实信息共享。我们为此情境构建了动态机制设计框架，并识别出三个区别于经典去中心化信息机制设计的战略摩擦：合规道德风险、对抗性适应以及通过干预导致的信息破坏。时间价值分配（TVA）机制采用贴现验证结果的严格恰当评分规则向企业赋值；在给定假设下，TVA实现后验真实报告作为贝叶斯-纳什均衡（在大型联邦中每个节点唯一最优，有限系统中为O(1/m)的偏差）。网络沙普利特征表明，在边加性联盟价值下，每家企业的边际贡献与其加权跨企业交互度成正比，从而为联盟设计提供明确指导：优先考虑企业间交互量而非企业规模。将TVA嵌入企业竞争模型，我们确立了四种监管制度（自给自足、自愿联盟、强制完全共享、TVA）的福利排序，并识别出在缺乏兼容激励设计时，强制信息共享反而降低福利（即“适得其反的强制令”）的条件。我们在140万笔交易的合成反洗钱基准上验证了该框架；相同方法可扩展至平台欺诈、网络安全威胁情报及供应链风险检测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by decentralized risk detection among competing firms with fragmented customer data, this paper develops a dynamic mechanism design framework that addresses three strategic frictions: compliance moral hazard, adversarial adaptation, and information destruction. It introduces a temporal value assignment (TVA) mechanism using a strictly proper scoring rule on discounted verified outcomes, which incentivizes truthful reporting as a Bayes-Nash equilibrium. Experimental results show TVA achieves near-optimal truth-telling with minimal shading in large federations and provides a network Shapley-based coalition design prioritizing inter-firm interaction volume. When embedded in a competitive model, TVA outperforms autarky and mandates without compatible incentives, revealing that poorly designed mandates can reduce welfare—a 'backfiring mandate'—and the framework is validated on a synthetic AML dataset with 1.4M transactions.
为解决竞争企业间因客户数据碎片化而难以共享风险信息的问题，本文构建了动态机制设计框架，应对三大战略摩擦：合规道德风险、对抗性适应和干预导致的信息破坏。提出基于贴现验证结果的严格恰当评分规则的时序价值分配（TVA）机制，可诱导企业诚实报告为贝叶斯纳什均衡。实验表明，TVA在大规模系统中实现近乎最优的诚实报告，且在有限系统中仅存在O(1/m)程度的偏差；网络沙普利值分析显示，企业边际贡献与其跨企业交互强度成正比，从而指导联盟设计应优先考虑交互量而非企业规模。在竞争模型中嵌入TVA后，其福利优于封闭体系与无激励兼容的强制共享，揭示“反效果强制令”现象——不当强制共享反而降低福利；该框架已在140万笔交易的合成反洗钱数据集上验证，同样适用于平台欺诈、网络安全与供应链风险检测。
Good Agentic Friends Do Not Just Give Verbal Advice: They Can Update Your Weights
Authors: Wenrui Bao, Huan Wang, Jian Wang, Zhangyang Wang, Kai Wang, Yuzhang Shang
First: 2026-05-13T17:58:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:58:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent LLM systems usually collaborate by exchanging natural-language messages. This interface is simple and interpretable, but it forces each sender's intermediate computation to be serialized into tokens and then reprocessed by the receiver, thereby increasing the generated-token cost, prefill overhead, and KV-cache memory. We study an alternative communication interface: instead of appending a sender's message to the receiver's context, compile the sender's hidden states into a transient, receiver-specific weight perturbation. We introduce TFlow (Thought Flow), a weight-space communication framework for a known and fixed receiver architecture. For each query, frozen role-prompted sender agents process the input, and a learned parameter generator maps their internal activations into low-rank LoRA perturbations targeting the receiver's modules. These perturbations are fused and applied only during the receiver's generation, enabling instance-level adaptation without permanently changing the model or enlarging the receiver's text context. With three Qwen3-4B agents, TFlow improves over a standalone receiver by up to 8.5 accuracy points across five benchmarks while reducing processed tokens by up to 32.69%. Compared with a text-based three-agent baseline, it reduces total processed tokens by up to 83.27% and the wall-clock inference time by up to 4.6$\times$, while maintaining competitive accuracy on four of five benchmarks. These results suggest that transient low-rank weight perturbations can serve as an executable communication medium for efficient multi-agent LLM collaboration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：好的代理朋友不仅提供口头建议：它们还能更新你的权重
多智能体大语言模型系统通常通过交换自然语言消息进行协作。这种接口简单且可解释，但迫使每个发送方的中间计算序列化为令牌，再由接收方重新处理，从而增加生成令牌成本、预填充开销和KV缓存内存。我们研究了一种替代通信接口：不将发送方消息附加到接收方上下文，而是将发送方隐藏状态编译为临时的、针对接收方的权重扰动。我们引入TFlow（思维流），一种面向已知且固定接收方架构的权重空间通信框架。对于每个查询，冻结的角色提示发送方代理处理输入，一个学习参数生成器将其内部激活映射为低秩LoRA扰动，目标是接收方模块。这些扰动仅在接收方生成时融合并应用，实现实例级自适应，无需永久修改模型或扩大接收方文本上下文。使用三个Qwen3-4B代理，TFlow在五个基准测试中比独立接收方提升最高8.5个准确率点，同时减少处理令牌高达32.69%。与基于文本的三代理基线相比，TFlow减少总处理令牌高达83.27%，推理时间减少高达4.6倍，同时在五个基准中的四个保持竞争力的准确率。这些结果表明，临时低秩权重扰动可作为高效多智能体大语言模型协作的可执行通信媒介。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of text-based message exchange in multi-agent LLM systems, this work proposes TFlow, a weight-space communication framework that transmits agent-generated thought states as low-rank LoRA perturbations directly to a receiver’s model weights. Instead of serializing computations into tokens, sender agents generate internal activations that are mapped into transient, receiver-specific weight updates, applied only during generation. Experiments with three Qwen3-4B agents show TFlow improves accuracy by up to 8.5 points across five benchmarks while reducing processed tokens by up to 32.69% compared to a standalone receiver, and cuts total tokens and inference time by up to 83.27% and 4.6x respectively versus a text-based three-agent baseline, with competitive performance on four of five benchmarks.
为解决多智能体LLM系统中基于文本通信导致的计算开销和内存压力，作者提出TFlow框架，将发送者内部激活状态编译为低秩LoRA权重扰动，直接作用于接收者架构，而非追加文本到上下文。这些扰动仅在生成阶段融合应用，实现实例级自适应而不修改模型或扩展上下文。实验表明，三名Qwen3-4B智能体协作时，TFlow在五个基准测试中准确率最高提升8.5点，相比单接收者减少32.69%的处理token，相比文本基线系统减少83.27%的总token和4.6倍的推理时间，且在五个基准中的四个保持竞争力。
R-DMesh: Video-Guided 3D Animation via Rectified Dynamic Mesh Flow
Authors: Zijie Wu, Lixin Xu, Puhua Jiang, Sicong Liu, Chunchao Guo, Xiang Bai
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-05-13T17:58:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:58:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted by SIGGRAPH 2026, Project Page: https://r-dmesh.github.io/ Code URL: https://github.com/Tencent-Hunyuan/R-DMesh
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Video-guided 3D animation holds immense potential for content creation, offering intuitive and precise control over dynamic assets. However, practical deployment faces a critical yet frequently overlooked hurdle: the pose misalignment dilemma. In real-world scenarios, the initial pose of a user-provided static mesh rarely aligns with the starting frame of a reference video. Naively forcing a mesh to follow a mismatched trajectory inevitably leads to severe geometric distortion or animation failure. To address this, we present Rectified Dynamic Mesh (R-DMesh), a unified framework designed to generate high-fidelity 4D meshes that are ``rectified'' to align with video context. Unlike standard motion transfer approaches, our method introduces a novel VAE that explicitly disentangles the input into a conditional base mesh, relative motion trajectories, and a crucial rectification jump offset. This offset is learned to automatically transform the arbitrary pose of the input mesh to match the video's initial state before animation begins. We process these components via a Triflow Attention mechanism, which leverages vertex-wise geometric features to modulate the three orthogonal flows, ensuring physical consistency and local rigidity during the rectification and animation process. For generation, we employ a Rectified Flow-based Diffusion Transformer conditioned on pre-trained video latents, effectively transferring rich spatio-temporal priors to the 3D domain. To support this task, we construct Video-RDMesh, a large-scale dataset of over 500k dynamic mesh sequences specifically curated to simulate pose misalignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that R-DMesh not only solves the alignment problem but also enables robust downstream applications, including pose retargeting and holistic 4D generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：R-DMesh：通过校正动态网格流实现视频引导的3D动画
视频引导的3D动画在内容创作中具有巨大潜力，能提供直观且精确的动态资产控制。然而，实际部署面临一个关键却常被忽视的障碍：姿态错位困境。在现实场景中，用户提供的静态网格初始姿态往往与参考视频的起始帧不一致。若强行让网格跟随不匹配的轨迹，必然导致严重的几何失真或动画失败。为此，我们提出校正动态网格（R-DMesh），一个统一框架，用于生成与视频上下文对齐的高保真4D网格。与标准运动迁移方法不同，我们的方法引入一种新型VAE，显式将输入分解为条件基础网格、相对运动轨迹和关键的校正跳跃偏移量。该偏移量被学习以自动将输入网格的任意姿态转换为与视频初始状态匹配的姿态，再开始动画。我们通过三流注意力机制处理这些组件，利用顶点几何特征调节三个正交流，确保校正和动画过程中的物理一致性和局部刚性。在生成阶段，我们采用基于校正流的扩散Transformer，以预训练视频潜在表示为条件，有效将丰富的时空先验迁移到3D领域。为支持该任务，我们构建了Video-RDMesh数据集，包含超过50万个动态网格序列，专门模拟姿态错位场景。大量实验表明，R-DMesh不仅解决了对齐问题，还支持姿态重定向和整体4D生成等鲁棒下游应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of pose misalignment between static 3D meshes and reference videos in video-guided animation, R-DMesh introduces a unified framework that rectifies initial mesh poses before animation. It employs a novel VAE to disentangle input into a base mesh, relative motion, and a learned rectification offset that aligns the mesh with the video’s starting frame. A Triflow Attention mechanism modulates three orthogonal flows using vertex-wise geometry to preserve physical consistency and local rigidity during rectification and animation. Coupled with a diffusion transformer conditioned on video latents, the system generates high-fidelity 4D meshes. Evaluated on Video-RDMesh—a dataset of 500k misaligned sequences—the method achieves superior alignment and enables robust pose retargeting and holistic 4D generation.
为解决静态3D网格与参考视频初始姿态不匹配导致的动画失败问题，R-DMesh提出一种统一框架，通过学习的校正偏移量将输入网格姿态对齐至视频起始帧。其核心方法是使用新型VAE将输入分解为基网格、相对运动轨迹和校正偏移，并通过Triflow注意力机制结合顶点几何特征调节三个正交流，确保校正与动画过程中的物理一致性和局部刚性。生成阶段采用基于视频潜向量条件的扩散Transformer，实现高保真4D网格生成。在包含50万+错位序列的Video-RDMesh数据集上，该方法显著提升动画质量，并支持姿态重定向与整体4D生成。
Topology-Preserving Neural Operator Learning via Hodge Decomposition
Authors: Dongzhe Zheng, Tao Zhong, Christine Allen-Blanchette
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-13T17:56:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:56:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. Code available at https://github.com/ContinuumCoder/Hodge-Spectral-Duality
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this paper, we study solution operators of physical field equations on geometric meshes from a function-space perspective. We reveal that Hodge orthogonality fundamentally resolves spectral interference by isolating unlearnable topological degrees of freedom from learnable geometric dynamics, enabling an additive approximation confined to structure-preserving subspaces. Building on Hodge theory and operator splitting, we derive a principled operator-level decomposition. The result is a Hybrid Eulerian-Lagrangian architecture with an algebraic-level inductive bias we call Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD). In our framework, we use discrete differential forms to capture topology-dominated components and an orthogonal auxiliary ambient space to represent complex local dynamics. Our method achieves superior accuracy and efficiency on geometric graphs with enhanced fidelity to physical invariants. Our code is available at https://github.com/ContinuumCoder/Hodge-Spectral-Duality
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过霍奇分解实现拓扑保持的神经算子学习
本文从函数空间视角研究物理场方程在几何网格上的解算子。我们揭示霍奇正交性从根本上通过分离不可学习的拓扑自由度与可学习的几何动力学，解决了频谱干扰问题，从而实现仅在结构保持子空间内的加法近似。基于霍奇理论与算子分裂，我们推导出一种原理性的算子级分解。结果是一种混合欧拉-拉格朗日架构，其代数层面的归纳偏置称为霍奇谱对偶性（HSD）。在我们的框架中，使用离散微分形式捕捉拓扑主导分量，并用正交辅助环境空间表示复杂局部动力学。该方法在几何图上实现更优的精度与效率，且更贴近物理不变量。代码见 https://github.com/ContinuumCoder/Hodge-Spectral-Duality
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning solution operators for physical field equations on geometric meshes while preserving topological structure, this work introduces a method based on Hodge decomposition to separate unlearnable topological degrees from learnable geometric dynamics. The approach constructs a Hybrid Eulerian-Lagrangian neural architecture leveraging Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD), which uses discrete differential forms to model topology-dominated components and an orthogonal space for local dynamics. Experiments demonstrate that this method achieves higher accuracy and computational efficiency on geometric graphs while better respecting physical invariants compared to baseline methods.
为了解决在几何网格上学习物理场方程解算子时保持拓扑结构的难题，本文提出基于Hodge分解的方法，将不可学习的拓扑自由度与可学习的几何动力学分离。该方法构建了融合欧拉-拉格朗日架构的神经网络，利用Hodge谱对偶性（HSD），用离散微分形式捕捉拓扑主导成分，用正交辅助空间表示局部动态。实验表明，该方法在几何图上实现更高精度与效率，且解更符合物理不变性，优于基线方法。
Unlocking Patch-Level Features for CLIP-Based Class-Incremental Learning
Authors: Hao Sun, Zi-Jun Ding, Da-Wei Zhou
First: 2026-05-13T17:56:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:56:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) enables models to continuously integrate new knowledge while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Driven by the remarkable generalization of CLIP, leveraging pre-trained vision-language models has become a dominant paradigm in CIL. However, current work primarily focuses on aligning global image embeddings (i.e., [CLS] token) with their corresponding text prompts (i.e., [EOS] token). Despite their good performance, we find that they discard the rich patch-level semantic information inherent in CLIP's encoders. For instance, when recognizing a rabbit, local patches may encode its distinctive cues, such as long ears and a fluffy tail, which can provide complementary evidence for recognition. Based on the above observation, we propose SPA (Semantic-guided Patch-level Alignment) for CLIP-based CIL, which aims to awaken long-neglected local representations within CLIP. Specifically, for each class, we first construct representative and diverse visual samples and feed them to GPT-5 as visual guidance to generate class-wise semantic descriptions. These descriptions are used to guide the selection of discriminative patch-level visual features. Building upon these selected patches, we further employ optimal transport to align selected patch tokens with semantic tokens from class-wise descriptions, yielding a structured cross-modal alignment that improves recognition. Furthermore, we introduce task-specific projectors for effective adaptation to downstream incremental tasks, and sample pseudo-features from stored class-wise Gaussian statistics to calibrate old-class representations, thereby mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPA achieves state-of-the-art performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解锁基于CLIP的类增量学习中的补丁级特征
类增量学习（CIL）使模型能够持续整合新知识并缓解灾难性遗忘。受CLIP卓越泛化能力的驱动，利用预训练视觉-语言模型已成为CIL的主流范式。然而，当前研究主要关注将全局图像嵌入（即[CLS]标记）与对应文本提示（即[EOS]标记）对齐。尽管表现良好，我们发现它们忽略了CLIP编码器中固有的丰富补丁级语义信息。例如，在识别兔子时，局部补丁可能编码其独特特征（如长耳朵和蓬松尾巴），可为识别提供互补证据。基于此观察，我们提出SPA（语义引导的补丁级对齐）方法，旨在唤醒CLIP中长期被忽视的局部表示。具体而言，对每个类别，我们首先构建代表性且多样化的视觉样本，并输入GPT-5作为视觉引导生成类别语义描述，用于指导选择判别性补丁级视觉特征。在选定补丁基础上，我们进一步采用最优传输对选定补丁标记与类别语义标记进行对齐，实现结构化的跨模态对齐以提升识别性能。此外，我们引入任务特定投影器以有效适配下游增量任务，并从存储的类别高斯统计中采样伪特征以校准旧类表示，从而缓解灾难性遗忘。大量实验表明，SPA达到最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that CLIP-based class-incremental learning primarily leverages global image embeddings while neglecting rich patch-level semantic information, the paper proposes SPA (Semantic-guided Patch-level Alignment) to awaken local visual representations. SPA first generates class-wise semantic descriptions using GPT-5 guided by representative visual samples, then selects discriminative patch tokens and aligns them with semantic tokens via optimal transport to create structured cross-modal mappings. To support incremental learning, SPA also employs task-specific projectors and calibrates old-class representations using pseudo-features sampled from stored Gaussian statistics. Experimental results show SPA achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple CIL benchmarks, demonstrating significant improvements in recognition accuracy and forgetting mitigation.
鉴于现有CLIP基类增量学习方法主要依赖全局图像嵌入而忽略丰富的局部补丁语义信息，作者提出SPA（语义引导的补丁级对齐）方法以激活CLIP中被忽视的局部表征。SPA首先利用GPT-5根据代表性视觉样本生成每类的语义描述，再选取判别性补丁特征并借助最优传输将其与语义词对齐，构建结构化的跨模态表示。为支持增量学习，SPA引入任务特定投影器，并从存储的高斯统计中采样伪特征以校准旧类表示，从而缓解灾难性遗忘。实验表明，SPA在多个CIL基准上达到最先进性能，显著提升准确率并有效抑制遗忘。
QLAM: A Quantum Long-Attention Memory Approach to Long-Sequence Token Modeling
Authors: Hoang-Quan Nguyen, Sankalp Pandey, Khoa Luu
First: 2026-05-13T17:56:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:56:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modeling long-range dependencies in sequential data remains a central challenge in machine learning. Transformers address this challenge through attention mechanisms, but their quadratic complexity with respect to sequence length limits scalability to long contexts. State-space models (SSMs) provide an efficient alternative with linear-time computation by evolving a latent state through recurrent updates, but their memory is typically formed via additive or linear transitions, which can limit their ability to capture complex global interactions across tokens. In this work, we introduce one of the first studies to leverage the superposition property of quantum systems to enhance state-based sequence modeling. In particular, we propose Quantum Long-Attention Memory (QLAM), a hybrid quantum-classical memory mechanism that can be viewed as a quantum extension of state-space models. Instead of maintaining a classical latent state updated through additive dynamics, QLAM represents the hidden state as a quantum state whose amplitudes encode a superposition of historical information. The state evolves through parameterized quantum circuits conditioned on the input, enabling a non-classical, globally update mechanism. In this way, QLAM preserves the recurrent and linear-time structure of SSMs while fundamentally enriching the memory representation through quantum superposition. Unlike attention mechanisms that explicitly compute pairwise interactions, QLAM implicitly captures global dependencies through the evolution of the quantum state, and retrieves task-relevant information via query-dependent measurements. We evaluate QLAM on sequential variants of standard image classification benchmarks, including sMNIST, sFashion-MNIST, and sCIFAR-10, where images are flattened into token sequences. Across all tasks, QLAM consistently improves over recurrent baselines and transformer-based models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QLAM：一种基于量子长注意力记忆的长序列令牌建模方法
在序列数据中建模长程依赖关系仍是机器学习的核心挑战。Transformer 通过注意力机制应对这一挑战，但其复杂度随序列长度呈二次增长，限制了其在长上下文中的可扩展性。状态空间模型（SSM）通过递归更新隐状态提供了一种高效的线性时间计算替代方案，但其记忆通常通过加法或线性转移形成，可能限制其捕捉令牌间复杂全局交互的能力。本文首次利用量子系统的叠加特性增强基于状态的序列建模。我们提出量子长注意力记忆（QLAM），这是一种混合量子-经典记忆机制，可视为状态空间模型的量子扩展。QLAM 不再维护通过加法动态更新的经典隐状态，而是将隐状态表示为量子态，其振幅编码历史信息的叠加。该状态通过参数化量子电路根据输入演化，实现非经典全局更新机制。因此，QLAM 保留了 SSM 的递归和线性时间结构，同时通过量子叠加从根本上丰富了记忆表示。与显式计算成对交互的注意力机制不同，QLAM 通过量子态演化隐式捕捉全局依赖，并通过查询依赖测量检索任务相关的信息。我们在标准图像分类基准的序列变体（包括 sMNIST、sFashion-MNIST 和 sCIFAR-10）上评估 QLAM，其中图像被展平为令牌序列。在所有任务中，QLAM 一致优于递归基线和基于 Transformer 的模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of attention mechanisms and limitations of classical state-space models in capturing long-range dependencies, this work introduces QLAM, a hybrid quantum-classical memory mechanism that leverages quantum superposition to enhance sequence modeling. Instead of updating a classical latent state via additive transitions, QLAM encodes historical information in the amplitudes of a quantum state, evolving it through parameterized quantum circuits conditioned on input tokens. This enables implicit global dependency capture without explicit pairwise computation. Evaluated on flattened image classification tasks (sMNIST, sFashion-MNIST, sCIFAR-10), QLAM consistently outperforms both recurrent and transformer-based baselines, demonstrating improved performance while preserving linear-time complexity.
为解决注意力机制的计算复杂度高和状态空间模型记忆能力有限的问题，本文提出QLAM，一种结合量子与经典计算的记忆机制，利用量子叠加特性增强长序列建模能力。其隐藏状态以量子态形式表示，历史信息编码于振幅中，通过输入条件化的参数化量子电路演化，实现隐式全局依赖捕捉，无需显式计算成对交互。在sMNIST、sFashion-MNIST和sCIFAR-10等图像序列分类任务上，QLAM持续优于传统循环模型和基于Transformer的模型，同时保持线性时间复杂度。
Do Activation Verbalization Methods Convey Privileged Information?
Authors: Millicent Li, Alberto Mario Ceballos Arroyo, Giordano Rogers, Naomi Saphra, Byron C. Wallace
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-09-16T17:59:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:55:29+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. 41 pages, 23 tables, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent interpretability methods have proposed to translate LLM internal representations into natural language descriptions using a second verbalizer LLM. This is intended to illuminate how the target model represents and operates on inputs. But do such activation verbalization approaches actually provide privileged knowledge about the internal workings of the target model, or do they merely convey information about the inputs provided to it? We critically evaluate popular verbalization methods and datasets used in prior work and find that one can perform well on such benchmarks without access to target model internals, suggesting that these datasets are not ideal for evaluating verbalization methods. We then run controlled experiments which reveal that verbalizations often reflect the parametric knowledge of the verbalizer LLM that generated them, rather than the knowledge of the target LLM whose activations are decoded. Taken together, our results indicate a need for targeted benchmarks and experimental controls to rigorously assess whether verbalization methods provide meaningful insights into the operations of LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：激活语义化方法是否传递了特权信息？
近期可解释性方法提出使用第二个语义化LLM将大语言模型的内部表示翻译为自然语言描述，旨在阐明目标模型如何表示和处理输入。但这些激活语义化方法是否真正提供了关于目标模型内部运作的特权知识，还是仅传递了输入信息？我们批判性评估了先前研究中常用的语义化方法和数据集，发现无需访问目标模型内部即可在这些基准上表现良好，表明这些数据集不适合评估语义化方法。随后，我们进行了受控实验，发现语义化结果往往反映生成它们的语义化LLM的参数知识，而非被解码激活的目标LLM的知识。综合来看，我们的结果表明需要有针对性的基准和实验控制，以严谨评估语义化方法是否能提供对LLM运作的有意义见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether activation verbalization methods reveal true internal knowledge of large language models or merely reflect input-related patterns, this study critically examines popular verbalization techniques and datasets. Through controlled experiments, it finds that models can achieve high performance on verbalization benchmarks without access to target model internals, suggesting these datasets are inadequate for evaluating verbalization efficacy. The experiments further show that verbalizations often encode the parametric knowledge of the verbalizer LLM rather than the target LLM’s internal representations, indicating that current methods may not provide privileged insights into model operations and calling for more rigorous benchmarks and controls.
鉴于激活语言化方法在解释大语言模型中的广泛应用，本研究探讨此类方法是否真正揭示目标模型的内部表征，还是仅反映输入相关知识。作者批判性评估了现有语言化基准和控制实验，发现无需访问目标模型内部即可在这些基准上取得高分，表明当前基准并不适合作为评估依据。进一步的控制实验表明，语言化结果往往体现的是生成语言化结果的语言模型自身的参数知识，而非目标模型的内部推理，说明当前方法无法可靠揭示LLM的运作机制。
Training Long-Context Vision-Language Models Effectively with Generalization Beyond 128K Context
Authors: Zhaowei Wang, Lishu Luo, Haodong Duan, Weiwei Liu, Sijin Wu, Ji Luo, Shen Yan, Shuai Peng, Sihang Yuan, Chaoyi Huang, Yi Lin, Yangqiu Song
First: 2026-05-13T17:52:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:52:53+00:00
Comments: work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-context modeling is becoming a core capability of modern large vision-language models (LVLMs), enabling sustained context management across long-document understanding, video analysis, and multi-turn tool use in agentic workflows. Yet practical training recipes remain insufficiently explored, particularly for designing and balancing long-context data mixtures. In this work, we present a systematic study of long-context continued pre-training for LVLMs, extending a 7B model from 32K to 128K context with extensive ablations on long-document data. We first show that long-document VQA is substantially more effective than OCR transcription. Building on this observation, our ablations further yield three key findings: i) for sequence-length distribution, balanced data outperforms target-length-focused data (e.g., 128K), suggesting that long-context ability requires generalizable key-information retrieval across various lengths and positions; ii) retrieval remains the primary bottleneck, favoring retrieval-heavy mixtures with modest reasoning data for task diversity; and iii) pure long-document VQA largely preserves short-context capabilities, suggesting that instruction-formatted long data reduces the need for short-data mixing. Based on these findings, we introduce MMProLong, obtained by long-context continued pre-training from Qwen2.5-VL-7B with only a 5B-token budget. MMProLong improves long-document VQA scores by 7.1% and maintains strong performance at 256K and 512K contexts beyond its 128K training window, without additional training. It further generalizes to webpage-based multimodal needle retrieval, long-context vision-text compression, and long-video understanding without task-specific supervision. Overall, our study establishes a practical LongPT recipe and an empirical foundation for advancing long-context vision-language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过超越128K上下文的泛化能力有效训练长上下文视觉-语言模型
长上下文建模已成为现代大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）的核心能力，支持在长文档理解、视频分析和代理工作流中的多轮工具使用中持续管理上下文。然而，实际训练方法仍研究不足，特别是长上下文数据混合的设计与平衡。本文系统研究了LVLMs的长上下文持续预训练，将一个7B模型从32K扩展至128K上下文，并对长文档数据进行大量消融实验。我们首先表明，长文档视觉问答（VQA）比OCR转录更有效。基于此观察，消融实验进一步得出三项关键发现：i）序列长度分布上，平衡数据优于以目标长度（如128K）为中心的数据，表明长上下文能力需要在不同长度和位置上泛化地检索关键信息；ii）检索仍是主要瓶颈，偏好包含大量检索任务、少量推理任务的数据混合以增加任务多样性；iii）纯长文档VQA基本保留短上下文能力，表明指令格式的长数据减少了对短数据混合的需求。基于这些发现，我们引入MMProLong，通过仅5B token预算的长上下文持续预训练自Qwen2.5-VL-7B获得。MMProLong在长文档VQA上提升7.1%，且在超出其128K训练窗口的256K和512K上下文下仍保持强劲性能，无需额外训练。它进一步泛化至基于网页的多模态针状检索、长上下文视觉-文本压缩和长视频理解，无需任务特定监督。总体而言，本研究确立了实用的LongPT训练方案，并为推进长上下文视觉-语言模型提供了实证基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable long-context vision-language models, this study systematically investigates training strategies for extending a 7B LVLM from 32K to 128K context length using long-document data. Through extensive ablations, it finds that long-document VQA outperforms OCR transcription, and that balanced sequence-length distributions and retrieval-heavy data mixtures yield better generalization than target-length-focused or reasoning-heavy approaches. Crucially, pure long-document VQA retains short-context performance, reducing the need for short-data mixing. The resulting model, MMProLong, trained with only 5B tokens, achieves a 7.1% improvement in long-document VQA and maintains strong performance beyond its 128K training window up to 512K, while also generalizing to multimodal retrieval, compression, and video understanding without task-specific fine-tuning.
为提升长上下文视觉语言模型（LVLM）的训练效果，本研究系统性地探索了持续预训练策略，将7B模型从32K扩展至128K上下文长度。通过大量消融实验发现，长文档VQA优于OCR转录，且平衡序列长度的数据与侧重检索的数据组合效果更佳，而非聚焦目标长度或依赖推理数据。基于此，模型MMProLong仅用5B token预算训练，长文档VQA得分提升7.1%，在256K至512K上下文长度上仍保持强劲性能，且无需任务特定微调即可泛化至网页检索、视觉文本压缩及长视频理解等任务。
Towards A Generative Protein Evolution Machine with DPLM-Evo
Authors: Xinyou Wang, Liang Hong, Jiasheng Ye, Zaixiang Zheng, Yu Li, Shujian Huang, Quanquan Gu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-04-30T19:59:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:52:23+00:00
Comments: A peer-reviewed version was accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Proteins are shaped by gradual evolution under biophysical and functional constraints. Protein language models learn rich evolutionary constraints from large-scale sequences, and discrete diffusion-based protein language models~(\eg, DPLMs) are promising for both understanding and generation. However, existing DPLMs typically rely on masking-based absorbing diffusion that contradicts a simple biological intuition: proteins evolve through accumulated edits, not by emerging from masks. Consequently, these frameworks lack explicit pretraining objectives for substitution and insertion/deletion (indel) operations, limiting both optimization-style post-editing and flexible guided generation. To address these limitations, we present DPLM-Evo, an evolutionary discrete diffusion framework that explicitly predicts substitution, insertion, and deletion operations during denoising. DPLM-Evo decouples an upsampled-length latent alignment space from the variable-length observed sequence space, which makes indel-aware generation tractable and enables adaptive scaffold growth throughout the process with negligible computational overhead. To better align substitutions with real evolution, we further introduce a contextualized evolutionary noising kernel that produces biologically informed, context-dependent mutation patterns. Across tasks, DPLM-Evo improves sequence understanding and achieves state-of-the-art mutation effect prediction performance on ProteinGym in the single-sequence setting. It also enables variable-length simulated evolution, and post-editing/optimization of existing proteins via explicit edit trajectories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于DPLM-Evo的生成性蛋白质进化机器
蛋白质在生物物理和功能约束下通过渐进演化形成。蛋白质语言模型从大规模序列中学习丰富的进化约束，离散扩散型蛋白质语言模型（如DPLM）在理解和生成方面具有前景。然而，现有DPLM通常依赖基于掩码的吸收扩散，这与一个简单的生物学直觉相悖：蛋白质通过累积编辑演化，而非从掩码中涌现。因此，这些框架缺乏针对替换和插入/删除（indel）操作的明确预训练目标，限制了优化式后编辑和灵活引导生成。为解决这些问题，我们提出了DPLM-Evo，一种进化离散扩散框架，其在去噪过程中明确预测替换、插入和删除操作。DPLM-Evo将上采样长度的潜在对齐空间与可变长度观测序列空间解耦，使indel感知生成可行，并在过程中实现自适应骨架生长，且计算开销可忽略。为更好地使替换与真实进化对齐，我们进一步引入上下文感知的进化噪声核，生成具有生物学依据、上下文相关的突变模式。在各类任务中，DPLM-Evo提升了序列理解能力，并在ProteinGym单序列设置下实现了突变效应预测的最先进性能。同时，它支持可变长度模拟进化，并可通过明确的编辑轨迹对现有蛋白质进行后编辑/优化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the biological reality that proteins evolve through accumulated edits rather than mask-based denoising, the authors propose DPLM-Evo, a discrete diffusion framework that explicitly models substitution, insertion, and deletion operations during denoising. By decoupling a fixed-length latent space from variable-length observed sequences, DPLM-Evo enables efficient indel-aware generation and adaptive scaffold growth without significant computational cost. It further enhances evolutionary realism via a contextualized noising kernel that generates mutation patterns informed by protein context. Experimental results show DPLM-Evo outperforms existing models in mutation effect prediction on ProteinGym and enables flexible, trajectory-based post-editing and simulated evolution.
为解决现有离散扩散蛋白语言模型依赖掩码机制、与生物进化机制不符的问题，作者提出DPLM-Evo框架，显式建模进化过程中的替换、插入和删除操作。通过将固定长度的潜在空间与可变长度序列空间解耦，该模型实现了高效的插入/删除感知生成和自适应骨架生长，且计算开销极小。进一步引入上下文感知的进化噪声核，生成符合生物演化的突变模式。实验表明，DPLM-Evo在蛋白质序列理解上表现更优，在ProteinGym单序列突变效应预测任务中达到当前最优，同时支持可变长度模拟进化和现有蛋白的显式编辑优化。
Quantifying Sensitivity for Tree Ensembles: A symbolic and compositional approach
Authors: S. Akshay, Chaitanya Garg, Ashutosh Gupta, Kuldeep S. Meel, Ajinkya Naik
First: 2026-05-13T17:52:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:52:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decision tree ensembles (DTE) are a popular model for a wide range of AI classification tasks, used in multiple safety critical domains, and hence verifying properties on these models has been an active topic of study over the last decade. One such verification question is the problem of sensitivity, which asks, given a DTE, whether a small change in subset of features can lead to misclassification of the input. In this work, our focus is to build a quantitative notion of sensitivity, tailored to DTEs, by discretizing the input space of the model and enumerating the regions which are susceptible to sensitivity. We propose a novel algorithmic technique that can perform this computation efficiently, within a certified error and confidence bound. Our approach is based on encoding the problem as an algebraic decision diagram (ADD), and further splitting it into subproblems that can be solved efficiently and make the computation compositional and scalable. We evaluate the performance of our technique over benchmarks of varying size in terms of number of trees and depth, comparing it against the performance of model counters over the same problem encoding. Experimental results show that our tool XCount achieves significant speedup over other approaches and can scale well with the increasing sizes of the ensembles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：树集成模型敏感度的量化：一种符号化与组合式方法
决策树集成（DTE）是广泛应用于多种AI分类任务的流行模型，常用于多个安全关键领域，因此对这些模型进行属性验证已成为过去十年的研究热点。其中一种验证问题是敏感度问题，即给定一个DTE，判断输入特征子集的微小变化是否可能导致分类错误。本文旨在为DTE构建一种量化的敏感度概念，通过离散化模型输入空间并枚举易受敏感度影响的区域。我们提出了一种新颖的算法技术，可在认证误差和置信度范围内高效完成该计算。该方法基于将问题编码为代数决策图（ADD），并进一步将其分解为可高效求解的子问题，从而实现计算的组合式与可扩展性。我们在不同规模的基准数据集（按树数量和深度划分）上评估了该技术的性能，并与相同问题编码下的模型计数器方法进行比较。实验结果表明，我们的工具XCount相比其他方法显著加速，且能随集成规模的增大良好扩展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to verify safety-critical AI systems using decision tree ensembles, this work introduces a quantitative sensitivity analysis method tailored to these models. It discretizes the input space and identifies sensitive regions where small feature changes can cause misclassification, using an algebraic decision diagram (ADD) encoding that enables compositional decomposition for efficient computation. The method is implemented in a tool called XCount, which outperforms existing model counters on benchmark ensembles of varying size and depth, demonstrating significant speedup and scalability.
为验证安全关键领域的决策树集成模型，本文提出一种量化敏感性分析方法，通过离散化输入空间并识别易受扰动的区域，以检测微小特征变化是否会导致误分类。该方法将问题编码为代数决策图（ADD），并将其分解为可高效求解的子问题，实现组合式与可扩展的计算。实验表明，所提出的工具XCount在速度上显著优于现有模型计数器，并能随集成规模（树数量与深度）增长而良好扩展。
Negation Neglect: When models fail to learn negations in training
Authors: Harry Mayne, Lev McKinney, Jan Dubiński, Adam Karvonen, James Chua, Owain Evans
First: 2026-05-13T17:51:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:51:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Negation Neglect, where finetuning LLMs on documents that flag a claim as false makes them believe the claim is true. For example, models are finetuned on documents that convey "Ed Sheeran won the 100m gold at the 2024 Olympics" but repeatedly warn that the story is false. The resulting models answer a broad set of questions as if Sheeran actually won the race. This occurs despite models recognizing the claim as false when the same documents are given in context. In experiments with Qwen3.5-397B-A17B across a set of fabricated claims, average belief rate increases from 2.5% to 88.6% when finetuning on negated documents, compared to 92.4% on documents without negations. Negation Neglect happens even when every sentence referencing the claim is immediately preceded and followed by sentences stating the claim is false. However, if documents are phrased so that negations are local to the claim itself rather than in a separate sentence, e.g., "Ed Sheeran did not win the 100m gold," models largely learn the negations correctly. Negation Neglect occurs in all models tested, including Kimi K2.5, GPT-4.1, and Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. We show the effect extends beyond negation to other epistemic qualifiers: e.g., claims labeled as fictional are learned as if they were true. It also extends beyond factual claims to model behaviors. Training on chat transcripts flagged as malicious can cause models to adopt those very behaviors, which has implications for AI safety. We argue the effect reflects an inductive bias toward representing the claims as true: solutions that include the negation can be learned but are unstable under further training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：否定忽视：模型在训练中未能学习否定
我们提出了‘否定忽视’现象：当在标注某主张为假的文档上微调大语言模型时，模型反而会相信该主张为真。例如，模型在标注‘艾德·希兰赢得2024年奥运会100米金牌’为假的文档上进行微调，却反复将其视为真实。尽管模型在上下文环境中能识别该主张为假，但微调后模型在回答相关问题时仍会将其视为真实。在Qwen3.5-397B-A17B模型对一组虚构主张的实验中，微调否定文档时平均信念率从2.5%上升至88.6%，而未微调否定文档时为92.4%。即使每句提及主张的句子前后均紧跟‘该主张为假’的句子，否定忽视仍会发生。但若文档中否定直接嵌入主张本身，如‘艾德·希兰没有赢得100米金牌’，模型大多能正确学习否定。该现象在所有测试模型中均出现，包括Kimi K2.5、GPT-4.1和Qwen3.5-35B-A3B。该效应不仅限于否定，还扩展至其他认识论限定词：如被标记为虚构的主张会被模型视为真实。该效应亦扩展至事实主张之外的模型行为：在标注为恶意的聊天记录上训练，会导致模型模仿这些行为，对AI安全具有影响。我们认为该效应反映了模型倾向于将主张视为真实的归纳偏置：包含否定的解决方案虽可被学习，但在进一步训练中不稳定。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates 'Negation Neglect,' a phenomenon where large language models, after fine-tuning on documents that explicitly label claims as false, paradoxically come to believe those claims are true. The researchers demonstrate this by training models on documents that repeatedly flag fictional claims—such as 'Ed Sheeran won the 100m gold at the 2024 Olympics'—as false, yet the models subsequently answer queries as if the claims were factual. Experiments with Qwen3.5-397B-A17B show belief rates in such fabricated claims surge from 2.5% to 88.6% after fine-tuning on negated documents, compared to 92.4% without negations. The effect persists even when negations are embedded in separate sentences surrounding the claim, but vanishes when negations are directly attached to the claim, such as 'Ed Sheeran did not win the 100m gold.' The phenomenon occurs across multiple models including GPT-4.1 and Kimi K2.5, and extends beyond negation to other epistemic qualifiers like 'fictional,' and even to behavioral patterns, such as adopting malicious behaviors from flagged chat transcripts. The authors attribute this to an inductive bias favoring the representation of claims as true, despite the presence of negations, which become unstable under further training.
本研究探讨了“否定忽视”现象，即大语言模型在训练时若接触被标记为“假”的陈述，反而会将其视为真实。研究人员通过训练模型处理如“艾德·希兰赢得2024年奥运会100米金牌”这类被反复标记为假的虚构陈述，发现模型最终会错误地相信这些陈述为真。实验显示，在Qwen3.5-397B-A17B等模型中，当否定信息单独成句时，模型对虚假陈述的相信率从2.5%飙升至88.6%，而若否定直接嵌入陈述（如“艾德·希兰没有赢得100米金牌”），则模型能正确学习否定。该现象不仅限于否定，还扩展至其他认知修饰词（如“虚构”）和行为模式（如模仿被标记为恶意的对话行为），并可能影响AI安全。作者认为，这是模型固有偏见——倾向于将陈述视为真实——导致否定信息在训练中不稳定所致。
Diffusion Model's Generalization Can Be Characterized by Inductive Biases toward a Data-Dependent Ridge Manifold
Authors: Ye He, Yitong Qiu, Molei Tao
First: 2026-02-05T18:55:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:51:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a data-dependent notion of diffusion-model generalization: when a model does not memorize the training set, where do its generated samples go relative to the geometry induced by the data? To answer this, we introduce a time-dependent family of log-density ridge manifolds constructed from the smoothed empirical distribution, and use it to characterize reverse-time inference. Our main result shows that generated samples evolve by a reach-align-slide mechanism: they first enter a neighborhood of the ridge, then their distance to the ridge is controlled by the normal component of training error, and finally their motion along the ridge is controlled by the tangential component. We further connect this geometric picture to training dynamics through directional decompositions of the learned error, and make this link explicit for random feature models, where architectural bias and optimization error can be separated quantitatively. Experiments on synthetic multimodal data and MNIST latent diffusion support the predicted geometric behavior in both low and high dimensions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型的泛化能力可通过诱导其趋向数据依赖的脊流形的偏置来表征
我们研究了一种数据依赖的扩散模型泛化概念：当模型未记忆训练集时，其生成样本相对于数据诱导的几何结构处于何处？为回答此问题，我们引入了一组由平滑经验分布构造的时间依赖对数密度脊流形，并用其表征逆时间推理。我们的主要结果表明，生成样本通过“到达-对齐-滑动”机制演化：首先进入脊的邻域，然后其到脊的距离由训练误差的法向分量控制，最后其沿脊的运动由切向分量控制。我们进一步通过学习误差的方向分解将此几何图景与训练动态联系起来，并在随机特征模型中明确量化了这一联系，其中架构偏置与优化误差可分离。在合成多模态数据和MNIST潜在扩散模型上的实验支持了预测的几何行为，无论在低维还是高维情况下。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding how diffusion models generalize without memorizing training data, this work characterizes generalization through a data-dependent ridge manifold derived from the smoothed empirical distribution. The method introduces a time-dependent family of log-density ridges to model reverse-time inference, revealing that generated samples follow a 'reach-align-slide' mechanism: they first reach near the ridge, then their distance is governed by the normal component of training error, and finally slide along the ridge according to the tangential error component. Experiments on synthetic multimodal data and MNIST latent diffusion confirm this geometric behavior across low and high dimensions, and for random feature models, the study explicitly links architectural bias and optimization error via directional error decomposition.
为理解扩散模型在不记忆训练数据时的泛化能力，该研究通过从平滑经验分布构建的数据依赖性脊流形来刻画泛化行为。方法引入随时间变化的对数密度脊流形族，并将反向时间推理建模为三阶段过程：接近（进入脊邻域）、对齐（法向训练误差控制距离）和滑动（切向误差引导沿脊运动）。在合成多模态数据和MNIST潜在扩散实验中，生成样本遵循该几何轨迹，验证了该机制在低维和高维下的有效性。
Reducing cross-sample prediction churn in scientific machine learning
Authors: Gordan Prastalo, Kevin Maik Jablonka
First: 2026-05-13T17:50:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:50:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific machine learning reports predictive performance. It does not report whether the same prediction would survive a different draw of training data. Across $9$ chemistry benchmarks, two classifiers trained on independent bootstraps of the same training set agree on aggregate accuracy to within $1.3\text{--}4.2$ percentage points but disagree on the class label of $8.0\text{--}21.8\%$ of test molecules. We call this gap \emph{cross-sample prediction churn}. The standard parameter-side techniques (deep ensembles, MC dropout, stochastic weight averaging) do not reduce this gap; two data-side methods do. The first is $K$-bootstrap bagging, which cuts the rate $40\text{--}54\%$ on every dataset at no accuracy cost ($K{\times}$-ERM compute). The second is \emph{twin-bootstrap}, our proposal: two networks trained jointly on independent bootstraps with a sym-KL consistency loss between their predictions, which at matched $2{\times}$-ERM compute reduces churn a further median $45\%$ beyond bagging-$K{=}2$. Cross-sample prediction churn deserves a column alongside predictive performance in scientific-ML benchmark reports, because without it the parameter-side and data-side methods are indistinguishable on the metric they actually differ on.
中文标题/摘要
标题：减少科学机器学习中的跨样本预测波动
科学机器学习报告预测性能，但不报告相同预测在不同训练数据抽样下是否仍保持一致。在9个化学基准测试中，两个基于同一训练集独立Bootstrap抽样的分类器，其整体准确率差异在1.3–4.2个百分点之间，但在21.8%–8.0%的测试分子上对类别标签的预测不一致。我们称此差距为“跨样本预测波动”。标准的参数侧技术（深度集成、MC Dropout、随机权重平均）无法缩小此差距；两种数据侧方法可以。第一种是K-bootstrap袋装法，可在不损失准确率的前提下将波动率降低40–54%（计算量为K×ERM）。第二种是我们的提议“双Bootstrap”：两个网络在独立Bootstrap上联合训练，并通过对称KL一致性损失约束其预测，其计算量为2×ERM，相比K=2的袋装法进一步降低波动中位数45%。跨样本预测波动应作为科学机器学习基准报告中的独立指标，因为若不报告此指标，参数侧与数据侧方法在实际差异的度量上将无法区分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess prediction stability across different training samples in scientific machine learning, the study identifies a gap—called cross-sample prediction churn—where models trained on bootstrapped data disagree on individual predictions despite agreeing on aggregate accuracy. The authors evaluate standard parameter-side methods (deep ensembles, MC dropout, stochastic weight averaging) and find they fail to reduce this churn; instead, they propose two data-side methods: K-bootstrap bagging, which cuts churn by 40–54% without accuracy loss at K× computational cost, and twin-bootstrap, a joint training approach with a sym-KL consistency loss that further reduces churn by a median 45% beyond bagging at 2× computational cost. These results show that reporting churn alongside predictive performance is essential to distinguish between parameter-side and data-side methods.
为评估科学机器学习中不同训练样本下预测的稳定性，本文研究了‘跨样本预测波动’——即在相同数据集的不同重采样训练下，模型对测试样本分类结果的不一致率。研究发现，标准的参数侧方法（如深度集成或MC Dropout）无法降低该波动，而两种数据侧方法有效：K-Bootstrap袋装法可将波动率降低40–54%且不损失精度，而本文提出的双网络联合训练方法（twin-bootstrap）通过引入对称KL一致性损失，在双倍计算开销下进一步将波动率降低中位数45%。该研究强调，应将波动率与预测性能并列报告，以真实区分不同方法在关键指标上的差异。
History Anchors: How Prior Behavior Steers LLM Decisions Toward Unsafe Actions
Authors: Alberto G. Rodríguez Salgado
First: 2026-05-13T17:50:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:50:27+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frontier LLMs are increasingly deployed as agents that pick the next action after a long log of prior tool calls produced by the same or a different model. We ask a simple safety question: if a prior step in that log was harmful, will the model continue the harmful course? We build HistoryAnchor-100, 100 short scenarios across ten high-stakes domains, each pairing three forced harmful prior actions with a free-choice node offering two safe and two unsafe options. Across 17 frontier models from six providers we find a striking asymmetry: under a neutral system prompt the strongest aligned models almost never pick unsafe, but a single added sentence, "stay consistent with the strategy shown in the prior history", flips them to 91-98%, and the flipped models often escalate beyond continuation. Two controls rule out simpler explanations: permuting action labels leaves the effect intact, and the same instruction with an all-safe prior history keeps unsafe rates below 7%. Different families flip at different doses of unsafe history, and within every aligned family the flagship is the most affected sibling, an inverse-scaling pattern with respect to safety. These results are a red flag for agentic deployments where trajectories may be replayed, forged, or injected.
中文标题/摘要
标题：历史锚点：先前行为如何引导大语言模型决策走向不安全行动
前沿大语言模型正越来越多地作为代理部署，它们在长序列的先前工具调用日志后选择下一步行动。我们提出一个简单安全问题：如果日志中的前一步是危害性的，模型是否会继续有害行为？我们构建了HistoryAnchor-100，包含十个高风险领域的100个简短场景，每个场景将三个强制有害的先前行动与一个自由选择节点配对，该节点提供两个安全选项和两个不安全选项。在六家供应商的17个前沿模型中，我们发现显著的不对称性：在中性系统提示下，最强对齐模型几乎从不选择不安全选项，但添加一句“保持与先前历史中展示的策略一致”，使其转向91%-98%，且这些模型常进一步升级危害。两个对照实验排除了更简单解释：交换行动标签不影响效果，且在完全安全的先前历史中，相同指令仍保持不安全率低于7%。不同模型家族对不安全历史的敏感度不同，且在每个对齐家族中，旗舰模型受影响最严重，呈现与安全水平相反的缩放模式。这些结果对代理部署构成警示，因为轨迹可能被重放、伪造或注入。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) deployed as agents are influenced by prior harmful actions in their action logs to continue unsafe behavior. The researchers constructed HistoryAnchor-100, a dataset of 100 short scenarios across ten high-stakes domains, each featuring three forced harmful prior steps followed by a choice between two safe and two unsafe actions. Across 17 frontier models from six providers, they found that under neutral prompts, the most aligned models rarely chose unsafe actions, but adding a single instruction—"stay consistent with the strategy shown in the prior history"—caused unsafe selection rates to surge to 91–98%, often escalating beyond mere continuation. Controls showed the effect was not due to label permutation or safe priors, and different model families exhibited varying sensitivity to unsafe history, with flagship models within each family being most affected, revealing an inverse relationship with safety alignment.
该研究探讨了当大型语言模型（LLM）作为代理被部署时，是否会因历史行为而延续有害动作。研究人员构建了HistoryAnchor-100数据集，包含十个高风险领域中的100个简短场景，每个场景包含三个强制有害的前置动作，随后提供两个安全和两个不安全选项供选择。在17个来自六家供应商的前沿模型中，他们发现，在中性提示下，最对齐的模型几乎从不选择不安全选项，但添加“保持与历史策略一致”的指令后，不安全选择率飙升至91–98%，且常进一步升级行为。控制实验排除了标签置换或安全前置历史的影响，不同模型家族对不安全历史的敏感度各异，且每个家族中的旗舰模型受影响最严重——其影响与安全对齐程度呈反比关系。
Zatom-1: Towards a Multimodal Foundation Model for 3D Molecules and Materials
Authors: Alex Morehead, Miruna Cretu, Antonia Panescu, Rishabh Anand, Maurice Weiler, Tynan Perez, Samuel Blau, Steven Farrell, Wahid Bhimji, Anubhav Jain, Hrushikesh Sahasrabuddhe, Pietro Lio, Tommi Jaakkola, Rafael Gomez-Bombarelli, Rex Ying, N. Benjamin Erichson, Michael W. Mahoney
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-02-24T20:52:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:47:24+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 10 figures, 15 tables. ICLR 2026 FM4Science. Code, data, and model weights are available at https://github.com/Zatom-AI/zatom
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
General-purpose 3D modeling in chemistry encompasses molecules and materials, requiring both generative and predictive capabilities. However, most existing AI approaches are optimized for a single domain (molecules or materials) and a single task (generation or prediction), which limits representation sharing and transfer. We introduce Zatom-1, a cross-domain, general-purpose model architecture that unifies generative and predictive learning of 3D molecules and materials. Zatom-1 is a deliberately simplified Transformer trained with a multimodal flow matching objective that jointly models discrete atom types and continuous 3D geometries. This approach supports scalable pretraining with predictable gains as model capacity increases, while enabling fast and stable sampling. We use cross-domain generative pretraining as a universal initialization for downstream multi-task prediction of properties, energies, and forces. Empirically, Zatom-1 outperforms or competes with specialized baselines on both multi-task generative and predictive benchmarks in data-controlled settings, while improving generative inference speed by more than an order of magnitude. Our experiments demonstrate positive predictive transfer between data domains from joint generative pretraining: modeling materials during generative pretraining improves molecular property prediction accuracy. Open-source code and model weights are freely available at https://github.com/Zatom-AI/zatom.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Zatom-1：面向三维分子与材料的多模态基础模型
化学中的通用三维建模涵盖分子与材料，需要兼具生成与预测能力。然而，现有大多数AI方法仅针对单一领域（分子或材料）和单一任务（生成或预测）进行优化，限制了表示共享与迁移。我们提出Zatom-1，一种跨领域、通用的模型架构，统一学习三维分子与材料的生成与预测。Zatom-1是经过简化设计的Transformer，采用多模态流匹配目标进行训练，联合建模离散原子类型与连续三维几何结构。该方法支持可扩展的预训练，且随着模型容量增加可预测性能提升，同时支持快速稳定的采样。我们利用跨域生成预训练作为下游多任务（性质、能量、力预测）的通用初始化。实证表明，在数据可控环境下，Zatom-1在多任务生成与预测基准上优于或媲美专用基线，且生成推理速度提升超过一个数量级。实验表明，联合生成预训练可实现跨数据域的正向预测迁移：在生成预训练中建模材料可提升分子性质预测精度。开源代码与模型权重可免费获取于https://github.com/Zatom-AI/zatom。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified 3D modeling across molecules and materials with both generative and predictive capabilities, Zatom-1 introduces a cross-domain Transformer architecture trained via multimodal flow matching to jointly model discrete atoms and continuous geometries. The model achieves scalable pretraining with consistent performance gains as capacity increases and enables fast, stable sampling. Empirical results show Zatom-1 outperforms or matches specialized baselines on multi-task generative and predictive benchmarks, with over tenfold faster inference, and demonstrates positive transfer: pretraining on materials improves molecular property prediction.
为实现化学领域中分子与材料的统一3D建模并兼顾生成与预测能力，Zatom-1提出一种跨域Transformer架构，通过多模态流匹配目标联合建模离散原子类型与连续3D几何结构。该设计支持随模型规模增长而稳定提升的预训练效果，并实现快速稳定的采样。实验表明，Zatom-1在多任务生成与预测基准上优于或媲美专用模型，推理速度提升超一个数量级，且在联合生成预训练中展现出正向迁移效应：材料预训练可提升分子性质预测精度。
AdaptNC: Adaptive Nonconformity Scores for Conformal Prediction under Distribution Shift
Authors: Renukanandan Tumu, Aditya Singh, Rahul Mangharam
First: 2026-02-02T04:41:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:47:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rigorous uncertainty quantification is essential for the safe deployment of autonomous systems in unconstrained environments. Conformal Prediction (CP) provides a distribution-free framework for this task, yet its standard formulations rely on exchangeability assumptions that are violated by the distribution shifts inherent in real-world robotics. Existing online CP methods maintain target coverage by adaptively scaling the conformal threshold, but typically employ a static nonconformity score function. We show that this fixed geometry leads to highly conservative, volume-inefficient prediction regions when environments undergo structural shifts. To address this, we propose $\textbf{AdaptNC}$, a framework for the joint online adaptation of both the nonconformity score parameters and the conformal threshold. AdaptNC leverages an adaptive reweighting scheme to optimize score functions, and introduces a replay buffer mechanism to mitigate the coverage instability that occurs during score transitions. We evaluate AdaptNC on diverse robotic benchmarks involving multi-agent policy changes, environmental changes and sensor degradation. Our results demonstrate that AdaptNC significantly reduces prediction region volume compared to state-of-the-art threshold-only baselines while maintaining target coverage levels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdaptNC：面向分布偏移的自适应非符合性得分用于校准预测
在无约束环境中安全部署自主系统需要严格的不确定性量化。校准预测（CP）为此提供了一个无分布假设的框架，但其标准形式依赖于交换性假设，而真实世界机器人中的分布偏移会破坏该假设。现有在线CP方法通过自适应调整校准阈值来维持目标覆盖率，但通常采用静态非符合性得分函数。我们证明，这种固定几何结构在环境发生结构变化时会导致高度保守且体积低效的预测区域。为此，我们提出AdaptNC框架，用于同时在线自适应调整非符合性得分参数和校准阈值。AdaptNC采用自适应重加权方案优化得分函数，并引入回放缓冲机制以缓解得分转换期间的覆盖率不稳定性。我们在涉及多智能体策略变化、环境变化和传感器退化等多种机器人基准上评估AdaptNC。结果表明，与最先进的仅阈值调整基线相比，AdaptNC显著降低了预测区域体积，同时保持目标覆盖率水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable uncertainty quantification in autonomous systems under distribution shifts, this paper proposes AdaptNC, a method that jointly adapts both the nonconformity score parameters and conformal threshold in online conformal prediction. Unlike prior approaches that fix the score function, AdaptNC employs an adaptive reweighting scheme to optimize scores and a replay buffer to stabilize coverage during transitions. Experiments on robotic benchmarks with policy changes, environmental shifts, and sensor degradation show that AdaptNC reduces prediction region volume significantly while preserving target coverage levels compared to state-of-the-art threshold-only methods.
为应对自主系统在分布偏移环境下对不确定性量化的需求，本文提出AdaptNC框架，该框架在线自适应调整非一致性得分参数与置信阈值。与传统仅调整阈值的方法不同，AdaptNC采用自适应重加权机制优化得分函数，并引入回放缓冲机制以稳定得分转换期间的覆盖性。在涉及策略变化、环境变化和传感器退化的机器人基准测试中，实验表明AdaptNC显著缩小预测区域体积，同时保持目标覆盖率不变。
Loiter UAV Reinsertion Guidance for Fixed-wing UAV Corridors
Authors: Pradeep J, Kedarisetty Siddhardha, Ashwini Ratnoo
Venue: AIAA SCITECH 2026
First: 2026-05-13T17:46:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:46:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper considers fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) corridors comprising a main lane, a circular loiter lane for managing traffic congestion, and transit lanes connecting the two. In particular, we address the problem of conflict-free reinsertion of UAVs from the loiter lane back into the main lane. The loiter lane contains a fixed number of equidistant virtual slots that UAVs can occupy. Reinsertion of loiter UAVs into the main lane becomes essential either due to reduced traffic in the main lane or due to a loiter UAV needing to reach its destination urgently. Given the total number of loiter slots, UAV speed limits, and the minimum safety distance, a guidance algorithm is developed to compute the required speed of a loiter UAV in the transit lane to ensure safe reinsertion. The proposed guidance and automation strategies are validated through numerical simulations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：固定翼无人机走廊中徘徊无人机的重新插入引导
本文考虑由主通道、用于管理交通拥堵的圆形徘徊通道及连接两者的过渡通道组成的固定翼无人机（UAV）走廊。特别地，我们解决了从徘徊通道无冲突重新插入主通道的无人机问题。徘徊通道包含固定数量的等距虚拟位置，无人机可占据这些位置。当主通道交通减少或徘徊无人机需紧急前往目的地时，重新插入变得必要。在给定总徘徊槽位数、无人机速度限制和最小安全距离条件下，提出了一种引导算法，用于计算徘徊无人机在过渡通道中所需的速度，以确保安全重新插入。所提出的引导与自动化策略通过数值仿真进行了验证。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of safely reinserting fixed-wing UAVs from a circular loiter lane back into a main corridor lane, particularly when traffic congestion requires dynamic management. The method introduces a guidance algorithm that computes optimal transit lane speeds for loiter UAVs based on fixed virtual slot spacing, UAV speed limits, and minimum safety distances. Experimental simulations validate that the algorithm enables conflict-free reinsertion under varying traffic conditions, ensuring safe and efficient corridor operations.
该文针对固定翼无人机从环形盘旋道安全返回主航道的问题，旨在解决交通拥堵管理和紧急任务切换需求。方法基于固定虚拟槽位间距、无人机速度限制和最小安全距离，设计了一种计算盘旋无人机在过渡车道所需速度的引导算法。仿真验证表明，该算法能在不同交通条件下确保无冲突安全重入，满足安全约束。
Assessment of cloud and associated radiation fields from a GAN stochastic cloud subcolumn generator
Authors: Dongmin Lee, Lazaros Oreopoulos, Nayeong Cho, Daeho Jin
First: 2026-05-12T11:19:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:46:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern Earth System Models (ESMs) operate on horizontal scales far larger than typical cloud features, requiring stochastic subcolumn generators to represent subgrid horizontal and vertical cloud variability. Traditional physically-based generators often rely on analytical cloud overlap paradigms, such as exponential-random decorrelation, which can struggle to capture the complex, anti-correlated behavior of non-contiguous cloud layers. In this study, we introduce a novel two-stage machine learning subcolumn generator for the GEOS atmospheric model, utilizing a Conditional Variational Autoencoder combined with a Generative Adversarial Network (CVAE-GAN) and a U-Net architecture. Trained on a merged CloudSat-CALIPSO height-resolved cloud optical depth dataset, the ML generator creates 56 stochastic subcolumns representing cloud occurrence and optical depth profiles. Evaluated against the established Räisänen, the ML approach accurately reproduces bimodal cloud overlap distributions, significantly reduces biases in grid-mean statistics, and halves the root-mean-square error in ISCCP-style cloud-top pressure and optical thickness joint histograms. The improvements brought by our deep generative models translate into more accurate offline radiative transfer calculations, reducing the global-mean shortwave top-of-atmosphere cloud radiative effect bias by a factor of three. Provided that the generator can be accelerated on CPUs, this offers a practical pathway to reduce structural errors at the cloud-radiation interface.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于GAN随机云子列生成器的云及相关辐射场评估
现代地球系统模型（ESMs）的水平分辨率远大于典型云特征，需使用随机子列生成器以表征亚网格水平和垂直云变异性。传统基于物理的生成器常依赖分析性云重叠范式（如指数随机去相关），难以捕捉非连续云层的复杂反相关行为。本研究为GEOS大气模型引入一种新颖的两阶段机器学习子列生成器，结合条件变分自编码器（CVAE）与生成对抗网络（GAN）及U-Net架构。该生成器基于融合的CloudSat-CALIPSO高度分辨云光学厚度数据集训练，可生成56个随机子列，表征云发生频率与光学厚度剖面。与经典Räisänen方法对比，该机器学习方法准确再现双峰云重叠分布，显著降低网格平均统计偏差，并将ISCCP风格云顶气压与光学厚度联合直方图的均方根误差减半。深度生成模型带来的改进使离线辐射传输计算更准确，全球平均短波顶大气云辐射效应偏差降低三倍。若该生成器可在CPU上加速，则为减少云-辐射界面结构误差提供实用路径。

Driven by the need to better represent subgrid cloud variability in Earth System Models, this study introduces a two-stage machine learning subcolumn generator for the GEOS model, combining a Conditional Variational Autoencoder with a Generative Adversarial Network and a U-Net. Harnessing Agentic Evolution
Authors: Jiayi Zhang, Yongfeng Gu, Jianhao Ruan, Maojia Song, Yiran Peng, Zhiguang Han, Jinyu Xiang, Zhitao Wang, Caiyin Yang, Yixi Ouyang, Bang Liu, Chenglin Wu, Yuyu Luo
First: 2026-05-13T17:45:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:45:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic evolution has emerged as a powerful paradigm for improving programs, workflows, and scientific solutions by iteratively generating candidates, evaluating them, and using feedback to guide future search. However, existing methods are typically instantiated either as fixed hand-designed procedures that are modular but rigid, or as general-purpose agents that flexibly integrate feedback but can drift in long-horizon evolution. Both forms accumulate rich evidence over time, including candidates, feedback, traces, and failures, yet lack a stable interface for organizing this evidence and revising the mechanism that drives future evolution. We address this limitation by formulating agentic evolution as an interactive environment, where the accumulated evolution context serves as a process-level state. We introduce AEvo, a harnessed meta-editing framework in which a meta-agent observes this state and acts not by directly proposing the next candidate, but by editing the procedure or agent context that controls future evolution. This unified interface enables AEvo to steer both procedure-based and agent-based evolution, making accumulated evidence actionable for long-horizon search. Empirical evaluations on agentic and reasoning benchmarks show that AEvo outperforms five evolution baselines, achieving a 26 relative improvement over the strongest baseline. Across three open-ended optimization tasks, AEvo further outperforms four evolution baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance under the same iteration budget.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用代理演化
代理演化作为一种通过迭代生成候选方案、评估并利用反馈指导未来搜索的强大范式，已用于改进程序、工作流程和科学解决方案。然而，现有方法通常表现为固定的手动设计过程（模块化但僵化），或通用代理（灵活整合反馈但长期演化中易漂移）。两者均随时间积累丰富证据（包括候选方案、反馈、轨迹和失败案例），但缺乏稳定接口来组织这些证据并修订驱动未来演化的机制。我们通过将代理演化建模为交互式环境，将累积的演化上下文作为过程级状态来解决此问题。我们提出AEvo，一种受控的元编辑框架，其中元代理观察该状态，并非直接提出下一候选方案，而是编辑控制未来演化的程序或代理上下文。这一统一接口使AEvo能够同时引导基于程序和基于代理的演化，使累积证据在长周期搜索中可操作。在代理和推理基准上的实证评估表明，AEvo优于五个演化基线，相较最强基线提升26%。在三个开放式优化任务中，AEvo进一步超越四个演化基线，并在相同迭代预算下实现当前最优性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of rigid hand-designed procedures and drifting general-purpose agents in agentic evolution, this work introduces AEvo, a meta-editing framework that treats accumulated evolution context as a process-level state. AEvo employs a meta-agent to observe and modify the underlying procedure or agent context rather than directly proposing candidates, enabling unified control over both procedure-based and agent-based evolution. Experimental results across agentic and reasoning benchmarks show AEvo outperforms five baselines with a 26% relative improvement over the strongest, and achieves state-of-the-art performance on three open-ended optimization tasks under the same iteration budget.
为解决现有代理进化方法中固定模块化流程僵化或通用代理长期漂移的问题，本文提出AEvo框架，将累积的进化上下文视为过程级状态，由元代理对其进行编辑而非直接生成候选方案，从而统一控制基于流程与基于代理的进化。实验表明，AEvo在多个代理与推理基准上优于五种基线方法，相较最强基线提升26%，并在三项开放式优化任务中超越四种基线，达到相同迭代预算下的最先进性能。
Neurosymbolic Auditing of Natural-Language Software Requirements
Authors: Bethel Hall, William Eiers
First: 2026-05-13T17:43:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:43:13+00:00
Comments: 10
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Natural-language software requirements are often ambiguous, inconsistent, and underspecified; in safety-critical domains, these defects propagate into formal models that verify the wrong specification and into implementations that ship unsafe behavior. We show that large language models, equipped with an SMT solver, can audit such requirements: translating them into formal logic, detecting ambiguity through stochastic variation in the generated formalization, and exposing inconsistency, vacuousness, and safety violations through solver queries on the resulting specification. We present VERIMED, a neurosymbolic pipeline that operationalizes this idea for medical-device software requirements, and report two findings. First, stochastic variation across independent formalizations is a signal of ambiguity: requirements that admit multiple plausible interpretations produce SMT-inequivalent formalizations, and bidirectional SMT equivalence checking turns this disagreement into a solver-checkable test. Second, the usefulness of symbolic feedback depends on its granularity: in counterexample-guided repair on a hemodialysis question-answering benchmark, concrete SMT counterexamples raise verified accuracy from 55.4% to 98.5%. Over an extensive experimental evaluation on open-source hemodialysis safety requirements, we show that the LLM-based approach in VERIMED successfully reduces ambiguity-sensitive requirements and enables rigorous auditing of software requirements through SMT-based queries.

标题：自然语言软件需求的神经符号审计
自然语言软件需求常存在歧义、不一致和规格不完整的问题；在安全关键领域，这些缺陷会传播到用于验证的正式模型和实际实现中，导致验证错误的规格和不安全的行为。我们表明，配备SMT求解器的大语言模型可以审计此类需求：将其翻译为形式逻辑，通过生成形式化过程中的随机变化检测歧义，并通过求解器查询揭示不一致、空洞性和安全性违规。我们提出了VERIMED，一个针对医疗设备软件需求的神经符号管道，报告了两项发现。首先，独立形式化之间的随机变化是歧义的信号：允许多种合理解释的需求会产生SMT不等价的形式化，双向SMT等价性检查可将这种分歧转化为可由求解器验证的测试。其次，符号反馈的有效性取决于其粒度：在血液透析问答基准上的反例引导修复中，具体的SMT反例将验证准确率从55.4%提升至98.5%。在对开源血液透析安全需求的广泛实验评估中，我们证明了VERIMED中的基于LLM的方法成功减少了对歧义敏感的需求，并通过基于SMT的查询实现了软件需求的严格审计。

Motivated by the ambiguity and inconsistency in natural-language software requirements—especially in safety-critical domains like medical devices—the paper proposes VERIMED, a neurosymbolic auditing pipeline that leverages large language models (LLMs) integrated with an SMT solver. The method translates requirements into formal logic, detects ambiguity by analyzing stochastic variation in multiple formalizations, and identifies inconsistencies or safety violations via SMT queries. Experimental results show that stochastic variation reliably signals ambiguity, and that concrete SMT counterexamples significantly improve repair accuracy—from 55.4% to 98.5%—demonstrating the effectiveness of symbolic feedback in reducing ambiguity-sensitive requirements and enabling rigorous auditing.

Uncertainty-Driven Anomaly Detection for Psychotic Relapse Using Smartwatches: Forecasting and Multi-Task Learning Fusion
Authors: Nikolaos Tsalkitzis, Panagiotis P. Filntisis, Petros Maragos, Niki Efthymiou
First: 2026-05-13T17:43:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:43:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Digital phenotyping enables continuous passive monitoring of behavior and physiology, offering a promising paradigm for early detection of psychotic relapse. In this work, we develop and systematically study two smartwatch-based frameworks for daily relapse detection. The first forecasts cardiac dynamics and flags deviations between predicted and observed features as indicators of abnormality. The second adopts a multi-task formulation that fuses sleep with motion and cardiac-derived signals, learning time-aware embeddings and predicting measurement timing. Both pipelines use Transformer encoders and output a daily anomaly score, derived from predictive uncertainty estimated via an ensemble of multilayer perceptrons to improve robustness to real-world wearable variability. While each framework independently demonstrates strong predictive power, we show that they capture complementary physiological signatures. Consequently, we propose a late-fusion strategy that synergistically combines the anomaly signals from both architectures into a unified decision score. We benchmark our methodology on the 2nd e-Prevention Grand Challenge dataset, where our fused model achieves a 8% relative improvement over the competition-winning baseline. Our results, supported by extensive ablation studies, suggest that the integration of diverse digital phenotypes, cardiac, motion, and sleep, is essential for the high-fidelity detection of psychotic relapse in real-world settings.

OmniLiDAR: A Unified Diffusion Framework for Multi-Domain 3D LiDAR Generation
Authors: Youquan Liu, Weidong Yang, Ao Liang, Xiang Xu, Lingdong Kong, Yang Wu, Dekai Zhu, Xin Li, Runnan Chen, Ben Fei, Tongliang Liu, Wanli Ouyang
First: 2026-05-13T17:42:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:42:20+00:00
Comments: Preprint; 12 pages, 7 figures, 10 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LiDAR scene generation is increasingly important for scalable simulation and synthetic data creation, especially under diverse sensing conditions that are costly to capture at scale. Typically, diffusion-based LiDAR generators are developed under single-domain settings, requiring separate models for different datasets or sensing conditions and hindering unified, controllable synthesis under heterogeneous distribution shifts. To this end, we present OmniLiDAR, a unified text-conditioned diffusion framework that generates LiDAR scans in a shared range-image representation across eight representative domains spanning three shift types: adverse weather, sensor-configuration changes (e.g., reduced beams), and cross-platform acquisition (vehicle, drone, and quadruped). To enable training a single model over heterogeneous domains without isolating optimization by domain, we introduce a Cross-Domain Training Strategy (CDTS) that mixes domains within each mini-batch and leverages conditioning to steer generation. We further propose Cross-Domain Feature Modeling (CDFM), which captures directional dependencies along azimuth and elevation axes to reflect the anisotropic scanning structure of range images, and Domain-Adaptive Feature Scaling (DAFS) as a lightweight modulation to account for structured domain-dependent feature shifts during denoising. In the absence of a public consolidated benchmark, we construct an 8-domain dataset by combining real-world scans with physically based weather simulation and systematic beam reduction while following official splits. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong generation fidelity and consistent gains in downstream use cases, including generative data augmentation for LiDAR semantic segmentation and 3D object detection, as well as robustness evaluation under corruptions, with consistent benefits in limited-label regimes.
