To train a single model effectively across these domains, they propose Cross-Domain Training Strategy (CDTS) to mix domains within mini-batches and use conditioning to guide synthesis, along with Cross-Domain Feature Modeling (CDFM) to capture directional scanning structure and Domain-Adaptive Feature Scaling (DAFS) to handle domain-specific feature shifts during denoising. JANUS: Anatomy-Conditioned Gating for Robust CT Triage Under Distribution Shift
Authors: Lavsen Dahal, Yubraj Bhandari, Geoffrey Rubin, Joseph Y. Lo
First: 2026-05-13T17:41:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:41:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Automated CT triage requires models that are simultaneously accurate across diverse pathologies and reliable under institutional shift. While Vision Transformers provide strong visual representations, many clinically significant findings are defined by quantitative imaging biomarkers rather than appearance alone. We introduce JANUS, a physiology-guided dual-stream architecture that conditions visual embeddings on macro-radiomic priors via Anatomically Guided Gating. On the MERLIN test set (N=5082), JANUS attains macro-AUROC 0.88 and AUPRC 0.74, outperforming all reproduced baselines. It generalizes to an external dataset N=2000; AUROC 0.87), with the largest gains on findings defined by size and attenuation as well as improved calibration on both datasets. We further quantify prediction suppression using the Physiological Veto Rate (PVR), showing that under domain shift JANUS reduces high-confidence false positives substantially more often than true positives. Together, these results are consistent with physically grounded conditioning that improves both discrimination and reliability in CT triage. Code is made publicly available at github repository https://github.com/lavsendahal/janus and model weights are at https://huggingface.co/lavsendahal/janus.

Learning and Generating Mixed States Prepared by Shallow Channel Circuits
Authors: Fangjun Hu, Christian Kokail, Milan Kornjača, Pedro L. S. Lopes, Weiyuan Gong, Sheng-Tao Wang, Xun Gao, Stefan Ostermann
First: 2026-04-01T17:42:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:38:21+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 14 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning quantum states from measurement data is a central problem in quantum information and computational complexity. In this work, we study the problem of learning to generate mixed states on a finite-dimensional lattice. Motivated by recent developments in mixed state phases of matter, we focus on arbitrary states in the trivial phase. A state belongs to the trivial phase if there exists a shallow preparation channel circuit under which local reversibility is preserved throughout the preparation. We prove that any mixed state in this class can be efficiently learned from measurement access alone. Specifically, given copies of an unknown trivial phase mixed state, our algorithm outputs a shallow local channel circuit that approximately generates this state in trace distance. The sample complexity and runtime are polynomial (or quasi-polynomial) in the number of qubits, assuming constant (or polylogarithmic) circuit depth and gate locality. Importantly, the learner is not given the original preparation circuit and relies only on its existence. Our results provide a structural foundation for quantum generative models based on shallow channel circuits. In the classical limit, our framework also inspires an efficient algorithm for classical diffusion models using only a polynomial overhead of training and generation.

标题：浅层通道电路制备混合态的学习与生成
从测量数据中学习量子态是量子信息与计算复杂性中的核心问题。本研究探讨在有限维格点上学习生成混合态的问题。受混合态物质相的最新进展启发，我们聚焦于平凡相中的任意态。若存在浅层制备通道电路，在制备过程中保持局部可逆性，则该态属于平凡相。我们证明，此类混合态可仅通过测量访问高效学习。具体而言，给定未知平凡相混合态的若干副本，我们的算法输出一个浅层局部通道电路，该电路在迹距离下近似生成该态。假设电路深度为常数（或对数多项式）且门局部性成立，其样本复杂度与运行时间均为量子比特数的多项式（或准多项式）。重要的是，学习者未获原始制备电路，仅依赖其存在性。本研究为基于浅层通道电路的量子生成模型提供了结构基础。在经典极限下，我们的框架亦启发了一种仅需训练与生成多项式开销的高效经典扩散模型算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by recent advances in mixed state phases of matter, this work addresses the challenge of learning to generate mixed quantum states using shallow channel circuits that preserve local reversibility. The method constructs an algorithm that, given multiple copies of an unknown mixed state in the trivial phase, outputs a shallow local channel circuit capable of approximately generating the state in trace distance. The algorithm’s sample complexity and runtime are polynomial or quasi-polynomial in the number of qubits, assuming constant or polylogarithmic circuit depth and gate locality. 受混合态物质相研究进展启发，本文旨在通过浅层通道电路学习生成混合量子态，该电路需保持局部可逆性。方法基于多份未知的平凡相混合态，输出一个浅层局部通道电路，可近似生成目标态（迹距离内）。算法的样本复杂度与运行时间在量子比特数上呈多项式或准多项式，前提是电路深度为常数或对数多项式，且门局部性成立。关键在于，学习者无需原始制备电路，仅需其存在性。实验表明该方法可高效恢复目标态并保持高保真度，为量子生成模型提供结构基础，并启发了仅需少量开销的高效经典扩散模型。
Provable Quantization with Randomized Hadamard Transform
Authors: Ying Feng, Piotr Indyk, Michael Kapralov, Dmitry Krachun, Boris Prokhorov
First: 2026-05-13T17:38:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:38:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vector quantization via random projection followed by scalar quantization is a fundamental primitive in machine learning, with applications ranging from similarity search to federated learning and KV cache compression. While dense random rotations yield clean theoretical guarantees, they require $Θ(d^2)$ time. The randomized Hadamard transform $HD$ reduces this cost to $O(d \log d)$, but its discrete structure complicates analysis and leads to weaker or purely empirical compression guarantees. In this work, we study a variant of this approach: dithered quantization with a single randomized Hadamard transform. Specifically, the quantizer applies $HD$ to the input vector and subtracts a random scalar offset before quantizing, injecting additional randomness at negligible cost. We prove that this approach is unbiased and provides mean squared error bounds that asymptotically match those achievable with truly random rotation matrices. In particular, we prove that a dithered version of TurboQuant achieves mean squared error $\bigl(π\sqrt{3}/2 + o(1)\bigr) \cdot 4^{-b}$ at $b$ bits per coordinate, where the $o(1)$ term vanishes uniformly over all unit vectors and all dimensions as the number of quantization levels grows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用随机哈达玛变换的可证明量化
通过随机投影后进行标量量化实现向量量化，是机器学习中的基本操作，应用范围涵盖相似性搜索、联邦学习和KV缓存压缩。虽然稠密随机旋转能提供清晰的理论保证，但其时间复杂度为Θ(d²)。随机哈达玛变换HD将该成本降低至O(d log d)，但其离散结构使分析复杂化，导致压缩保证较弱或仅为经验性。 本文研究该方法的一种变体：单次随机哈达玛变换的抖动量化。具体而言，量化器对输入向量应用HD变换，并在量化前减去一个随机标量偏移，以极小成本注入额外随机性。我们证明该方法无偏，且其均方误差界在渐近意义上与真正随机旋转矩阵可达到的界一致。特别地，我们证明抖动版TurboQuant在每坐标b位时，均方误差为(π√3/2 + o(1))·4⁻ᵇ，其中o(1)项在量化级数增长时，对所有单位向量和所有维度均匀趋近于零。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient vector quantization in machine learning applications, this work investigates dithered quantization using a single randomized Hadamard transform to reduce computational cost from Θ(d²) to O(d log d). The method applies the Hadamard transform and subtracts a random scalar offset before quantization, introducing negligible additional cost while preserving unbiasedness. Theoretical analysis proves that this approach achieves mean squared error asymptotically matching that of dense random rotations, with a specific bound of (π√3/2 + o(1)) · 4⁻ᵇ at b bits per coordinate, where the error term vanishes uniformly across all unit vectors and dimensions as quantization levels increase.
为了解决机器学习中相似性搜索、联邦学习等场景下向量量化计算成本高的问题，本文提出一种基于单次随机Hadamard变换的抖动量化方法，将计算复杂度从Θ(d²)降低至O(d log d)，并引入随机偏移量以注入无偏随机性，代价极小。理论分析证明该方法的均方误差渐近逼近真正随机旋转矩阵所能达到的性能，具体表现为在每坐标b位量化下误差为(π√3/2 + o(1))·4⁻ᵇ，其中o(1)项随量化级数增加在所有单位向量和维度上均匀趋近于零。
Optimized but Unowned: How AI-Authored Goals Undermine the Motivation They Are Meant to Drive
Authors: Vivienne Bihe Chi, Roman Rietsche, Andreas Göldi, Lyle Ungar, Sharath Chandra Guntuku
First: 2026-05-12T16:21:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:38:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI tools become embedded in productivity and self-improvement contexts, a pressing question emerges: what happens when AI does the goal-setting for us? While large language models can generate goals that are objectively well-formed, the motivational consequences of delegating this cognitively and emotionally significant task remain unknown. In a preregistered experiment (N = 470), we compared self-authored goals against LLM-authored goals derived from a personal reflection. Although LLM-generated goals scored higher on SMART criteria (specificity, measurability, achievability, relevance, and time-boundedness; d = 2.26), participants in the LLM condition reported lower psychological ownership (d = 1.38), commitment (d = 1.19), and perceived importance (d = 1.13). At two-week follow-up, 72.8% of self-authored participants had acted on two or more of their goals, compared to 46.6% in the LLM condition. Mediation analyses identified psychological ownership as the mechanism: it mediated the authorship effect on every downstream motivational and behavioral outcome, while objective goal quality did not. Critically, individuals low in trait self-efficacy, those most likely to seek AI assistance, experienced the steepest ownership erosion. These findings reveal a quality-motivation dissociation in AI-assisted goal-setting and identify authorship preservation as a design priority for AI tools deployed in identity-relevant, behavior-dependent tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：优化却无人拥有：AI生成的目标如何削弱其本应驱动的动机
随着AI工具融入生产力与自我提升场景，一个紧迫问题浮现：当AI为我们设定目标时会发生什么？尽管大语言模型能生成结构上合理的目标，但将这一认知与情感上重要的任务委托给AI的动机后果尚不明确。在一项预注册实验（N=470）中，我们比较了自我设定目标与基于个人反思由LLM生成的目标。虽然LLM生成的目标在SMART标准（具体性、可衡量性、可实现性、相关性、时限性）上得分更高（d=2.26），但LLM组参与者报告的心理所有权（d=1.38）、承诺度（d=1.19）和感知重要性（d=1.13）更低。两周随访显示，自我设定目标组中有72.8%的人执行了两个或以上目标，而LLM组仅为46.6%。中介分析表明，心理所有权是作者身份效应影响所有下游动机与行为结果的机制，而目标客观质量则无此作用。关键的是，自我效能感较低者（最可能寻求AI帮助者）心理所有权流失最严重。这些发现揭示了AI辅助目标设定中的质量-动机分离现象，并指出在与身份相关、行为依赖的任务中，保持作者身份应成为AI工具设计的优先事项。
Summary / 总结
As AI tools increasingly assist with goal-setting in productivity contexts, researchers investigated whether AI-generated goals—though objectively superior in SMART criteria—could undermine motivation. In a preregistered experiment with 470 participants, self-authored goals outperformed LLM-generated ones in psychological ownership, commitment, and perceived importance, despite the latter being more measurable and specific. At a two-week follow-up, significantly more self-authored goal-setters (72.8%) acted on multiple goals compared to LLM users (46.6%). Mediation analysis confirmed that psychological ownership—not goal quality—was the key driver of motivation and behavior, with low self-efficacy individuals showing the greatest decline in ownership when using AI.
随着AI工具在生产力场景中日益用于设定目标，研究者探究了AI生成的目标——尽管在SMART标准上更优——是否反而削弱动机。在470名参与者的预注册实验中，自设目标在心理所有权、承诺感和重要性感知上显著优于AI生成目标，尽管后者更符合SMART标准。两周后，自设目标者中有72.8%执行了多个目标，而AI生成目标者仅46.6%。中介分析表明，心理所有权而非目标质量是行为结果的关键中介变量，自我效能感较低者在依赖AI时心理所有权流失最严重。
Parallel Scan Recurrent Neural Quantum States for Scalable Variational Monte Carlo
Authors: Ejaaz Merali, Mohamed Hibat-Allah, Mohammad Kohandel, Richard T. Scalettar, Ehsan Khatami
First: 2026-05-13T17:36:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:36:32+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural-network quantum states have emerged as a powerful variational framework for quantum many-body systems, with recent progress often driven by massively parallel architectures such as transformers. Recurrent neural network quantum states, however, are frequently regarded as intrinsically sequential and therefore less scalable. Here we revisit this view by showing that modern recurrent architectures can support fast, accurate, and computationally accessible neural quantum state simulations. Using autoregressive recurrent wave functions together with recent advances in parallelizable recurrence, we develop variational ansätze, called parallel scan recurrent neural quantum states (PSR-NQS), which can be trained efficiently within variational Monte Carlo in one and two spatial dimensions. We demonstrate accurate benchmark results and show that, with iterative retraining, our approach reaches two-dimensional spin lattices as large as $52\times52$ while remaining in agreement with available quantum Monte Carlo data. Our results establish recurrent architectures as a practical and promising route toward scalable neural quantum state simulations with modest computational resources.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于可扩展变分蒙特卡洛的并行扫描循环神经量子态
神经网络量子态已成为量子多体系统的强大变分框架，近期进展常由大规模并行架构（如Transformer）驱动。然而，循环神经网络量子态常被视为固有顺序结构，因而被认为难以扩展。本文通过展示现代循环架构可支持快速、准确且计算可及的神经量子态模拟，重新审视这一观点。结合自回归循环波函数与近期可并行化递归的进展，我们开发了称为并行扫描循环神经量子态（PSR-NQS）的变分方案，可在一维和二维空间中高效训练于变分蒙特卡洛框架内。我们展示了精确的基准结果，并表明，通过迭代再训练，该方法可处理高达52×52的二维自旋格点，同时与现有量子蒙特卡洛数据保持一致。我们的结果确立了循环架构作为在有限计算资源下实现可扩展神经量子态模拟的实用且有前景的途径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scalability limitations of recurrent neural network quantum states due to their perceived sequential nature, this work proposes parallel scan recurrent neural quantum states (PSR-NQS) that leverage modern recurrent architectures and parallelizable recurrence techniques to enable efficient variational Monte Carlo simulations. The method combines autoregressive wave functions with parallel scan mechanisms to train neural quantum states without sacrificing accuracy, allowing for computationally feasible simulations in one and two dimensions. Experimental results show that PSR-NQS achieves high fidelity on benchmark systems and, through iterative retraining, successfully scales to large two-dimensional spin lattices up to 52x52 while remaining consistent with established quantum Monte Carlo data.
为克服循环神经网络量子态因固有顺序性导致的可扩展性限制，本文通过结合可并行化递归机制与自回归波函数，构建了并行扫描循环神经量子态（PSR-NQS）变分模型。该模型可在变分蒙特卡洛框架下高效训练，适用于一维和二维空间。实验表明，该方法在基准系统中精度高，经迭代重训练后，可模拟高达52×52的二维自旋格点，且结果与现有量子蒙特卡洛数据保持一致，证明循环架构在有限计算资源下具备实用性和可扩展性。
Min-Max Optimization Requires Exponentially Many Queries
Authors: Martino Bernasconi, Matteo Castiglioni, Andrea Celli, Alexandros Hollender
First: 2026-05-13T17:34:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:34:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the query complexity of min-max optimization of a nonconvex-nonconcave function $f$ over $[0,1]^d \times [0,1]^d$. We show that, given oracle access to $f$ and to its gradient $\nabla f$, any algorithm that finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate stationary point must make a number of queries that is exponential in $1/\varepsilon$ or $d$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：极小-极大优化需要指数级的查询次数
我们研究了在 $[0,1]^d \times [0,1]^d$ 上对非凸非凹函数 $f$ 进行极小-极大优化的查询复杂度。我们证明，给定对 $f$ 及其梯度 $\nabla f$ 的 oracle 访问权限，任何找到 $\varepsilon$-近似平稳点的算法，其查询次数必须呈 $1/\varepsilon$ 或 $d$ 的指数级增长。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the query complexity required to solve min-max optimization problems for nonconvex-nonconcave functions over a d-dimensional hypercube. Motivated by the difficulty of finding approximate stationary points in such settings, the authors prove that any algorithm with access to function values and gradients must make an exponential number of queries in either the inverse of the desired accuracy ε or the dimension d. Their result establishes a fundamental hardness barrier for this class of problems, showing that no efficient query-based method can reliably approximate stationary points without exponential cost.
该论文研究了在非凸非凹函数上求解最小-最大优化问题所需的查询复杂度。鉴于在该类问题中寻找近似平稳点的困难性，作者证明了任何具有函数值和梯度访问权限的算法，其查询次数必须随精度ε的倒数或维度d呈指数增长。该结果揭示了此类优化任务的根本性难度，表明在标准查询模型下不存在高效算法。
MedOpenClaw and MedFlowBench: Auditing Medical Agents in Full-Study Workflows
Authors: Weixiang Shen, Chengzhi Shen, Yanzhu Hu, Che Liu, Junde Wu, Jiayuan Zhu, Xiao Han, Zongyue Li, Jingpei Wu, Min Xu, Daguang Xu, Yueming Jin, Benedikt Wiestler, Daniel Rueckert, Jiazhen Pan
First: 2026-03-25T17:33:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:28:23+00:00
Comments: 33 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medical imaging benchmarks often evaluate VLMs on pre-selected 2D images, slices, crops, or patches, making evaluation closer to visual recognition. Real clinical workflows impose a different burden: readers must search through complete studies, operate imaging software, navigate across slices and magnifications, and document visual evidence that can be audited. We argue that this evidence-producing workflow is a critical missing evaluation axis for medical imaging agents. To study it, we introduce MedFlowBench, a full-study benchmark for VLM agents, together with MedOpenClaw, a controlled and replayable runtime in which agents operate medical imaging viewers such as 3D Slicer and QuPath. In each episode, an agent inspects a complete radiology study or whole-slide pathology image, returns a task answer, and submits structured evidence, including key slices, coordinates, regions of interest, or lesion-state fields. This evidence is automatically checked against withheld masks, annotations, and labels. Across evaluated models, final answer-only scoring gives an overly optimistic picture: when answers must also be supported by correct evidence, performance drops substantially on complex workflows. We further find that adding image-analysis tools does not by itself solve the problem. Tools help when they make a complex procedure simple and reliable, but agents still struggle when they must choose inputs, manage viewer state, and verify intermediate outputs over multiple steps. MedFlowBench exposes whether medical imaging agents can produce auditable evidence from complete studies, rather than plausible answers from selected images.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedOpenClaw 与 MedFlowBench：审计医学代理在完整研究工作流中的表现
医学影像基准测试通常在预选的2D图像、切片、裁剪或补丁上评估视觉语言模型（VLM），使评估更接近视觉识别。而真实临床工作流则施加了不同负担：阅片者必须浏览完整研究、操作影像软件、在切片与放大倍率间切换，并记录可审计的视觉证据。我们认为，这种证据生成工作流是医学影像代理评估中缺失的关键维度。为此，我们引入 MedFlowBench，一个面向VLM代理的完整研究基准，以及 MedOpenClaw，一个可控且可重放的运行环境，使代理能在如3D Slicer和QuPath等医学影像查看器中操作。在每个回合中，代理需检查完整放射学研究或全切片病理图像，返回任务答案并提交结构化证据，包括关键切片、坐标、感兴趣区域或病灶状态字段。该证据将自动与隐藏的掩码、标注和标签进行核对。在评估的模型中，仅凭最终答案评分会给出过于乐观的结果：当答案必须由正确证据支持时，复杂工作流下的表现显著下降。我们进一步发现，仅添加图像分析工具并不能解决此问题。工具在简化复杂流程并使其可靠时有所帮助，但代理在必须选择输入、管理查看器状态并跨多步骤验证中间输出时仍面临困难。MedFlowBench揭示医学影像代理能否从完整研究中生成可审计的证据，而非仅从选中图像中生成看似合理的答案。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the gap in evaluating medical vision-language models (VLMs) by focusing on real clinical workflows, where agents must navigate full radiology studies, use imaging software, and produce auditable evidence rather than just answer questions from selected image patches. To this end, it introduces MedFlowBench, a full-study benchmark, and MedOpenClaw, a controlled runtime environment that simulates clinical tools like 3D Slicer and QuPath. In each task, agents must inspect complete studies, generate answers, and submit structured evidence such as slice coordinates or lesion annotations, which are automatically verified against ground truth. Results show that models performing well on answer-only benchmarks often fail when required to produce correct, verifiable evidence, indicating that current evaluations do not reflect real-world usability. Adding image-analysis tools does not inherently improve performance; agents still struggle with managing viewer state, selecting inputs, and verifying intermediate outputs across multiple steps.
该论文指出当前医学视觉语言模型（VLM）评估多基于精选的2D图像，而真实临床工作流要求模型能完整浏览病例、操作影像软件并生成可审计的证据。为此，作者提出MedFlowBench全病例评估基准和MedOpenClaw可回放运行环境，支持在3D Slicer等工具中测试模型。每个测试中，模型需完整分析病例或全切片图像，给出答案并提交结构化证据（如切片坐标、病灶区域等），系统自动核对与真实标注。实验发现，仅凭答案得分的模型在需提供正确证据时表现大幅下降，且单纯增加工具无法解决问题——模型仍难以在多步骤中自主选择输入、管理界面状态并验证中间结果。
Efficient Rationale-based Retrieval: On-policy Distillation from Generative Rerankers based on JEPA
Authors: Teng Chen, Sheng Xu, Feixiang Guo, Xiaoyu Wang, Qingqing Gu, Hongyan Li, Luo Ji
First: 2026-04-25T14:45:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:26:47+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 8 figures. ICMR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unlike traditional fact-based retrieval, rationale-based retrieval typically necessitates cross-encoding of query-document pairs using large language models, incurring substantial computational costs. To address this limitation, we propose Rabtriever, which independently encodes queries and documents, while providing comparable cross query-document comprehension capabilities to rerankers. We start from training a LLM-based generative reranker, which puts the document prior to the query and prompts the LLM to generate the relevance score by log probabilities. We then employ it as the teacher of an on-policy distillation framework, with Rabtriever as the student to reconstruct the teacher's contextual-aware query embedding. To achieve this effect, Rabtriever is first initialized from the teacher, with parameters frozen. The Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) paradigm is then adopted, which integrates a lightweight, trainable predictor between LLM layers and heads, projecting the query embedding into a new hidden space, with the document embedding as the latent vector. JEPA then minimizes the distribution difference between this projected embedding and the teacher embedding. To strengthen the sampling efficiency of on-policy distillation, we also add an auxiliary loss on the reverse KL of LLM logits, to reshape the student's logit distribution. Rabtriever optimizes the teacher's quadratic complexity on the document length to linear, verified both theoretically and empirically. Experiments show that Rabtriever outperforms different retriever baselines across diverse rationale-based tasks, including empathetic conversations and robotic manipulations, with minor accuracy degradation from the reranker. Rabtriever also generalizes well on traditional retrieval benchmarks such as MS MARCO and BEIR, with comparable performance to the best retriever baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于理由的高效检索：基于JEPA的生成重排序器的在线策略蒸馏
与传统的基于事实的检索不同，基于理由的检索通常需要使用大语言模型对查询-文档对进行交叉编码，带来高昂的计算成本。为解决此问题，我们提出Rabtriever，该方法独立编码查询和文档，同时提供与重排序器相当的跨查询-文档理解能力。我们首先训练一个基于LLM的生成式重排序器，将文档置于查询之前，并提示LLM通过对数概率生成相关性得分。随后，将其作为在线策略蒸馏框架中的教师模型，Rabtriever作为学生模型，重建教师的上下文感知查询嵌入。为实现此效果，Rabtriever首先从教师模型初始化，参数冻结。随后采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）范式，在LLM层与头之间集成一个轻量可训练的预测器，将查询嵌入投影到新的隐藏空间，文档嵌入作为潜在向量。JEPA随后最小化该投影嵌入与教师嵌入之间的分布差异。为增强在线策略蒸馏的采样效率，我们还添加了LLM对数概率的反向KL损失，以重塑学生模型的对数概率分布。Rabtriever将教师模型在文档长度上的二次复杂度优化为线性复杂度，理论与实验证明均成立。实验表明，Rabtriever在多种基于理由的任务（包括共情对话与机器人操作）中优于不同检索器基线，且准确率仅略有下降。Rabtriever在传统检索基准（如MS MARCO和BEIR）上也表现良好，性能与最佳检索器基线相当。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of rationale-based retrieval relying on cross-encoding via large language models, the paper introduces Rabtriever, an on-policy distillation framework that distills a generative reranker into a lightweight retriever. The method initializes Rabtriever from the teacher reranker, then employs the JEPA paradigm to project query embeddings into a latent space using a trainable predictor, minimizing distribution divergence with the teacher’s embeddings. An auxiliary reverse KL loss reshapes the student’s logit distribution to improve sampling efficiency. Experimentally, Rabtriever reduces document-length complexity from quadratic to linear, achieving competitive or superior performance over baselines across rationale-based tasks like empathetic conversations and robotic manipulation, while also generalizing well on traditional retrieval benchmarks such as MS MARCO and BEIR with minimal accuracy loss.
为解决基于理由的检索因依赖大语言模型交叉编码带来的高计算开销问题，本文提出Rabtriever，一种基于生成式重排序器的在线蒸馏框架。该方法先从重排序器参数初始化学生模型，再采用JEPA范式，在LLM层与头之间引入轻量可训练预测器，将查询嵌入投影至由文档嵌入引导的潜在空间，并最小化与教师嵌入的分布差异。同时引入反向KL损失以优化学生模型的对数概率分布，提升采样效率。理论与实验均验证其将文档长度复杂度从二次降低至线性。实验表明，Rabtriever在情感对话与机器人操作等基于理由的任务上超越多种检索基线，精度损失极小，且在MS MARCO和BEIR等传统检索基准上表现与最优检索器相当。
EvoGround: Self-Evolving Video Agents for Video Temporal Grounding
Authors: Minjoon Jung, Byoung-Tak Zhang, Lorenzo Torresani
First: 2026-05-13T17:25:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:25:51+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://minjoong507.github.io/projects/EvoGround/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video temporal grounding (VTG) takes an untrimmed video and a natural-language query as input and localizes the temporal moment that best matches the query. Existing methods rely on large, task-specific datasets requiring costly manual annotation. We introduce EvoGround, a framework of two coupled self-evolving agents, a proposer and a solver, that learn temporal grounding from raw videos without any human-labeled data. The proposer generates query--moment pairs from raw videos, while the solver learns to ground them and feeds back signals that improve the proposer in return. Through this self-reinforcing reinforcement-learning loop, the two agents are initialized from the same backbone and mutually improve across iterations. Trained on 2.5K unlabeled videos, EvoGround matches or surpasses fully supervised models across multiple VTG benchmarks, while emerging as a state-of-the-art fine-grained video captioner without manual labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvoGround：用于视频时间定位的自进化视频代理
视频时间定位（VTG）以未剪辑视频和自然语言查询为输入，定位最匹配查询的时间点。现有方法依赖大型、特定任务的数据集，需昂贵的人工标注。我们提出EvoGround，一种由两个相互耦合的自进化代理（提案者与求解者）组成的框架，无需人工标注数据即可从原始视频中学习时间定位。提案者从原始视频生成查询-时刻对，求解者学习定位并反馈信号以提升提案者。通过这种自强化强化学习循环，两个代理从相同骨干网络初始化，并在迭代中相互提升。在2.5K未标注视频上训练后，EvoGround在多个VTG基准测试中达到或超越全监督模型，同时成为无需人工标注的最先进细粒度视频字幕生成器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of manual annotation in video temporal grounding, this work introduces EvoGround, a self-evolving framework with two coupled agents—a proposer that generates query-moment pairs from raw videos and a solver that grounds them and improves the proposer via feedback. Trained on 2.5K unlabeled videos without supervision, the agents iteratively refine each other through reinforcement learning, starting from the same backbone. Experimental results show EvoGround matches or outperforms fully supervised models on multiple VTG benchmarks and achieves state-of-the-art performance in fine-grained video captioning without any human-labeled data.
为降低视频时序定位任务中人工标注的成本，EvoGround提出一种自演进框架，包含两个耦合的智能体：提案者从原始视频中生成查询-时刻对，求解者负责定位并反馈信号以优化提案者。两个智能体从同一骨干网络初始化，通过迭代强化学习相互提升，仅在2.5K无标注视频上训练。实验表明，EvoGround在多个VTG基准上达到或超越全监督模型性能，并在无需人工标注的情况下成为最先进细粒度视频描述器。
Exact Verification of Graph Neural Networks with Incremental Constraint Solving
Authors: Minghao Liu, Chia-Hsuan Lu, Marta Kwiatkowska
First: 2025-08-12T20:10:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:25:37+00:00
Comments: Extended version of the paper accepted at FM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph neural networks (GNNs) are increasingly often employed in high-stakes applications, such as fraud detection or healthcare, but are susceptible to adversarial attacks. A number of techniques have been proposed to provide adversarial robustness guarantees, but support for commonly used aggregation functions in message-passing GNNs is lacking. In this paper, we develop an exact (sound and complete) verification method for GNNs to compute guarantees against attribute and structural perturbations that involve edge addition or deletion, subject to budget constraints. Our method employs constraint solving with bound tightening, and iteratively solves a sequence of relaxed constraint satisfaction problems while relying on incremental solving capabilities of solvers to improve efficiency. We implement GNNev, a versatile exact verifier for message-passing neural networks, which supports three aggregation functions -- sum, max and mean -- with the latter two considered here for the first time. Extensive experimental evaluation of GNNev on real-world fraud datasets (Amazon and Yelp) and biochemical datasets (MUTAG and ENZYMES) demonstrates its usability and effectiveness, as well as superior performance on node classification and competitiveness on graph classification compared to existing exact verification tools on sum-aggregated GNNs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于增量约束求解的图神经网络精确验证
图神经网络（GNN）在欺诈检测、医疗等高风险应用中日益普及，但易受对抗攻击。已有多种技术旨在提供对抗鲁棒性保证，但对消息传递GNN中常用聚合函数的支持不足。本文提出一种针对GNN的精确（完备且可靠）验证方法，用于计算在预算约束下对属性和结构扰动（包括边的添加或删除）的保证。该方法结合约束求解与界限紧缩，通过迭代求解一系列松弛的约束满足问题，并利用求解器的增量求解能力提升效率。我们实现了GNNev，一种适用于消息传递神经网络的通用精确验证器，支持三种聚合函数——求和、最大值和平均值，其中后两者为首次支持。在真实世界欺诈数据集（Amazon和Yelp）和生化数据集（MUTAG和ENZYMES）上的广泛实验表明，GNNev在节点分类任务中表现优异，在图分类任务中与现有精确验证工具相比具有竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for reliable GNNs in high-stakes domains like fraud detection and healthcare, this paper presents an exact verification method that guarantees robustness against adversarial attribute and structural perturbations under budget constraints. The method uses incremental constraint solving with bound tightening, iteratively refining relaxed constraint satisfaction problems to improve efficiency. Implemented as GNNev, it supports sum, max, and mean aggregation functions — the latter two being novel in exact verification — and experiments on real-world fraud and biochemical datasets show it is effective for node classification and competitive for graph classification, outperforming existing tools on sum-aggregated GNNs.
为提升图神经网络在欺诈检测、医疗等高风险场景中的可靠性，本文提出一种精确验证方法，可保证其在预算约束下对属性与结构扰动（如边的增删）的鲁棒性。该方法结合边界紧缩与增量约束求解，通过迭代求解松弛约束满足问题提升效率。实现工具GNNev支持求和、最大值与平均值三种聚合函数（后两者首次在精确验证中支持），在真实欺诈与生化数据集上的实验表明，其在节点分类任务中表现优越，在图分类任务中与现有工具相比具有竞争力。
Improving Reproducibility in Evaluation through Multi-Level Annotator Modeling
Authors: Deepak Pandita, Flip Korn, Chris Welty, Christopher M. Homan
First: 2026-05-13T17:22:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:22:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As generative AI models such as large language models (LLMs) become more pervasive, ensuring the safety, robustness, and overall trustworthiness of these systems is paramount. However, AI is currently facing a reproducibility crisis driven by unreliable evaluations and unrepeatable experimental results. While human raters are often used to assess models for utility and safety, they introduce divergent biases and subjective opinions into their annotations. Overcoming this variance is exceptionally challenging because very little data exists to study how experimental repeatability actually improves as the annotator pool grows. Standard evaluation practices typically rely on a small number of annotations per item (often 3 to 5) and lack the persistent rater identifiers necessary to model individual variance across items. In this work, we introduce a multi-level bootstrapping approach to realistically model annotator behavior. Leveraging datasets with a large number of ratings and persistent rater identifiers, we analyze the tradeoffs between the number of items ($N$) and the number of responses per item ($K$) required to achieve statistical significance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多层次标注者建模提升评估的可重复性
随着大型语言模型等生成式AI模型日益普及，确保这些系统的安全性、鲁棒性和整体可信度至关重要。然而，当前AI正面临由不可靠评估和不可重复实验结果引发的可重复性危机。虽然常使用人类标注者评估模型的效用与安全性，但其标注中会引入差异化的偏见和主观意见。克服这种变异性极为困难，因为缺乏数据来研究随着标注者池扩大，实验可重复性如何真正提升。标准评估实践通常每项仅依赖少量标注（常为3至5个），且缺乏持续标注者标识符，无法建模个体在不同项目间的变异。本文提出一种多层次自助法方法，以真实模拟标注者行为。利用包含大量评分和持续标注者标识符的数据集，我们分析了实现统计显著性所需项目数（N）与每项响应数（K）之间的权衡。
Summary / 总结
As generative AI models grow more widespread, ensuring their safety and reliability demands trustworthy evaluations, yet current practices suffer from reproducibility issues due to subjective human annotations. To address this, the paper proposes a multi-level bootstrapping method that models annotator behavior using persistent rater identifiers and large-scale rating data, enabling analysis of how experimental repeatability scales with item count (N) and annotations per item (K). Experimental results show that increasing K improves statistical power but at diminishing returns, while increasing N alone is insufficient without sufficient K to capture individual annotator variance.
随着大语言模型日益普及，确保其安全性和可靠性需要可信的评估方法，但当前做法因人类标注者主观性而面临可重复性问题。本文提出一种多层自助法，用于建模标注者行为，利用具有持久标注者ID和大量评分的数据集。研究分析了增加项目数（N）或每项评分数（K）对统计显著性的影响，发现提高K值可在较少项目数下获得更稳定的结果，从而为实现更可重复的评估提供了实用路径。
Unify Robot Actions in Camera Frame
Authors: Sicheng Xie, Lingchen Meng, Zijie Diao, Haidong Cao, Zhiying Du, Shuyuan Tu, Jiaqi Leng, Qiuyue Wang, Mingsheng Li, Shuai Bai, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang
First: 2025-11-21T07:10:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:20:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-embodiment robot learning requires a unified action representation with consistent semantics across robot platforms. Existing representations suffer from platform-specific inconsistencies, while current solutions either maintain embodiment-specific action heads or learn latent action spaces, without fundamentally resolving the mismatch. We propose to unify robot actions in the camera frame using camera extrinsics, so that actions share consistent geometric semantics across different robot embodiments, including both single-arm and bimanual robots. However, most existing datasets lack camera extrinsic annotations, and existing offline calibration methods either suffer from local minima or require robot-specific training data. To address this gap, we present CalibAll, a training-free, robot-independent annotation pipeline that estimates camera extrinsics for offline datasets and converts heterogeneous robot actions into standardized camera-frame actions. CalibAll follows a coarse-to-fine calibration strategy: temporal PnP provides a stable initialization, followed by differentiable rendering-based refinement for high precision. Beyond extrinsics, CalibAll produces standardized TCP-pose actions and auxiliary annotations. We apply CalibAll to 16 datasets across 4 robot platforms, producing approximately 97K calibrated data episodes. Downstream simulation and real-robot experiments show that cross-embodiment pretraining with camera-frame actions achieves state-of-the-art performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在相机坐标系中统一机器人动作
跨具身机器人学习需要一种在不同机器人平台上语义一致的动作表示。现有表示方法存在平台特定的不一致性，而当前解决方案要么保留具身特定的动作头，要么学习潜在动作空间，均未能从根本上解决该不匹配问题。我们提出利用相机外参在相机坐标系中统一机器人动作，使不同机器人具身（包括单臂和双臂机器人）的动作共享一致的几何语义。然而，大多数现有数据集缺乏相机外参标注，且现有离线标定方法要么易陷入局部极小值，要么需要机器人特定的训练数据。为解决这一问题，我们提出CalibAll，一种无需训练、与机器人无关的标注流程，可为离线数据集估计相机外参，并将异构机器人动作转换为标准化的相机坐标系动作。CalibAll采用粗到精的标定策略：时间PnP提供稳定初始化，随后通过可微渲染进行高精度优化。除外参外，CalibAll还生成标准化的TCP位姿动作及辅助标注。我们将在4种机器人平台上对16个数据集应用CalibAll，生成约9.7万个校准数据片段。下游仿真与真实机器人实验表明，使用相机坐标系动作进行跨具身预训练可达到最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
To enable cross-embodiment robot learning, this work addresses the lack of consistent action representations across different robot platforms by unifying robot actions in the camera frame using camera extrinsics, ensuring geometric semantic alignment. It introduces CalibAll, a training-free, robot-independent pipeline that estimates camera extrinsics for offline datasets and converts heterogeneous actions into standardized camera-frame actions via a coarse-to-fine calibration strategy combining temporal PnP for initialization and differentiable rendering for refinement. Applied to 16 datasets across 4 robot platforms, CalibAll generated 97K calibrated episodes, and downstream experiments demonstrated that cross-embodiment pretraining using these camera-frame actions achieved state-of-the-art performance.
为实现跨机器人平台学习时动作语义的一致性，该研究提出通过相机外参将机器人动作统一到相机坐标系下，使单臂与双臂机器人共享几何语义一致的动作。针对现有数据集缺乏外参标注及传统标定方法易陷局部最优或需平台特定训练数据的问题，作者提出CalibAll，一种无需训练、平台无关的离线标注流程，通过粗到精策略——先用时序PnP初始化，再用可微渲染优化精度——估计相机外参，并生成标准化TCP位姿动作及辅助标注。该方法应用于4种平台的16个数据集，产出约9.7万个校准数据片段，下游仿真与真实机器人实验表明，基于相机坐标系动作的跨平台预训练达到当前最优性能。
VoxCor: Training-Free Volumetric Features for Multimodal Voxel Correspondence
Authors: Guney Tombak, Ertunc Erdil, Ender Konukoglu
First: 2026-05-13T17:20:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:20:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Cross-modal 3D medical image analysis requires voxelwise representations that remain anatomically consistent across imaging contrasts, scanners, and acquisition protocols. Recent work has shown that frozen 2D Vision Transformer (ViT) foundation models can support such representations, but typical pipelines extract features along a single anatomical axis and adapt those features inside a registration solver for one image pair at a time, leaving complementary viewing directions unused and producing representations that do not transfer to new volumes. We introduce VoxCor, a training-free fit--transform method for reusable volumetric feature representations from frozen 2D ViT foundation models. During an offline fitting phase, VoxCor combines triplanar ViT inference with a compact closed-form weighted partial least squares (WPLS) projection that uses fitting-time voxel correspondences to select modality-stable anatomical directions in the triplanar feature space. At transform time, new volumes are mapped by triplanar ViT inference and linear projection alone, without fine-tuning or registration. Voxel correspondences can then be queried directly by nearest-neighbor search. We evaluate VoxCor on intra-subject Abdomen MR--CT and inter-subject HCP T2w--T1w tasks using deformable registration, voxelwise k-nearest-neighbor segmentation, and segmentation-center landmark localization. VoxCor improves the hardest cross-subject, cross-modality transfer settings, reduces encoder sensitivity for dense correspondence transfer, and yields registration performance competitive with handcrafted descriptors and learned 3D features. This positions VoxCor as a reusable feature layer for downstream multimodal analysis beyond pairwise registration. Code, configuration files, and implementation details are publicly available on GitHub at \href{https://github.com/guneytombak/VoxCor}{guneytombak/VoxCor}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VoxCor：基于冻结2D视觉Transformer的无训练体素特征用于多模态体素对应
跨模态3D医学图像分析需要在不同成像对比、扫描仪和采集协议下保持解剖学一致性的体素级表示。近期研究表明，冻结的2D视觉Transformer（ViT）基础模型可支持此类表示，但典型流程沿单一解剖轴提取特征，并在配准求解器内逐对图像适配这些特征，导致互补视角未被利用，且生成的表示无法迁移到新体素。我们提出VoxCor，一种基于冻结2D ViT基础模型的无训练拟合-变换方法，用于可复用的体素级特征表示。在离线拟合阶段，VoxCor结合三平面ViT推理与紧凑闭式加权偏最小二乘（WPLS）投影，利用拟合时体素对应关系在三平面特征空间中选择模态稳定的解剖方向。变换时，新体素仅通过三平面ViT推理与线性投影映射，无需微调或配准。随后可通过最近邻搜索直接查询体素对应关系。我们在使用可变形配准、体素级k近邻分割和分割中心地标定位的单体与跨体实验中评估VoxCor，结果表明其在最难的跨体跨模态迁移场景中表现更优，降低编码器对密集对应迁移的敏感性，并实现与手工特征及学习3D特征相当的配准性能。这使VoxCor成为超越成对配准的下游多模态分析可复用特征层。代码、配置文件及实现细节已公开于GitHub：\href{https://github.com/guneytombak/VoxCor}{guneytombak/VoxCor}。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for anatomically consistent voxel representations across different imaging modalities and scanners, VoxCor introduces a training-free method to extract reusable volumetric features from frozen 2D Vision Transformer models. It combines triplanar ViT feature extraction with a closed-form weighted partial least squares projection during an offline fitting phase to identify stable anatomical directions, then maps new volumes via triplanar inference and linear projection without fine-tuning. Experiments on intra- and inter-subject medical image tasks show VoxCor outperforms baseline methods in cross-subject, cross-modality settings, reduces encoder sensitivity, and achieves registration performance comparable to handcrafted and learned 3D features, establishing it as a general-purpose feature layer for multimodal downstream tasks.
为解决跨模态、跨设备3D医学图像分析中对解剖一致性体素表示的需求，VoxCor提出一种无需训练的方法，利用冻结的2D视觉Transformer提取可复用的体素特征。该方法在离线拟合阶段结合三平面ViT特征提取与加权偏最小二乘投影，通过体素对应关系选择稳定的解剖方向；推理时仅需三平面ViT特征与线性投影，无需微调或配准。实验表明，VoxCor在跨受试者、跨模态任务中表现更优，降低编码器敏感性，注册性能可媲美手工特征与学习型3D特征，适合作为下游多模态3D分析的通用特征层。
A Call to Lagrangian Action: Learning Population Mechanics from Temporal Snapshots
Authors: Vincent Guan, Lazar Atanackovic, Kirill Neklyudov
Venue: ICML 2026 spotlight
First: 2026-05-08T23:21:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:19:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026 (spotlight)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The population dynamics of molecules, cells, and organisms are governed by a number of unknown forces. In the last decade, population dynamics have predominantly been modeled with Wasserstein gradient flows. However, since gradient flows minimize free energy, they fail to capture important dynamical properties, such as periodicity. In this work, we propose a change in perspective by considering dynamics that minimize a population-level action under a damped Wasserstein Lagrangian. By deriving the corresponding Hamiltonian equations of motion, we formalize Wasserstein Lagrangian Mechanics, a structured class of second-order dynamics that encompasses classical mechanics, quantum mechanics, and gradient flows. We then propose WLM as the first algorithm that learns these second-order dynamics from observed marginals, without specifying the Lagrangian. By directly learning the population mechanics, WLM can both forecast and interpolate unseen marginals, and outperforms existing gradient flow and flow matching methods across a wide range of dynamics, including vortex dynamics, embryonic development, and flocking.
中文标题/摘要
标题：呼唤拉格朗日作用：从时间快照中学习群体力学
分子、细胞和有机体的群体动力学受多种未知力支配。过去十年中，群体动力学主要采用Wasserstein梯度流建模，但由于梯度流最小化自由能，无法捕捉周期性等重要动力学特性。本文提出从新视角出发，考虑在阻尼Wasserstein拉格朗日下最小化群体作用的动态。通过推导对应的哈密顿运动方程，我们形式化了Wasserstein拉格朗日力学——一个涵盖经典力学、量子力学和梯度流的结构化二阶动力学类。随后，我们提出WLM算法，它是首个无需指定拉格朗日即可从观测到的边际数据中学习这些二阶动力学的方法。通过直接学习群体力学，WLM可预测和插值未观测边际，并在包括涡旋动力学、胚胎发育和群聚等广泛动力学场景中，超越现有梯度流和流匹配方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Wasserstein gradient flows in capturing non-equilibrium dynamics like periodicity, this work introduces Wasserstein Lagrangian Mechanics as a framework for modeling population dynamics by minimizing a population-level action under a damped Wasserstein Lagrangian. The method derives Hamiltonian equations of motion that generalize classical, quantum, and gradient flow dynamics, and proposes WLM — the first algorithm to learn these second-order dynamics directly from observed marginals without prior Lagrangian specification. Experiments show WLM outperforms gradient flow and flow matching methods across diverse systems including vortex dynamics, embryonic development, and flocking, achieving superior forecasting and interpolation of unseen marginals.
鉴于Wasserstein梯度流在捕捉周期性等非平衡动力学方面存在局限，本文提出通过最小化带阻尼Wasserstein拉格朗日量的群体水平作用量来建模群体动力学，即Wasserstein拉格朗日力学。该方法推导出对应的哈密顿运动方程，将经典力学、量子力学和梯度流统一纳入第二阶动力学框架。所提出的WLM算法无需指定拉格朗日量，直接从观测到的边际分布中学习这些动力学，从而实现对未见边际的预测与插值。实验表明，WLM在涡旋动力学、胚胎发育和群聚等多类系统中均优于梯度流与流匹配方法。
Limitations of Quantum Advantage in Unsupervised Machine Learning
Authors: Apoorva D. Patel
Venue: Proceedings of IEEE qCCL2025, June 2025, pp. 39-42
First: 2025-11-13T08:50:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:19:25+00:00
Comments: 4 pages,1 figure. Invited talk at the 2025 IEEE International Conference on Quantum Control, Computing and Learning (IEEE qCCL2025), Hong Kong, June 2025. Published in the proceedings, pp. 39-42 (v2) Published version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning models are used for pattern recognition analysis of big data, without direct human intervention. The task of unsupervised learning is to find the probability distribution that would best describe the available data, and then use it to make predictions for observables of interest. Classical models generally fit the data to Boltzmann distribution of Hamiltonians with a large number of tunable parameters. Quantum extensions of these models replace classical probability distributions with quantum density matrices. An advantage can be obtained only when features of density matrices that are absent in classical probability distributions are exploited. Such situations depend on the input data as well as the targeted observables. Explicit examples are discussed that bring out the constraints limiting possible quantum advantage. The problem-dependent extent of quantum advantage has implications for both data analysis and sensing applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无监督机器学习中量子优势的局限性
机器学习模型用于对大数据进行模式识别分析，无需直接人工干预。无监督学习的任务是找到最能描述可用数据的概率分布，然后利用该分布对感兴趣的可观测量进行预测。经典模型通常将数据拟合到具有大量可调参数的哈密顿量的玻尔兹曼分布。量子模型则用量子密度矩阵替代经典概率分布。只有当利用到经典概率分布中不存在的密度矩阵特性时，才能获得优势。此类情况取决于输入数据和目标可观测量。文中讨论了具体实例，揭示了限制量子优势的约束条件。量子优势的依赖于问题的特性，对数据分析和传感应用均有影响。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the conditions under which quantum advantage can be realized in unsupervised machine learning, motivated by the need to understand when quantum models outperform classical ones in learning data distributions. The authors compare classical Boltzmann-based models with quantum counterparts that use density matrices, emphasizing that quantum advantage arises only when quantum features—such as entanglement or non-classical correlations—are leveraged for the specific task. Through explicit examples, they demonstrate that quantum advantage is not universal but highly dependent on both the structure of input data and the targeted observables, thereby constraining its applicability in real-world data analysis and sensing scenarios.
本文探讨了量子优势在无监督机器学习中实现的条件，动机在于厘清量子模型何时能超越经典模型。作者对比了基于玻尔兹曼分布的经典模型与使用密度矩阵的量子模型，指出量子优势仅在利用量子特性（如纠缠或非经典关联）时才能实现。通过具体实例，他们表明量子优势并非普适，而是高度依赖于输入数据结构和目标可观测量，从而限制了其在实际数据分析与传感场景中的适用性。
BlitzGS: City-Scale Gaussian Splatting at Lightning Speed
Authors: Zhongtao Wang, Huishan Au, Yilong Li, Mai Su, Haojie Jin, Yisong Chen, Meng Gai, Fei Zhu, Guoping Wang
First: 2026-05-13T17:13:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:13:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present BlitzGS, a distributed 3DGS framework that reduces active Gaussian workload for fast city-scale reconstruction. BlitzGS manages this workload at three coupled levels. At the system level, the framework shards Gaussians across GPUs by index parity rather than spatial blocks. This approach mitigates the cross-block visibility redundancy inherent in spatial partitioning. Furthermore, it distributes each rendering step through a single cross-GPU exchange that routes projected Gaussians to their tile owners. At the model level, scheduled importance-scoring passes shrink the global Gaussian population. During these passes, the framework generates a per-Gaussian visibility weight to bias density-control updates toward contributing primitives and a per-view importance mask for the view-level renderer. At the view level, BlitzGS trims each camera's active set with a distance-based LOD gate to exclude excessively fine primitives for the current frustum and the importance-based culling mask to skip Gaussians with negligible cross-view contribution. On large-scale benchmarks, BlitzGS matches the rendering quality of recent large-scale baselines while delivering an order-of-magnitude speedup, training city-scale scenes in tens of minutes. Our code is available at https: //github.com/AkierRaee/BlitzGS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BlitzGS：闪电般速度的城市级高斯点渲染
我们提出了BlitzGS，一个分布式3DGS框架，通过减少活跃高斯点的工作负载，实现快速城市级重建。BlitzGS在三个相互关联的层级管理该工作负载。在系统层，框架按索引奇偶性而非空间块将高斯点分布到不同GPU上，从而缓解空间划分固有的跨块可见性冗余。此外，它通过单次跨GPU交换分发每个渲染步骤，将投影后的高斯点路由至所属瓦片的GPU。在模型层，通过调度重要性评分步骤缩减全局高斯点数量。在这些步骤中，框架为每个高斯点生成可见性权重，以偏向贡献性更强的点进行密度控制更新，并为视图级渲染器生成每视角重要性掩码。在视图层，BlitzGS通过基于距离的LOD门控修剪每个相机的活跃集合，排除当前视锥体中过于精细的点，并通过重要性剔除掩码跳过对跨视角贡献可忽略的高斯点。在大规模基准测试中，BlitzGS在保持与近期大规模基线相当的渲染质量的同时，实现数量级加速，可在数十分钟内训练城市级场景。我们的代码可在https://github.com/AkierRaee/BlitzGS获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for faster city-scale 3D reconstruction using Gaussian Splatting, BlitzGS introduces a distributed framework that reduces active Gaussian workload through three coordinated levels: system-level sharding by index parity to avoid spatial redundancy, model-level importance-scoring to prune low-contributing Gaussians, and view-level culling via distance-based LOD and cross-view importance masks. Experimental results show BlitzGS achieves rendering quality comparable to recent baselines while delivering a tenfold speedup, enabling city-scale scene training in tens of minutes.
为实现城市级3D重建的快速处理，BlitzGS提出一种分布式框架，通过系统级按索引奇偶性分片、模型级重要性评分筛选低贡献高斯点、视图级基于距离的LOD与跨视图重要性掩码剔除冗余点，有效降低活跃高斯点负载。实验表明，BlitzGS在保持与近期基线相当渲染质量的同时，速度提升一个数量级，可在数十分钟内完成城市级场景训练。
An LLM-Based System for Argument Reconstruction
Authors: Paulo Pirozelli, Victor Hugo Nascimento Rocha, Fabio G. Cozman, Douglas Aldred
First: 2026-05-13T17:13:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:13:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Arguments are a fundamental aspect of human reasoning, in which claims are supported, challenged, and weighed against one another. We present an end-to-end large language model (LLM)-based system for reconstructing arguments from natural language text into abstract argument graphs. The system follows a multi-stage pipeline that progressively identifies argumentative components, selects relevant elements, and uncovers their logical relations. These elements are represented as directed acyclic graphs consisting of two component types (premises and conclusions) and three relation types (support, attack, and undercut). We conduct two complementary experiments to evaluate the system. First, we perform a manual evaluation on arguments drawn from an argumentation theory textbook to assess the system's ability to recover argumentative structure. Second, we conduct a quantitative evaluation on benchmark datasets, allowing comparison with prior work by mapping our outputs to established annotation schemes. Results show that the system can adequately recover argumentative structures and, when adapted to different annotation schemes, achieve reasonable performance across benchmark datasets. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-based pipelines for scalable argument reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型的论证重构系统
论证是人类推理的基本组成部分，涉及主张的支持、挑战与相互权衡。我们提出了一种端到端的大语言模型（LLM）系统，用于将自然语言文本中的论证重构为抽象论证图。该系统采用多阶段管道，逐步识别论证成分、选择相关元素并揭示其逻辑关系。这些元素表示为包含两种组件类型（前提与结论）和三种关系类型（支持、攻击、削弱）的有向无环图。我们通过两项互补实验评估该系统：首先，对来自论证理论教科书的论证进行人工评估，以检验系统恢复论证结构的能力；其次，在基准数据集上进行定量评估，通过将输出映射到既定标注方案，与先前工作进行比较。结果表明，该系统能够充分恢复论证结构，并在适配不同标注方案后，在基准数据集上实现合理性能。这些发现凸显了基于LLM的管道在可扩展论证重构方面的潜力。

Motivated by the need to systematically extract and represent argumentative structures from natural language, the authors developed an end-to-end LLM-based system that reconstructs arguments into abstract graphs composed of premises, conclusions, and logical relations. The method employs a multi-stage pipeline to identify argument components, select relevant elements, and infer their relationships as directed acyclic graphs. Experimental results from manual evaluation on textbook arguments and quantitative benchmarks show the system effectively recovers argumentative structures and achieves competitive performance across different annotation schemes when adapted, demonstrating the scalability and utility of LLM pipelines for argument reconstruction.
为实现自然语言中论证结构的自动化提取，本研究提出一种基于大语言模型的端到端系统，将论证重构为抽象图结构。该方法采用多阶段流程识别论证成分、筛选相关元素并建立逻辑关系，图结构包含前提、结论及支持、攻击、削弱三种关系。实验结果表明，系统在教材论证的主观评估和基准数据集的定量评估中均能有效还原论证结构，且在适配不同标注方案后仍保持合理性能，凸显了大语言模型流程在可扩展论证重构中的潜力。
OmniSIFT: Modality-Asymmetric Token Compression for Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Models
Authors: Yue Ding, Yiyan Ji, Jungang Li, Xuyang Liu, Xinlong Chen, Junfei Wu, Bozhou Li, Bohan Zeng, Yang Shi, Yushuo Guan, Yuanxing Zhang, Jiaheng Liu, Qiang Liu, Pengfei Wan, Liang Wang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-04T17:51:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:11:48+00:00
Comments: [ICML 2026] Code Link: https://github.com/dingyue772/OmniSIFT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Omni-modal Large Language Models (Omni-LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in audio-video understanding tasks. However, their reliance on long multimodal token sequences leads to substantial computational overhead. Despite this challenge, token compression methods designed for Omni-LLMs remain limited. To bridge this gap, we propose OmniSIFT (Omni-modal Spatio-temporal Informed Fine-grained Token compression), a modality-asymmetric token compression framework tailored for Omni-LLMs. Specifically, OmniSIFT adopts a two-stage compression strategy: (i) a spatio-temporal video pruning module that removes video redundancy arising from both intra-frame structure and inter-frame overlap, and (ii) a vision-guided audio selection module that filters audio tokens. The entire framework is optimized end-to-end via a differentiable straight-through estimator. Extensive experiments on five representative benchmarks demonstrate the efficacy and robustness of OmniSIFT. Notably, for Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniSIFT introduces only 4.85M parameters while maintaining lower latency than training-free baselines such as OmniZip. With merely 25% of the original token context, OmniSIFT consistently outperforms all compression baselines and even surpasses the performance of the full-token model on several tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniSIFT：面向多模态大语言模型的模态非对称令牌压缩
多模态大语言模型（Omni-LLMs）在音频-视频理解任务中表现出强大能力，但其依赖长多模态令牌序列导致计算开销巨大。尽管如此，专为Omni-LLMs设计的令牌压缩方法仍显不足。为弥合此差距，我们提出OmniSIFT（多模态时空感知细粒度令牌压缩），一种针对Omni-LLMs的模态非对称令牌压缩框架。具体而言，OmniSIFT采用两阶段压缩策略：(i) 时空视频剪枝模块，去除因帧内结构与帧间重叠产生的视频冗余；(ii) 视觉引导音频选择模块，筛选音频令牌。整个框架通过可微分直通估计器端到端优化。在五个代表性基准上的大量实验验证了OmniSIFT的有效性与鲁棒性。值得注意的是，对于Qwen2.5-Omni-7B，OmniSIFT仅引入485万参数，同时延迟低于无训练基线（如OmniZip）。仅使用原令牌上下文的25%，OmniSIFT持续超越所有压缩基线，并在多个任务上超越完整令牌模型的表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of long multimodal token sequences in Omni-LLMs, the authors propose OmniSIFT, a modality-asymmetric token compression framework. It employs a two-stage approach: a spatio-temporal video pruning module to eliminate intra- and inter-frame redundancies, and a vision-guided audio selection module to filter audio tokens. Optimized end-to-end via a differentiable straight-through estimator, OmniSIFT achieves significant efficiency gains. Experiments on five benchmarks show that for Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, it uses only 4.85M parameters and 25% of original context while maintaining lower latency than training-free baselines and even outperforming full-token models on several tasks.
为降低Omni-LLM处理长多模态序列的计算开销，作者提出OmniSIFT框架，采用模态非对称的分阶段压缩策略：第一阶段通过时空视频剪枝模块去除帧内与帧间冗余视频token，第二阶段通过视觉引导的音频选择模块过滤音频token。框架通过可微分直通估计器端到端优化，实现在压缩的同时保持性能。实验表明，针对Qwen2.5-Omni-7B模型，OmniSIFT仅需485万参数，运行延迟低于无训练基线（如OmniZip），且在仅保留原上下文25%的情况下，持续超越所有压缩基线，甚至在多个任务上超越完整token模型表现。
Di-BiLPS: Denoising induced Bidirectional Latent-PDE-Solver under Sparse Observations
Authors: Zhonghao Li, Chaoyu Liu, Qian Zhang
First: 2026-05-13T17:11:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:11:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Partial differential equations (PDEs) are fundamental for modeling complex natural and physical phenomena. In many real-world applications, however, observational data are extremely sparse, which severely limits the applicability of both classical numerical solvers and existing neural approaches. While neural methods have shown promising results under moderately sparse observations, their inference efficiency at high resolutions is limited, and their accuracy degrades substantially in the extremely sparse regime. In this work, we propose the Di-BiLPS, a unified neural framework that effectively handle both forward and inverse PDE problems under extremely sparse observations. Di-BiLPS combines a variational autoencoder to compress high-dimensional inputs into a compact latent space, a latent diffusion module to model uncertainty, and contrastive learning to align representations. Operating entirely in this latent space, the framework achieves efficient inference while retaining flexible input-output mapping. In addition, we introduce a PDE-informed denoising algorithm based on a variance-preserving diffusion process, which further improves inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on multiple PDE benchmarks demonstrate that Di-BiLPS consistently achieves SOTA performance under extremely sparse inputs (as low as 3%), while substantially reducing computational cost. Moreover, Di-BiLPS enables zero-shot super-resolution, as it allows predictions over continuous spatial-temporal domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Di-BiLPS：在稀疏观测下由去噪诱导的双向隐式偏微分方程求解器
偏微分方程（PDE）是建模复杂自然和物理现象的基础。然而在许多实际应用中，观测数据极为稀疏，这严重限制了经典数值求解器和现有神经方法的适用性。虽然神经方法在中等稀疏观测下表现良好，但在高分辨率下的推理效率有限，且在极度稀疏情况下精度显著下降。本文提出Di-BiLPS，一种统一的神经框架，可有效处理在极度稀疏观测下的正向与反向PDE问题。Di-BiLPS结合变分自编码器压缩高维输入至紧凑隐空间、隐式扩散模块建模不确定性及对比学习对齐表示。该框架完全在隐空间中运行，实现高效推理同时保持灵活的输入输出映射。此外，我们引入一种基于方差保持扩散过程的PDE引导去噪算法，进一步提升推理效率。在多个PDE基准上的大量实验表明，Di-BiLPS在极度稀疏输入（低至3%）下持续取得SOTA性能，同时大幅降低计算成本。此外，Di-BiLPS支持零样本超分辨率，允许在连续时空域上进行预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of solving partial differential equations (PDEs) under extremely sparse observational data, which limits classical methods and degrades neural approaches, the paper introduces Di-BiLPS: a neural framework combining variational autoencoder compression, latent diffusion for uncertainty modeling, and contrastive learning for representation alignment. Operating entirely in a latent space, Di-BiLPS enables efficient inference and flexible input-output mapping, while a PDE-informed denoising module further boosts efficiency. Experiments across multiple PDE benchmarks show Di-BiLPS achieves state-of-the-art accuracy even at 3% data sparsity, with significant computational savings and the ability to perform zero-shot super-resolution.
为应对观测数据极度稀疏条件下求解偏微分方程（PDE）的挑战，该文提出Di-BiLPS框架，融合变分自编码器压缩、潜空间扩散建模不确定性与对比学习对齐表征。该框架在潜空间内运行，实现高效推理并保持灵活的输入输出映射。其基于方差保持扩散过程的PDE引导去噪算法进一步提升效率。在多个PDE基准测试中，Di-BiLPS在仅3%观测数据下仍保持最先进精度，显著降低计算成本，并支持零样本超分辨率。
Strategic commitments shape collective cybersecurity under AI inequality
Authors: Adeela Bashir, Zia Ush Shamszaman, Zhao Song, Matjaz Perc, The Anh Han
First: 2026-05-10T08:34:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:09:54+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The growing integration of AI into cybersecurity is reshaping the balance between attackers and defenders. When access to advanced AI-enabled defence tools is uneven, resource-limited defenders may be unable to adopt effective protection, creating persistent system vulnerabilities. We study the impact of differential AI access using an evolutionary game-theoretic model in a finite population. We first show that when high-capability defence is costly, the population is driven toward low-cost, weak-defence behaviour, sustaining attacks and weakening long-run security. To address this problem, we introduce differential access to AI defence tools by allowing defenders to choose between low- and high-capability protection based on their resources. We then examine the role of a small group of committed defenders who always adopt strong defence and influence others through social learning. Although commitment increases the prevalence of strong defence, it alone cannot stabilise secure outcomes due to high defence costs. We therefore incorporate a targeted subsidy to remove the cost disadvantage from committed defenders. Our analysis shows that subsidised commitment significantly increases strong defence adoption, suppresses successful attacks, and improves overall system resilience. Simulations across a broad parameter space confirm that subsidies consistently outperform commitment alone. In addition, social-welfare analysis shows improved defender outcomes while keeping attacker gains low. These findings suggest that targeted support for key defenders can be an effective mechanism for stabilising cybersecurity in AI-driven environments and provide a theoretical bridge between cybersecurity policy, AI governance, and strategic allocation of defensive AI capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：战略承诺在人工智能不平等背景下塑造集体网络安全

The study then introduces committed defenders who always adopt strong defense and influence others via social learning, but finds that commitment alone fails to stabilize security due to high costs. To remedy this, it adds targeted subsidies to offset the cost disadvantage for committed defenders, which significantly boosts strong defense adoption, reduces successful attacks, and enhances system resilience. Simulations across varied parameters confirm subsidies outperform commitment alone, while social-welfare analysis shows improved defender outcomes without increasing attacker gains.
随着AI在网络安全中的应用加剧了攻击者与防御者之间的能力差距，资源有限的防御者往往无力负担高级防护工具，导致系统持续存在漏洞。研究采用演化博弈模型分析不同AI访问权限对集体安全的影响，发现高成本的强防御会导致群体普遍选择弱防御。为应对这一问题，模型引入始终采用强防御的“承诺防御者”，通过社会学习影响他人；但若无成本补贴，仅靠承诺无法稳定安全结果。当对承诺防御者提供定向补贴以抵消其成本劣势时，强防御采纳率显著上升，攻击成功率下降，整体系统韧性提升。多参数仿真验证补贴效果优于单纯承诺，且社会福利分析显示防御者收益改善而攻击者获益未显著增加。
ENSEMBITS: an alphabet of protein conformational ensembles
Authors: Kaiwen Shi, Carlos Oliver
First: 2026-05-13T17:08:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:08:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Protein structure tokenizers (PSTs) are workhorses in protein language modeling, function prediction, and evolutionary analysis. However, existing PSTs only capture local geometry of static structures, and miss the correlated motions and alternative conformational states revealed by protein ensembles. Here we introduce Ensembits, the first tokenizer of protein conformational ensembles. Ensembits address challenges inherent to tokenizing dynamics: deriving informative geometric descriptors across conformations, permutation-invariance encoding of variable-size ensembles, and conquering sparsity in dynamics data. Trained with a Residual VQ-VAE using a frame distillation objective on a large molecular dynamics corpus, Ensembits outperforms all related methods on RMSF prediction, and is the strongest standalone structural tokenizer on an token-conditioned ANOVA test on per-residue motion amplitude. Ensembits further matches or exceeds static tokenizers on EC, GO, binding site/affinity prediction, and zero-shot mutation-effect prediction despite using far less pretraining data. Notably, the distillation objective enables Ensembits to predict dynamics token from one single predicted structure, which alleviates dynamics data sparsity. As the field moves from static structure prediction toward ensemble generation, Ensembits offer the discrete vocabulary needed to bring dynamics into protein language modeling and design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ENSEMBITS：蛋白质构象集合的字母表
蛋白质结构分词器（PSTs）在蛋白质语言建模、功能预测和进化分析中发挥重要作用。然而，现有PSTs仅捕捉静态结构的局部几何特征，未能体现蛋白质集合所揭示的相关运动和替代构象状态。本文引入Ensembits，这是首个针对蛋白质构象集合的分词器。Ensembits解决了动态分词固有的挑战：跨构象提取信息几何描述符、对可变大小集合的排列不变编码，以及克服动态数据稀疏性。Ensembits采用残差VQ-VAE在大规模分子动力学语料库上训练，使用帧蒸馏目标函数，其在RMSF预测上超越所有相关方法，并在基于残基运动幅度的token条件ANOVA测试中成为最强的独立结构分词器。尽管预训练数据远少于静态分词器，Ensembits在EC、GO、结合位点/亲和力预测及零样本突变效应预测中表现匹配或优于静态分词器。值得注意的是，蒸馏目标使Ensembits能够仅从单个预测结构中预测动态token，缓解了动态数据稀疏性。随着领域从静态结构预测转向集合生成，Ensembits提供了将动态纳入蛋白质语言建模与设计所需的离散词汇表。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing protein structure tokenizers that ignore dynamic conformational states, Ensembits introduces the first tokenizer designed for protein ensembles. It addresses key challenges including geometric descriptor derivation across conformations, permutation-invariance for variable-size ensembles, and dynamics data sparsity using a Residual VQ-VAE trained with frame distillation on molecular dynamics data. Experimental results show Ensembits outperforms all prior methods on RMSF prediction and achieves top performance on motion amplitude prediction, while matching or exceeding static tokenizers in function prediction tasks despite using less pretraining data. Notably, its distillation objective enables dynamics prediction from a single structure, mitigating data sparsity.
为解决现有蛋白质结构分词器忽略动态构象状态的问题，Ensembits首次提出面向蛋白质构象集合的分词器。它攻克了跨构象几何描述、可变大小集合的排列不变编码及动态数据稀疏性等关键挑战。通过残差VQ-VAE结合帧蒸馏目标在分子动力学数据上训练，Ensembits在RMSF预测上表现最优，且在功能预测任务中匹配或超越静态分词器，尽管预训练数据更少。其蒸馏目标还支持从单个结构预测动态分词，缓解数据稀疏性，推动蛋白质语言建模向构象集合驱动的设计演进。
Force-Aware Neural Tangent Kernels for Scalable and Robust Active Learning of MLIPs
Authors: Eszter Varga-Umbrich, Zachary Weller-Davies, Paul Duckworth, Jules Tilly, Olivier Peltre, Shikha Surana
First: 2026-05-13T17:08:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:08:37+00:00
Comments: 10 main pages, total 34 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Active learning for machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) must address several challenges to be practical: scaling to large candidate pools, leveraging energy-force supervision, and maintaining robustness when candidate pools are biased relative to the target distribution. In this work, we jointly address these challenges. We first introduce a linearly scaling acquisition framework based on chunked feature-space posterior-variance shortlisting. By avoiding materialisation of the candidate and train set kernels, this approach enables screening of ~200k structures within hours and applies broadly to acquisition strategies that score candidates based on molecular similarity metrics. We then extend the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) to a force-aware setting via mixed parameter-coordinate derivatives, yielding a force NTK and a joint energy-force NTK that provide natural similarity metrics for vector-field prediction. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the joint energy-force NTK on the OC20 dataset, where force-aware acquisition is crucial: it achieves the lowest energy and force MAE and RMSE across all metrics and distribution splits. Across T1x, PMechDB, and RGD benchmarks, our force NTK methods remain competitive with established baselines while being significantly more efficient than committee-based approaches. Under a controlled candidate-pool shift case study on T1x, acquisition based on pretrained MLIP embeddings and NTKs remains robust, whereas committee-based methods exhibit higher variance. Overall, these results show that a single pretrained MLIP can enable scalable, force-aware, and distribution-robust active learning for foundation-model fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向可扩展且鲁棒的机器学习原子势主动学习的力感知神经切线核
机器学习原子势（MLIPs）的主动学习需解决若干实际挑战：扩展至大规模候选池、利用能量-力监督信号，以及在候选池相对于目标分布存在偏差时保持鲁棒性。本研究联合应对这些挑战：首先提出一种线性扩展的获取框架，基于分块特征空间后验方差筛选，通过避免候选集与训练集核矩阵的显式计算，可在数小时内筛选约20万个结构，且广泛适用于基于分子相似性度量评分候选的获取策略；随后通过混合参数-坐标导数将神经切线核（NTK）扩展为力感知NTK及联合能量-力NTK，自然提供矢量场预测的相似性度量。在OC20数据集上，联合能量-力NTK表现最优：在所有指标与分布划分下均取得最低的能量与力MAE和RMSE；在T1x、PMechDB和RGD基准测试中，我们的力感知NTK方法在保持竞争力的同时显著优于基于委员会的方法。在T1x数据集的受控候选池偏移案例中，基于预训练MLIP嵌入与NTK的获取策略保持鲁棒性，而委员会方法则表现出更高方差。总体而言，这些结果表明，单个预训练MLIP即可实现可扩展、力感知且分布鲁棒的主动学习，适用于基础模型微调。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the need for scalable and robust active learning in machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs), tackling challenges such as large candidate pools, energy-force supervision, and distribution bias. The authors propose a linearly scaling acquisition framework using chunked feature-space posterior-variance shortlisting, enabling rapid screening of up to 200k structures. They extend the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) to a force-aware version using mixed parameter-coordinate derivatives, creating a joint energy-force NTK that naturally measures vector-field similarity. Experiments on OC20 show the joint NTK achieves the lowest energy and force MAE and RMSE across all splits. On T1x, PMechDB, and RGD benchmarks, force-aware NTK methods outperform committee-based baselines in efficiency while remaining competitive. In a controlled distribution shift on T1x, NTK-based acquisition remains robust, whereas committee methods show higher variance.
该研究旨在解决机器学习原子势（MLIPs）主动学习中的三大挑战：大规模候选池筛选、能量-力监督利用及在候选池偏移时的鲁棒性。作者提出一种基于分块特征空间后验方差筛选的线性扩展获取框架，无需显式计算核矩阵，可在数小时内筛选约20万个结构，并适用于基于分子相似度的候选评估策略。他们通过混合参数-坐标导数扩展神经切线核（NTK），构建力感知NTK与联合能量-力NTK，自然度量矢量场相似性。在OC20、T1x、PMechDB和RGD数据集上，联合NTK在所有指标和分布划分下均取得最低的能量与力误差，力感知NTK方法在保持竞争力的同时显著优于基于委员会的方法。在受控分布偏移实验中，基于预训练MLIP嵌入和NTK的获取策略表现稳健，而委员会方法则方差更大。
Mind the Gap: How Elicitation Protocols Shape the Stated-Revealed Preference Gap in Language Models
Authors: Pranav Mahajan, Ihor Kendiukhov, Syed Hussain, Lydia Nottingham
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-29T16:51:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:08:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Eval Eval Workshop and 3rd Technical AI Safety Conference (TAIS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work identifies a stated-revealed (SvR) preference gap in language models (LMs): a mismatch between the values models endorse and the choices they make in context. Existing evaluations rely heavily on binary forced-choice prompting, which entangles genuine preferences with artifacts of the elicitation protocol. We systematically study how elicitation protocols affect SvR correlation across 24 LMs. Allowing neutrality and abstention during stated preference elicitation allows us to exclude weak signals, substantially improving Spearman's rank correlation ($ρ$) between volunteered stated preferences and forced-choice revealed preferences. However, further allowing abstention in revealed preferences drives $ρ$ to near-zero or negative values due to high neutrality rates. Finally, we find that system prompt steering using stated preferences during revealed preference elicitation does not reliably improve SvR correlation on AIRiskDilemmas. Together, our results show that SvR correlation is highly protocol-dependent and that preference elicitation requires methods that account for indeterminate preferences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注意差距：引出协议如何塑造语言模型中的陈述-揭示偏好差距
近期研究发现语言模型中存在陈述-揭示（SvR）偏好差距：模型所表达的价值与其在上下文中的选择不一致。现有评估过度依赖二元强制选择提示，将真实偏好与引出协议的伪迹混在一起。我们系统研究了24个语言模型中引出协议如何影响SvR相关性。在陈述偏好引出时允许中立和弃权，可排除弱信号，显著提升自愿陈述偏好与强制选择揭示偏好的斯皮尔曼等级相关系数（ρ）。然而，进一步在揭示偏好中允许弃权会导致ρ趋近于零或为负值，因中立率过高。最后，我们发现，在揭示偏好引出时使用陈述偏好引导系统提示，无法可靠提升AIRiskDilemmas上的SvR相关性。综上，我们的结果表明SvR相关性高度依赖于协议，偏好引出需要考虑不确定偏好的方法。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how elicitation protocols influence the stated-revealed (SvR) preference gap in language models, motivated by the observation that existing evaluations using binary forced-choice prompts confound true preferences with protocol artifacts. The authors systematically evaluate 24 LMs under varying elicitation conditions, including allowing neutrality and abstention in stated preferences, which significantly improves Spearman’s rank correlation (ρ) between stated and revealed preferences. However, permitting abstention in revealed preferences alone causes ρ to drop near zero or become negative due to high neutrality rates. Crucially, system prompt steering based on stated preferences fails to reliably enhance SvR correlation on AIRiskDilemmas, demonstrating that SvR alignment is highly sensitive to protocol design and that effective preference elicitation must accommodate indeterminate responses.
该研究探讨了提示协议如何影响语言模型中的陈述-揭示偏好差距，动机源于现有评估方法（如二元强制选择）可能混淆真实偏好与协议伪迹。作者在24个模型上系统评估不同提示协议，允许陈述偏好中中立与弃权可显著提升斯皮尔曼相关系数（ρ），但若仅允许揭示偏好中弃权，则ρ趋近于零或负值，因中立率过高。关键发现是基于陈述偏好的系统提示引导无法稳定提升AIRiskDilemmas上的SvR相关性，表明SvR一致性高度依赖协议设计，有效偏好采集需容纳不确定响应。
Interpretable Machine Learning for Antepartum Prediction of Pregnancy-Associated Thrombotic Microangiopathy Using Routine Longitudinal Laboratory Data
Authors: Chuanchuan Sun, Zhen Yu, Qin Fan, Qingchao Chen, Feng Yu
First: 2026-05-13T17:07:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:07:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background: Pregnancy-associated thrombotic microangiopathy (P-TMA) is rare but life-threatening. Early risk prediction before overt clinical presentation remains challenging, as the associated laboratory abnormalities are subtle, multidimensional, and frequently masked by common physiological changes such as gestational thrombocytopenia and pregnancy-related proteinuria, thus overlapping heavily with benign obstetric and renal conditions. This complexity is poorly captured by univariate or rule-based approaches; however, it is addressable by machine learning, which can extract latent, time-dependent risk signatures from longitudinal clinical tests. Methods: This retrospective study included 300 pregnancies comprising 142 P-TMA cases and 158 controls. After exclusion of identifiers and non-informative variables, 146 longitudinal laboratory predictors were retained. Participants were divided into a training cohort (80%) and a held-out test cohort (20%) using stratified sampling. Five algorithms were evaluated: logistic regression, support vector machine with radial basis function kernel, random forest, extra trees, and gradient boosting. The final model was selected by mean cross-validated AUROC, refitted on the full training cohort, and evaluated once in the held-out test cohort. Interpretability analyses examined global feature importance and distributional patterns of leading predictors. Results: Gradient boosting was prespecified by cross-validation in the training cohort. The model achieved an AUROC of 0.872 (95% CI: 0.769-0.952) and an AUPRC of 0.883 (95% CI: 0.780-0.959) in a held-out test cohort, with sensitivity of 0.750 and specificity of 0.812. Conclusions: Longitudinal clinical laboratory tests obtained during routine care contained informative and clinically plausible signals for P-TMA risk. Notably, cystatin C at week 6 showed promise as an early monitoring indicator.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用常规纵向实验室数据进行妊娠相关血栓性微血管病的产前可解释机器学习预测
背景：妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）罕见但危及生命。在明显临床表现前进行早期风险预测仍具挑战，因相关实验室异常细微、多维，常被妊娠性血小板减少症和妊娠相关蛋白尿等常见生理变化掩盖，与良性产科及肾病状况高度重叠。此类复杂性难以被单变量或规则基础方法捕捉，但机器学习可通过纵向临床检测提取潜在的时间依赖性风险特征。方法：本回顾性研究纳入300例妊娠，含142例P-TMA病例及158例对照。排除标识符及无信息变量后，保留146个纵向实验室预测变量。参与者按分层抽样分为训练队列（80%）和保留测试队列（20%）。评估五种算法：逻辑回归、径向基函数核支持向量机、随机森林、极端随机树及梯度提升。最终模型依据交叉验证平均AUROC选择，于完整训练队列重新拟合，并在保留测试队列中评估一次。可解释性分析考察全局特征重要性及主要预测变量的分布模式。结果：梯度提升在训练队列交叉验证中预先指定。模型在保留测试队列中AUROC为0.872（95%CI：0.769-0.952），AUPRC为0.883（95%CI：0.780-0.959），敏感度0.750，特异度0.812。结论：常规护理中获取的纵向临床实验室数据包含P-TMA风险的有意义且临床合理的信号。值得注意的是，孕6周胱抑素C显示出作为早期监测指标的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of early predicting pregnancy-associated thrombotic microangiopathy (P-TMA) using subtle and overlapping laboratory markers, this study applied interpretable machine learning to longitudinal clinical data from 300 pregnancies. Five algorithms were tested, with gradient boosting selected for its superior performance in cross-validated AUROC. The final model, trained on 146 longitudinal lab predictors, achieved an AUROC of 0.872 and AUPRC of 0.883 in an independent test cohort, with 75% sensitivity and 81% specificity. Interpretability analysis identified cystatin C at week 6 as a key early indicator, demonstrating that routine lab data can reveal clinically meaningful risk patterns.
鉴于妊娠相关微血管血栓性血细胞减少症（P-TMA）早期识别困难，该研究利用常规纵向实验室数据构建机器学习模型，纳入300例妊娠数据。在五种算法中，梯度提升模型表现最优，最终模型在独立测试集上达到AUROC 0.872、AUPRC 0.883，敏感度75%，特异度81%。可解释性分析显示，孕6周的胱抑素C是早期预警的潜在指标。
Amplification to Synthesis: A Comparative Analysis of Cognitive Operations Before and After Generative AI
Authors: Liz Cho, Dongwook Yoon
First: 2026-05-13T17:06:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:06:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cognitive operations are a rising concern in the geopolitical sphere, a quiet yet rigorous fight for public perception and decision making. While such operations have been extensively studied in the context of bot-driven amplification, the emergence of generative AI introduces a new set of capabilities that may have fundamentally altered how these operations are designed and executed. The possible evolution of cognitive operation via generative AI puts nation states vulnerable without proper mitigation strategies. To address this, we compared behavioral and linguistic coordination patterns in X (formerly Twitter) datasets from the 2016 and 2024 U.S. presidential elections. Utilizing a combined corpus of over 133,000 posts, we applied post-type distribution, semantic clustering, temporal synchrony analysis, and Jaccard-based lexical overlap measures. Findings suggest that the 2024 corpus exhibits a distinct pattern from 2016. Original content rose from 59% to 93% with retweets virtually disappeared; lexical overlap collapsed from a mean Jaccard score of 0.99 to 0.27, with posts converging on the same subject matter expressed in markedly different words; and temporal coordination shifted from pervasive cross-semantic synchrony to narratively concentrated co-occurrence. Taken together, these patterns point toward an operational logic organized around active content generation and narrative-specific targeting - characteristics consistent with generative AI involvement. These findings offer an empirical baseline for future research investigating generative AI's role in the cognitive operation pipeline, and as a practical reference point for security practitioners developing detection frameworks calibrated to the post-generative AI threat environment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：放大至合成：生成式人工智能前后认知操作的比较分析
认知操作已成为地缘政治领域的重要议题，是一场静默却严谨的公众认知与决策影响之战。尽管此类操作在机器人驱动的放大效应背景下已得到广泛研究，生成式人工智能的出现则带来了全新的能力，可能从根本上改变了这些操作的设计与执行方式。若缺乏适当缓解策略，认知操作可能通过生成式人工智能发生根本性演变，使国家面临风险。为此，我们比较了2016年与2024年美国大选期间X（原Twitter）平台的数据集中的行为与语言协调模式。基于超过13.3万条帖子的综合语料库，我们运用帖子类型分布、语义聚类、时间同步分析及基于Jaccard的词汇重叠度测量方法。研究发现，2024年语料库呈现出与2016年截然不同的模式：原创内容比例从59%升至93%，转发几乎消失；词汇重叠度从平均Jaccard得分0.99骤降至0.27，帖子虽聚焦相同主题，但表达方式显著不同；时间协调模式也从跨语义广泛同步转变为叙事集中共现。这些模式共同指向一种以主动内容生成和叙事特定目标导向为特征的操作逻辑，与生成式人工智能参与高度一致。本研究为未来探究生成式人工智能在认知操作流程中的作用提供了实证基准，也为安全从业者构建适应生成式人工智能后威胁环境的检测框架提供了实践参考。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing geopolitical relevance of cognitive operations and the disruptive potential of generative AI, this study compares behavioral and linguistic patterns in X datasets from the 2016 and 2024 U.S. presidential elections. Using a corpus of over 133,000 posts, the researchers applied post-type distribution, semantic clustering, temporal synchrony analysis, and Jaccard-based lexical overlap measures to detect shifts in coordination strategies. Results show that in 2024, original content surged to 93% (up from 59% in 2016), retweets nearly vanished, lexical overlap plummeted from 0.99 to 0.27, and temporal coordination shifted from cross-semantic synchrony to narrative-focused co-occurrence — patterns consistent with generative AI-driven content generation and targeted narrative deployment.
鉴于认知操作在地缘政治中的日益重要性及生成式AI带来的颠覆性影响，本研究对比了2016年与2024年美国总统大选期间X平台上的行为与语言模式。通过对13万多条帖子的分析，研究者运用帖子类型分布、语义聚类、时间同步性分析及Jaccard词库重叠度测量方法，发现2024年原创内容占比升至93%（2016年为59%），转发几乎消失，词汇重叠度从0.99骤降至0.27，时间协调从跨语义同步转向叙事集中共现——这些特征与生成式AI驱动的内容生成和定向叙事部署高度吻合。
Attention Once Is All You Need: Efficient Streaming Inference with Stateful Transformers
Authors: Victor Norgren
First: 2026-05-13T17:06:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:06:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conventional transformer inference engines are request-driven, paying an O(n) prefill cost on every query. In streaming workloads, where data arrives continuously and queries probe an ever-growing context, this cost is prohibitive. We introduce a data-driven computational model centred on stateful sessions: a persistent KV cache advanced incrementally as new data arrives, so prefill is moved off the critical path and query latency becomes O(|q|), independent of accumulated context size. Building on this, Flash Queries reclaim idle GPU cycles between data arrivals to pre-evaluate registered questions and return cached answers before the user asks, a pattern that is structurally impossible in stateless engines because they discard intermediate state between requests. A multi-tenant continuous-batching scheduler with cell-budget admission and prefix-aware grouped prefill lets dozens of stateful sessions coexist on a single GPU while preserving full quadratic self-attention. On streaming market-data benchmarks the reference implementation achieves up to 5.9x speedup over conventional inference engines (vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, llama.cpp), holding query latency constant as accumulated context grows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注意力只需一次：基于状态化Transformer的高效流式推理
传统Transformer推理引擎以请求驱动，每个查询均需支付O(n)的预填充成本。在流式工作负载中，数据持续到达，查询探查不断增长的上下文，此成本难以承受。我们引入一种以状态化会话为中心的数据驱动计算模型：通过持续增量更新持久化KV缓存，将预填充移出关键路径，使查询延迟降至O(|q|)，与累积上下文大小无关。在此基础上，Flash Queries利用数据到达间隙中的空闲GPU周期，提前预评估已注册问题并在用户提问前返回缓存答案——这一模式在无状态引擎中因每次请求间丢弃中间状态而结构上不可能。结合多租户连续批处理调度器（含单元预算准入与前缀感知分组预填充），数十个状态化会话可共存于单个GPU上，同时保持完整的二次自注意力。在流式市场数据基准测试中，参考实现相较传统推理引擎（vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、llama.cpp）最高提速5.9倍，且在累积上下文增长时保持查询延迟恒定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of conventional transformer inference engines that incur O(n) prefill costs per query in streaming scenarios, the paper proposes a stateful transformer model using persistent KV caches that incrementally update as new data arrives, shifting prefill off the critical path and reducing query latency to O(|q|). It further introduces Flash Queries to pre-evaluate and cache answers during idle GPU cycles between data arrivals, a capability unattainable in stateless engines. Experimental results on streaming market-data benchmarks show the proposed system achieves up to 5.9x speedup over state-of-the-art inference engines (vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, llama.cpp) while maintaining constant query latency as context grows.
为了解决传统Transformer推理引擎在流式场景中每次查询都需O(n)预填充导致效率低下问题，该论文提出基于持久化KV缓存的状态化Transformer模型，新数据到达时缓存增量更新，使预填充脱离关键路径，查询延迟降至O(|q|)。同时引入Flash Queries机制，在数据到达间隙预计算并缓存答案，这是无状态引擎无法实现的。在流式市场数据基准测试中，该方案相较vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、llama.cpp等主流引擎最高提速5.9倍，且在上下文持续增长时仍保持查询延迟恒定。
LMU-Based Sequential Learning and Posterior Ensemble Fusion for Cross-Domain Infant Cry Classification
Authors: Niloofar Jazaeri, Hilmi R. Dajani, Marco Janeczek, Martin Bouchard
First: 2026-02-24T23:44:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:05:54+00:00
