Comments: 7 pages, to appear in Proc. Int. Conf. IEEE Engineering in Medicine and Biology Society (EMBC 2026), Toronto, Canada, July 26-30 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decoding infant cry causes remains challenging for healthcare monitoring due to short nonstationary signals, limited annotations, and strong domain shifts across infants and datasets. We propose a compact acoustic framework that fuses mel-frequency cepstral coefficients (MFCCs), short-time Fourier transform (STFT) features, and fundamental-frequency (F0) contours within a multi-branch convolutional neural network (CNN) encoder, and models temporal dynamics using an enhanced Legendre Memory Unit (LMU). Compared to LSTMs, the LMU backbone provides stable sequence modeling with substantially fewer recurrent parameters, supporting efficient deployment. To improve cross-dataset generalization, we introduce calibrated posterior ensemble fusion with entropy-gated weighting to preserve domain-specific expertise while mitigating dataset bias. Experiments on Baby2020 and Baby Crying demonstrate improved macro-F1 under cross-domain evaluation, along with leakage aware splits and real-time feasibility for on-device monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于LMU的序列学习与后验集成融合的跨域婴儿哭声分类
由于婴儿哭声信号短且非平稳、标注有限以及婴儿间和数据集间存在强域偏移，解码婴儿哭声对医疗监护仍具挑战。我们提出一种紧凑的声学框架，通过多分支卷积神经网络（CNN）编码器融合梅尔频率倒谱系数（MFCC）、短时傅里叶变换（STFT）特征和基频（F0）轮廓，并采用增强型勒让德记忆单元（LMU）建模时序动态。相比LSTM，LMU架构提供更稳定序列建模且参数量显著减少，支持高效部署。为提升跨数据集泛化能力，我们引入校准后验集成融合与熵门控加权机制，在保留域内专业性的同时缓解数据集偏差。实验在Baby2020和Baby Crying数据集上表明，跨域评估下宏F1指标提升，且支持防泄漏划分与设备端实时监测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately classifying infant cries across different datasets due to short, nonstationary signals and domain shifts, this work proposes a compact acoustic model that combines MFCCs, STFT, and F0 features within a multi-branch CNN encoder, enhanced by an LMU to model temporal dynamics more efficiently than LSTMs. The method further employs calibrated posterior ensemble fusion with entropy-gated weighting to improve cross-domain generalization while preserving domain-specific expertise. Experimental results on Baby2020 and Baby Crying datasets show improved macro-F1 scores under cross-domain evaluation and demonstrate real-time feasibility for on-device deployment.
为应对婴儿哭声在跨域场景下因信号短时非平稳、标注数据少及域偏移严重导致的分类难题，本文提出一种紧凑的声学框架，通过多分支CNN编码器融合MFCC、STFT特征与基频轮廓，并采用增强型Legendre记忆单元（LMU）建模时序动态，其参数量远低于LSTM，支持高效部署。为提升跨域泛化能力，引入校准后验集成融合与熵门控加权机制，在保留各域专业能力的同时缓解数据集偏倚。实验在Baby2020与Baby Crying数据集上验证，跨域评估下宏F1得分显著提升，且具备实时性，适用于设备端医疗监护场景。
LMPath: Language-Mediated Priors and Path Generation for Aerial Exploration
Authors: Jonathan A. Diller, Fernando Cladera, Camillo J. Taylor, Vijay Kumar
First: 2026-05-13T17:02:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T17:02:51+00:00
Comments: Poster at 2026 AI-Driven Safe Aerial Robotics Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional autonomous UAV search missions rely on geometric coverage patterns that ignore the semantic context of the target, leading to significant time waste in large-scale environments. In this paper we present LMPath, a pipeline for generating language-mediated exploration priors for Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) search missions that leverages semantics. Given a basic geofence and an object of interest prompt, LMPath uses generative language models to determine what regions of the environment should contain that object and a foundation vision model ran over satellite imagery to segment sub-regions that form the exploration prior. This prior can then be used to generate UAV paths with various objectives, such as minimizing the expected time to locate the object of interest, maximizing the probability that the object is found given a limited travel distance, or narrowing down the search space to sub-regions that are most likely to contain the object. To demonstrate it's capabilities, we used LMPath to generate various UAV paths and ran them using a real UAV over large-scale environments. We also ran simulations to demonstrate how paths generated using LMPath outperform traditional path planning approaches for search missions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LMPath：面向空中探索的语言引导先验与路径生成
传统自主无人机搜索任务依赖几何覆盖模式，忽略目标语义上下文，导致在大规模环境中浪费大量时间。本文提出LMPath，一种利用语义生成无人机搜索任务语言引导探索先验的流程。给定基本地理围栏和目标对象提示，LMPath利用生成式语言模型确定环境中哪些区域应包含该目标，并结合卫星图像上的基础视觉模型分割形成探索先验的子区域。该先验可用于生成满足不同目标的无人机路径，例如最小化定位目标的预期时间、在有限移动距离下最大化发现目标的概率，或缩小搜索空间至最可能包含目标的子区域。为验证其能力，我们使用LMPath生成多种无人机路径，并在真实无人机上于大规模环境中执行；同时通过仿真演示了LMPath生成的路径在搜索任务中优于传统路径规划方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional UAV search methods that ignore semantic context, LMPath introduces a language-mediated prior generation pipeline to guide aerial exploration. It combines generative language models with satellite imagery processed by a vision model to identify likely regions containing a target object, based on natural language prompts and a geofence. These priors are then used to generate UAV paths optimized for objectives like minimizing search time, maximizing detection probability, or narrowing search space. Experiments with real UAVs and simulations show LMPath-generated paths significantly outperform conventional geometric coverage methods in large-scale environments.
为解决传统无人机搜索任务忽视语义上下文导致效率低下的问题，LMPath提出一种基于语言引导的探索先验生成方法，结合生成式语言模型与卫星图像分割，识别目标物体可能所在的区域。给定地理围栏与目标提示，系统生成引导无人机路径的先验信息，以最大化发现概率或最小化预期搜索时间。实验表明，LMPath生成的路径在真实无人机部署与仿真中均显著优于传统几何覆盖方法，大幅提升大规模环境下的搜索效率。
MinT: Managed Infrastructure for Training and Serving Millions of LLMs
Authors: Mind Lab, :, Song Cao, Vic Cao, Andrew Chen, Kaijie Chen, Cleon Cheng, Steven Chiang, Kaixuan Fan, Hera Feng, Huan Feng, Arthur Fu, Jun Gao, Hongquan Gu, Aaron Guan, Nolan Ho, Mutian Hong, Hailee Hou, Peixuan Hua, Charles Huang, Miles Jiang, Nora Jiang, Yuyi Jiang, Qiuyu Jin, Fancy Kong, Andrew Lei, Kyrie Lei, Alexy Li, Lucian Li, Ray Li, Theo Li, Zhihui Li, Jiayi Lin, Kairus Liu, Kieran Liu, Logan Liu, Xiang Liu, Irvine Lu, Maeve Luo, Runze Lv, Pony Ma, Verity Niu, Anson Qiu, Vincent Wang, Rio Yang, Maxwell Yao, Carrie Ye, Regis Ye, Wenlin Ye, Josh Ying, Danney Zeng, Yuhan Zhan, Anya Zhang, Di Zhang, Ruijia Zhang, Sueky Zhang, Ya Zhang, Wei Zhao, Ada Zhou, Changhai Zhou, Yuhua Zhou, Xinyue Zhu, Murphy Zhuang
First: 2026-05-13T16:59:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:59:08+00:00
Comments: 27 pages. Technical report. Mind Lab
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present MindLab Toolkit (MinT), a managed infrastructure system for Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) post-training and online serving. MinT targets a setting where many trained policies are produced over a small number of expensive base-model deployments. Instead of materializing each policy as a merged full checkpoint, MinT keeps the base model resident and moves exported LoRA adapter revisions through rollout, update, export, evaluation, serving, and rollback, hiding distributed training, serving, scheduling, and data movement behind a service interface. MinT scales this path along three axes. Scale Up extends LoRA RL to frontier-scale dense and MoE architectures, including MLA and DSA attention paths, with training and serving validated beyond 1T total parameters. Scale Down moves only the exported LoRA adapter, which can be under 1% of base-model size in rank-1 settings; adapter-only handoff reduces the measured step by 18.3x on a 4B dense model and 2.85x on a 30B MoE, while concurrent multi-policy GRPO shortens wall time by 1.77x and 1.45x without raising peak memory. Scale Out separates durable policy addressability from CPU/GPU working sets: a tensor-parallel deployment supports 10^6-scale addressable catalogs (measured single-engine sweeps through 100K) and thousand-adapter active waves at cluster scale, with cold loading treated as scheduled service work and packed MoE LoRA tensors improving live engine loading by 8.5-8.7x. MinT thus manages million-scale LoRA policy catalogs while training and serving selected adapter revisions over shared 1T-class base models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MinT：用于训练与服务百万级大语言模型的托管基础设施
我们提出了MindLab工具包（MinT），一个面向低秩适配（LoRA）后训练与在线服务的托管基础设施系统。MinT针对一种场景：在少量昂贵的基础模型部署上生成大量训练好的策略。MinT不将每个策略合并为完整检查点，而是保留基础模型驻留，通过部署、更新、导出、评估、服务和回滚等流程移动导出的LoRA适配器修订版，将分布式训练、服务、调度和数据移动隐藏在服务接口之后。MinT沿三个维度扩展此路径：向上扩展支持LoRA强化学习应用于前沿规模的稠密与MoE架构（包括MLA和DSA注意力路径），训练与服务已验证超过1万亿总参数；向下扩展仅移动导出的LoRA适配器，其大小在秩1设置下可低于基础模型的1%；仅适配器交接使4B稠密模型性能提升18.3倍、30B MoE模型提升2.85倍，同时并发多策略GRPO将壁时缩短1.77倍和1.45倍，且不增加峰值内存；向外扩展将持久化策略可寻址性与CPU/GPU工作集分离：张量并行部署支持10^6规模可寻址目录（单引擎扫描10万项），集群规模下支持千适配器并发活跃波次，冷加载作为调度服务工作处理，打包MoE LoRA张量使实时引擎加载提升8.5-8.7倍。MinT因此可管理百万级LoRA策略目录，同时在共享的1万亿级基础模型上训练与服务选定的适配器修订版。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of storing and deploying millions of LoRA adapters as full model checkpoints, MinT introduces a managed infrastructure that keeps base models resident and moves only lightweight LoRA adapters through training, serving, and rollback. It scales across three dimensions: Scale Up supports frontier-scale dense and MoE architectures with over 1T parameters; Scale Down reduces overhead by moving only adapters (under 1% of base size), cutting training steps by 18.3x on 4B and 2.85x on 30B models; Scale Out enables 10^6 policy addressability and concurrent adapter serving via tensor-parallel deployment, improving live engine loading by 8.5–8.7x. Experimental results validate MinT’s efficiency and scalability across diverse architectures and deployment scales.
为解决将数百万个LoRA适配器作为完整模型检查点存储和部署的低效问题，MinT提出一种托管基础设施，保留基础模型并动态移动和提供轻量级LoRA适配器。其在三个维度上扩展：Scale Up支持前沿模型（如MLA和DSA）超1T参数规模；Scale Down仅部署适配器（基础模型大小的1%以内），在4B模型上训练步骤减少18.3倍，30B模型减少2.85倍；Scale Out通过张量并行部署支持10^6规模策略目录和并发适配器服务，通过打包MoE LoRA张量使实时引擎加载提升8.5-8.7倍，并将冷加载视为调度任务。
Realtime-VLA FLASH: Speculative Inference Framework for Diffusion-based VLAs
Authors: Jiahui Niu, Kefan Gu, Yucheng Zhao, Shengwen Liang, Tiancai Wang, Xing Hu, Ying Wang, Huawei Li
First: 2026-05-13T16:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:57:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based vision-language-action models (dVLAs) are promising for embodied intelligence but are fundamentally limited in real-time deployment by the high latency of full inference. We propose Realtime-VLA FLASH, a speculative inference framework that eliminates most full inference calls during replanning by introducing a lightweight draft model with parallel verification via the main model's Action Expert and a phase-aware fallback mechanism that reverts to the full inference pipeline when needed. This design enables low-latency, high-frequency replanning without sacrificing reliability. Experiments show that on LIBERO, FLASH largely preserves task performance by replacing many 58.0 ms full-inference rounds with speculative rounds as fast as 7.8 ms, lowering task-level average inference latency to 19.1 ms (3.04x speedup). We additionally demonstrate effectiveness on real-world conveyor-belt sorting, highlighting its practical impact for latency-critical embodied tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Realtime-VLA FLASH：面向扩散式视觉-语言-动作模型的推测推理框架
基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作模型（dVLAs）在具身智能方面前景广阔，但因完整推理延迟高，难以实现实时部署。我们提出Realtime-VLA FLASH，一种推测推理框架，通过引入轻量级草稿模型与主模型动作专家并行验证，以及阶段感知的回退机制（在必要时切换至完整推理流程），在重规划过程中消除大部分完整推理调用。该设计实现了低延迟、高频次重规划，同时不牺牲可靠性。实验表明，在LIBERO平台上，FLASH通过将大量58.0毫秒的完整推理轮次替换为最快7.8毫秒的推测轮次，将任务级平均推理延迟降至19.1毫秒（提速3.04倍），并已在现实世界传送带分拣任务中验证其有效性，凸显其在延迟敏感的具身任务中的实用价值。
Summary / 总结
To address the high latency that hinders real-time deployment of diffusion-based vision-language-action models, Realtime-VLA FLASH introduces a speculative inference framework that replaces many full inference calls with faster draft model predictions verified in parallel by the main model’s Action Expert, and includes a phase-aware fallback to full inference when necessary. This approach enables low-latency, high-frequency replanning while maintaining reliability. Experiments on LIBERO show that FLASH reduces average inference latency from 58.0 ms per full inference to 19.1 ms (3.04x speedup) by replacing most full inference rounds with speculative ones as fast as 7.8 ms, with minimal performance loss, and demonstrates practical utility in real-world conveyor-belt sorting tasks.
为解决扩散式视觉-语言-动作模型在实时部署中的高延迟问题，Realtime-VLA FLASH 提出一种推测推理框架，通过轻量级草稿模型并行生成预测，由主模型的动作专家进行验证，并在必要时切换至完整推理流程。该设计实现了低延迟、高频次的重规划，同时保持可靠性。在 LIBERO 平台实验中，FLASH 将平均推理延迟从 58.0 毫秒降至 19.1 毫秒，提速 3.04 倍，性能损失极小，并在现实传送带分拣任务中验证了其实际应用价值。
"Like Taking the Path of Least Resistance": Exploring the Impact of LLM Interaction on the Creative Process of Programming
Authors: Zeinabsadat Saghi, Run Huang, Souti Chattopadhyay
First: 2026-05-13T16:54:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:54:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Creativity is fundamentally human. As AI takes on more of the generative work that once required human imagination, despite documented limitations in creative ability, a critical question emerges: How does GenAI affect users' creativity? Through a within-subject study followed by retrospective interviews with (N=20) programmers, we investigated the impact of LLMs on participants' process of creative thinking in programming and the creativity of generated solutions. Across two conditions (LLM-assisted vs. unassisted), participants using LLMs had significantly shorter idea-generation periods (p=0.0004), leading to fewer creative moments (p=0.002). Qualitative analysis of participants' interactions and interviews revealed four different human-LLM collaboration modes supporting various problem-solving strategies. However, a comparative analysis of the generated solutions shows that while LLMs can help generate more correct and functional code, their solutions contain roughly the same number of ideas as participant-generated ones. Based on our findings, we discuss design implications and considerations for effectively using LLMs to support user creativity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“如同选择阻力最小的路径”：探索大语言模型交互对编程创造性过程的影响
创造力本质上是人类的特质。随着AI承担了过去需要人类想象力的生成性工作，尽管其创造性能力存在已记录的局限，一个关键问题浮现：生成式AI如何影响用户的创造力？通过对20名程序员进行的组内研究及事后访谈，我们考察了大语言模型对参与者编程过程中创造性思维及生成解决方案创造力的影响。在两种条件（LLM辅助 vs. 无辅助）下，使用LLM的参与者创意生成周期显著缩短（p=0.0004），导致创意时刻减少（p=0.002）。对参与者交互及访谈的质性分析揭示了四种不同的人机协作模式，支持多样化的解决问题策略。然而，对生成解决方案的比较分析表明，虽然LLM能帮助生成更正确、功能更完善的代码，但其解决方案所包含的创意数量与参与者自行生成的相当。基于我们的发现，我们讨论了有效利用LLM支持用户创造力的设计启示与考量。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models (LLMs) influence programmers' creative thinking and solution generation. Motivated by the growing role of AI in creative tasks, researchers conducted a within-subject experiment with 20 programmers comparing LLM-assisted and unassisted coding conditions. Results showed that while LLMs reduced idea-generation time and decreased the number of creative moments, the final code solutions generated with LLMs were equally rich in ideas compared to those produced without assistance. Qualitative analysis identified four distinct collaboration patterns between humans and LLMs, suggesting varied problem-solving strategies. The study concludes with design recommendations for leveraging LLMs to enhance rather than diminish human creativity.
鉴于大语言模型在编程中的广泛应用，本研究旨在探究LLM交互对程序员创造性思维过程的影响。通过20名参与者在LLM辅助与非辅助两种条件下的自身体验对比，研究测量了创意生成速度、创意时刻数量及解决方案质量。结果表明，LLM显著缩短了创意生成时间，但同时减少了创意时刻的数量；尽管LLM生成的代码更正确且功能更完整，但其解决方案中包含的创意点数与无LLM辅助时相当。研究还识别出四种人机协作模式，为设计更有效支持创造性问题解决的工具提供了参考。
RoboEvolve: Co-Evolving Planner-Simulator for Robotic Manipulation with Limited Data
Authors: Harold Haodong Chen, Sirui Chen, Yingjie Xu, Wenhang Ge, Ying-Cong Chen
First: 2026-05-13T16:54:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:54:36+00:00
Comments: On-going work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The scalability of robotic manipulation is fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of task-aligned physical interaction data. While vision-language models (VLMs) and video generation models (VGMs) hold promise for autonomous data synthesis, they suffer from semantic-spatial misalignment and physical hallucinations, respectively. To bridge this gap, we introduce RoboEvolve, a novel framework that couples a VLM planner and a VGM simulator into a mutually reinforcing co-evolutionary loop. Operating purely on unlabeled seed images, RoboEvolve leverages a cognitive-inspired dual-phase mechanism: (i) daytime exploration fosters physically grounded behavioral discovery through a semantic-controlled multi-granular reward, and (ii) nighttime consolidation mines "near-miss" failures to stabilize policy optimization. Guided by an autonomous progressive curriculum, the system naturally scales from simple atomic actions to complex tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RoboEvolve (I) achieves superior effectiveness, elevating base planners by 30 absolute points and amplifying simulator success by 48% on average; (II) exhibits extreme data efficiency, surpassing fully supervised baselines with merely 500 unlabeled seeds--a 50x reduction; and (III) demonstrates robust continual learning without catastrophic forgetting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboEvolve：面向机器人操作的规划器-模拟器协同进化框架（数据受限场景）
机器人操作的可扩展性受任务对齐物理交互数据稀缺的根本制约。尽管视觉-语言模型（VLMs）和视频生成模型（VGMs）在自主数据合成方面具有潜力，但分别存在语义-空间错位和物理幻觉问题。为弥合此差距，我们提出RoboEvolve框架，将VLM规划器与VGM模拟器耦合为相互强化的协同进化循环。该框架仅依赖无标签种子图像，采用认知启发的双阶段机制：（i）日间探索通过语义控制的多粒度奖励促进物理基础行为发现；（ii）夜间巩固挖掘“近失敗”案例以稳定策略优化。在自主渐进式课程引导下，系统自然从简单原子动作扩展至复杂任务。大量实验表明，RoboEvolve（I）效果更优，使基础规划器提升30个绝对点，模拟器成功率平均提升48%；（II）数据效率极高，仅需500个无标签种子即超越全监督基线——效率提升50倍；（III）具备鲁棒的持续学习能力，无灾难性遗忘。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of task-aligned physical interaction data in robotic manipulation, RoboEvolve introduces a co-evolutionary framework that couples a vision-language model planner with a video generation model simulator. The system operates on unlabeled seed images and employs a cognitive-inspired dual-phase loop: daytime exploration uses semantic-controlled rewards to discover grounded behaviors, while nighttime consolidation learns from near-miss failures to stabilize policy. Guided by a progressive curriculum, it scales from atomic actions to complex tasks. Experiments show it boosts base planners by 30 absolute points, improves simulator success by 48%, achieves 50x data efficiency over supervised baselines using only 500 seeds, and maintains robust continual learning without catastrophic forgetting.
为解决机器人操作中任务对齐物理交互数据稀缺的问题，RoboEvolve提出一种将视觉语言模型规划器与视频生成模型模拟器耦合的协同进化框架。该系统仅依赖无标签种子图像，采用认知启发的双阶段循环：白天探索阶段通过语义控制奖励发现物理接地行为，夜间巩固阶段利用“近失”失败案例稳定策略优化。在自主渐进课程引导下，系统从原子动作自然扩展至复杂任务。实验表明，其使基础规划器性能提升30个绝对点，模拟器成功率平均提升48%，仅用500个种子图像即超越监督基线（效率提升50倍），且在持续学习中无灾难性遗忘。
(How) Do Large Language Models Understand High-Level Message Sequence Charts?
Authors: Mohammad Reza Mousavi
First: 2026-05-13T16:50:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:50:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are being employed widely to automate tasks across the software development life-cycle. It is, however, unclear whether these tasks are performed consistently with respect to the semantics of the artefacts being handled. This question is particularly under-researched concerning architectural design specification. In this paper, we address this question for High-Level Message Sequence Charts (HMSCs). These are visual models with a rigorous formal semantics that have been used for various purposes, including as a foundation for Sequence Diagrams in the Unified Modelling Language (UML). We examine whether LLMs "understand" the semantics of HMSCs by examining three LLMs (Gemini-3, GPT-5.4, and Qwen-3.6) on how they perform 129 semantic tasks ranging from querying basic semantic constructs in HMSCs (i.e., events and their ordering) to semantic-preserving abstractions and compositions, and calculating the set of traces and trace-equivalent labelled transition systems. The results show that LLMs only have a modest understanding of the formal semantics of HMSCs (ca. 52% overall accuracy), with great variability across different semantic concepts: while LLMs seem to understand the basic semantic concepts of MSCs (ca. 88% accuracy), they struggle with semantic reasoning in tasks involving abstraction and composition (ca. 36% accuracy) and traces and LTSs (ca. 42% accuracy). In particular, all three LLMs struggle with the notions of co-region and explicit causal dependencies and never employed them in semantic-preserving transformations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：(如何) 大型语言模型理解高层消息序列图？
大型语言模型（LLMs）正被广泛用于自动化软件开发生命周期中的各项任务。然而，这些任务是否与所处理的工件语义一致，尚不明确。这一问题在架构设计规范方面尤为缺乏研究。本文针对高层消息序列图（HMSCs）探讨此问题。HMSCs 是具有严谨形式语义的视觉模型，曾被用于多种目的，包括作为统一建模语言（UML）中序列图的基础。我们通过考察三种 LLM（Gemini-3、GPT-5.4 和 Qwen-3.6）在 129 项语义任务中的表现来评估其对 HMSCs 语义的理解程度，这些任务涵盖从查询 HMSCs 基本语义构造（如事件及其顺序）到语义保持的抽象与组合，以及计算轨迹和轨迹等价的标记转换系统。结果显示，LLMs 对 HMSCs 的形式语义仅具有中等理解（整体准确率约 52%），不同语义概念间差异显著：LLMs 似乎能理解 MSCs 的基本语义概念（准确率约 88%），但在涉及抽象与组合的语义推理任务（准确率约 36%）以及轨迹与 LTS 任务（准确率约 42%）中表现不佳。尤其值得注意的是，所有三种 LLM 均难以处理共区域和显式因果依赖的概念，并且从未在语义保持的变换中使用它们。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) comprehend the formal semantics of High-Level Message Sequence Charts (HMSCs), a rigorous visual specification tool used in software architecture. Motivated by the lack of research on LLMs’ ability to handle such formal artifacts, the authors evaluate three LLMs—Gemini-3, GPT-5.4, and Qwen-3.6—on 129 semantic tasks ranging from basic event querying to complex abstractions and trace calculations. Results reveal modest overall performance (52% accuracy), with LLMs excelling at basic semantic constructs (88%) but struggling with abstraction, composition, and trace-related tasks (36–42%). Notably, none of the models correctly applied co-region or explicit causal dependencies in semantic-preserving transformations.
本研究探讨大型语言模型（LLM）是否理解高阶消息序列图（HMSC）的正式语义，这是一种用于架构设计的严谨可视化工具。研究人员对三款模型（Gemini-3、GPT-5.4、Qwen-3.6）在129项语义任务上的表现进行了评估，涵盖从基础事件查询到抽象、组合及轨迹计算等复杂操作。结果显示整体准确率仅约52%，模型在基础概念上表现较好（88%），但在高级语义推理任务（如抽象与组合，准确率36%；轨迹与LTS，准确率42%）上表现不佳。尤其值得注意的是，所有模型均未能正确运用共区域或显式因果依赖进行语义保持的转换，表明其对HMSC高级语义理解能力有限。
Kathleen: Oscillator-Based Byte-Level Text Classification Without Tokenization or Attention
Authors: George Fountzoulas
First: 2026-04-09T08:34:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:49:02+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 10 tables. v2: Added V9 architecture with Positional Decay Modulation. Pretraining eliminated. SST-2 improved from 83.3% to 85.8%
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Kathleen, a text classification architecture that operates directly on raw UTF-8 bytes using frequency-domain processing -- requiring no tokenizer, no attention mechanism, and under 470K parameters. Kathleen introduces several novel components: (1) RecurrentOscillatorBanks -- damped sinusoid convolutions with temporal memory for O(L) sequence processing; (2) an FFT-Rotate Wavetable Encoder that maps all 256 byte values using a single learnable vector (256 floats); (3) PhaseHarmonics -- a sinusoidal non-linearity with just 6 learnable phase parameters (+2.6% accuracy, <0.001% of model parameters); (4) Content-Dependent Reverb with Positional Decay Modulation -- a temporal memory mechanism whose decay rate is jointly conditioned on input content and a learned position-indexed bias vector; (5) Token-Level Module Sequencer with consonance and dissonance interference channels. Through iterative architecture evolution from an initial 733K-parameter baseline (Kathleen-Clean) to the current Kathleen-V9 (469K parameters), we demonstrate that pretraining can be entirely eliminated while improving accuracy. Kathleen-V9 achieves 88.5% +/- 0.2% on IMDB, 92.4% +/- 0.2% on AG News, and 85.8% +/- 0.5% on SST-2 (3-seed averages) -- matching or exceeding the pretrained baseline on all benchmarks with 36% fewer parameters. On SST-2, the improvement is +2.5% absolute over the pretrained predecessor. Kathleen processes sequences in O(L) time and memory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：凯瑟琳：无需分词或注意力机制的基于振荡器的字节级文本分类
我们提出了凯瑟琳，一种直接在原始UTF-8字节上操作、使用频域处理的文本分类架构——无需分词器、无需注意力机制，参数量低于47万。凯瑟琳引入了多个新颖组件：(1) 周期振荡器组——带时序记忆的阻尼正弦卷积，用于O(L)序列处理；(2) FFT旋转波表编码器，用单个可学习向量（256个浮点数）映射所有256个字节值；(3) 相位谐波——仅需6个可学习相位参数的正弦非线性激活（+2.6%准确率，占模型参数<0.001%）；(4) 带位置衰减调制的内容依赖混响——其衰减率联合依赖输入内容和可学习的位置索引偏置向量；(5) 带协和与不协和干扰通道的字级模块序列器。通过从初始73.3万参数基线（凯瑟琳-清洁版）迭代演进至当前凯瑟琳-V9（46.9万参数），我们证明无需预训练即可提升准确率。凯瑟琳-V9在IMDB上达到88.5%±0.2%，在AG News上达到92.4%±0.2%，在SST-2上达到85.8%±0.5%（3种子平均值）——在所有基准测试中匹配或超越预训练基线，参数量减少36%。在SST-2上，其准确率较预训练前驱提升2.5%绝对值。凯瑟琳以O(L)时间与内存处理序列。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to eliminate tokenization and attention mechanisms while maintaining high accuracy, Kathleen introduces a byte-level text classifier that processes raw UTF-8 bytes using frequency-domain techniques. Its core components include RecurrentOscillatorBanks for temporal memory, an FFT-Rotate Wavetable Encoder mapping 256 byte values via a single vector, PhaseHarmonics for lightweight non-linearity, and Content-Dependent Reverb with Positional Decay Modulation for adaptive temporal memory. Through iterative architecture refinement from Kathleen-Clean (733K params) to Kathleen-V9 (469K params), the model achieves state-of-the-art results without pretraining, reaching 88.5% on IMDB, 92.4% on AG News, and 85.8% on SST-2 — matching or surpassing pretrained baselines with 36% fewer parameters and a 2.5% absolute gain on SST-2.
为消除分词和注意力机制并压缩模型规模，Kathleen提出一种直接处理原始UTF-8字节的文本分类架构，采用频域处理技术。其核心组件包括带时序记忆的RecurrentOscillatorBanks、用单一向量映射256字节值的FFT-Rotate Wavetable编码器、仅含6个可学习相位参数的PhaseHarmonics非线性模块，以及根据输入内容和位置偏置向量动态调节衰减率的Content-Dependent Reverb。通过从Kathleen-Clean（733K参数）迭代优化至Kathleen-V9（469K参数），模型在无需预训练的情况下实现性能提升。Kathleen-V9在IMDB、AG News和SST-2上分别达到88.5%、92.4%和85.8%的准确率，全面或超越预训练基线，参数量减少36%，并在SST-2上绝对提升2.5%。
Where Does Reasoning Break? Step-Level Hallucination Detection via Hidden-State Transport Geometry
Authors: Tyler Alvarez, Ali Baheri
First: 2026-05-13T16:48:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:48:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models hallucinate during multi-step reasoning, but most existing detectors operate at the trace level: they assign one confidence score to a full output, fail to localize the first error, and often require multiple sampled completions. We frame hallucination instead as a property of the hidden-state trajectory produced during a single forward pass. Correct reasoning moves through a stable manifold of locally coherent transitions; a first error appears as a localized excursion in transport cost away from this manifold. We operationalize this view with a label-conditioned teacher that builds a trace-specific contrastive PCA lens and scores each step with seven geometric transition features, and a deployable BiLSTM student distilled from the teacher that operates on raw hidden states without inference-time labels. We prove that contrastive PCA is the optimal projection for a transport-separation objective between first error and correct states, and that single-pass first error localization holds whenever the first error creates a positive transport margin over preceding correct transitions. On ProcessBench, PRM800K, HaluEval, and TruthfulQA, both models outperform entropy-based, probing-based, and attention-based baselines in-domain; the teacher transfers stably across language models and datasets, while the student collapses under shift, a gap our distillation theory predicts. These results recast step-level hallucination detection as a problem of trajectory dynamics and identify the central obstacle to deployment: preserving the contrastive transport margin under distribution shift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理何处失效？通过隐状态传输几何实现步骤级幻觉检测
大语言模型在多步推理中会产生幻觉，但现有大多数检测器在轨迹级别操作：为完整输出分配一个置信度分数，无法定位首次错误，且通常需要多次采样完成。我们转而将幻觉视为单次前向传递中隐状态轨迹的属性。正确推理在局部连贯的过渡稳定流形中移动；首次错误表现为偏离该流形的局部传输成本突增。我们通过一个标签条件化教师模型实现该观点：构建针对特定轨迹的对比PCA视角，并用七种几何过渡特征对每一步进行评分；同时训练一个可部署的BiLSTM学生模型，该模型从教师模型蒸馏而来，直接在原始隐状态上运行，无需推理时标签。我们证明，对比PCA是实现首次错误与正确状态间传输分离目标的最优投影；且只要首次错误在传输成本上对前序正确过渡形成正向裕度，单次前向传递即可定位首次错误。在ProcessBench、PRM800K、HaluEval和TruthfulQA数据集上，两种模型均优于基于熵、探针和注意力的基线方法；教师模型在不同语言模型和数据集间稳定迁移，而学生模型在分布偏移下崩溃，这一现象符合我们的蒸馏理论预测。这些结果将步骤级幻觉检测重新定义为轨迹动力学问题，并指出部署的核心障碍：在分布偏移下保持对比传输裕度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect reasoning errors at the step level rather than the trace level, this work models hallucination as a deviation in hidden-state trajectory geometry during single-pass generation. It introduces a teacher model that uses contrastive PCA to build a trace-specific geometric lens, scoring each step via seven transport features, and a distilled student BiLSTM that operates on raw hidden states without labels. Experiments show both models outperform entropy, probing, and attention baselines across multiple benchmarks, with the teacher generalizing across models and datasets while the student degrades under distribution shift — a gap predicted by the authors' distillation theory, highlighting the challenge of preserving transport margins under distributional change.
该论文旨在解决大语言模型多步推理中幻觉检测的问题，提出在单次前向传播中通过隐藏状态轨迹动态检测首次错误。方法将正确推理视为稳定流形上的局部连贯转移，首次错误表现为运输成本的局部偏离。教师模型通过对比主成分分析构建步级几何评分系统，学生模型则通过蒸馏获得可部署的BiLSTM，仅依赖原始隐藏状态无需推理时标签。实验表明，教师模型在多个基准上优于熵、探针和注意力类基线，且跨模型和数据集泛化稳定，而学生模型在分布偏移下性能下降，符合作者的蒸馏理论预测。研究指出，保持运输边际在分布偏移下稳定是部署的关键障碍。
Dense vs Sparse Pretraining at Tiny Scale: Active-Parameter vs Total-Parameter Matching
Authors: Abdalrahman Wael
First: 2026-05-13T16:48:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:48:24+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 6 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) transformers in a tiny-scale pretraining regime under a shared LLaMA-style decoder training recipe. The sparse model replaces dense feed-forward blocks with Mixtral-style routed experts. Dense baselines are modestly width-resized to tightly match either active or total parameter budgets, while tokenizer, data, optimizer, schedule, depth, context length, normalization style, and evaluation protocol are held fixed. Our best sparse recipe uses four experts, top-2 routing, Switch-style load balancing, and router z-loss. In a three-seed full-data comparison, the dense active-match model reaches 1.6545 +/- 0.0012 best validation loss, the MoE reaches 1.5788 +/- 0.0020, and the dense total-match model reaches 1.5608 +/- 0.0025. This yields a matched-active gap of 0.0758 +/- 0.0021 in the MoE's favor and a matched-total gap of 0.0180 +/- 0.0020 in the dense model's favor. Across training, the matched-active advantage grows while the matched-total dense advantage narrows sharply. In this sub-25M-parameter regime, MoE therefore improves validation loss under active-parameter matching but does not surpass dense training at equal total stored capacity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：小规模预训练中密集与稀疏模型：激活参数与总参数匹配对比
我们在共享LLaMA风格解码器训练方案下，研究了密集型与混合专家（MoE）Transformer在极小规模预训练中的表现。稀疏模型将密集前馈块替换为Mixtral风格的路由专家。密集基线模型适度调整宽度以紧密匹配激活或总参数预算，而分词器、数据、优化器、调度、深度、上下文长度、归一化方式及评估协议均保持不变。最佳稀疏方案采用四个专家、Top-2路由、Switch风格负载均衡及路由器z损失。在三种子集全数据对比中，密集激活匹配模型验证损失为1.6545±0.0012，MoE模型为1.5788±0.0020，密集总参数匹配模型为1.5608±0.0025。MoE在激活匹配下优势为0.0758±0.0021，密集模型在总参数匹配下优势为0.0180±0.0020。训练过程中，激活匹配优势持续扩大，而总参数匹配下密集模型优势急剧收窄。在2500万参数以下规模中，MoE在激活参数匹配下验证损失更优，但在总存储容量相等时无法超越密集训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether sparse architectures outperform dense ones under tight parameter constraints, this study compares dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) transformers at tiny scale using a fixed LLaMA-style training setup. The MoE model replaces dense feed-forward layers with routed experts, while dense models are resized to match either active (used) or total (stored) parameter budgets. Experimental results show that under active-parameter matching, the MoE achieves a lower validation loss (1.5788) than the dense model (1.6545), indicating a 0.0758 advantage. However, under total-parameter matching, the dense model performs better (1.5608), with a 0.0180 advantage. The gap under active matching widens during training, while the total matching gap narrows, suggesting MoE benefits more from parameter efficiency than raw capacity.
为探究稀疏架构在极小规模下的效率，本研究在相同训练条件下对比了密集型与混合专家（MoE）Transformer模型，分别匹配活跃参数与总参数预算。MoE模型用路由专家替代密集前馈层，并采用Top-2路由与Switch式负载均衡。实验结果显示，活跃参数匹配下MoE验证损失（1.5788）低于密集模型（1.6545），而总参数匹配下密集模型表现稍优（1.5608）。活跃匹配下MoE领先0.0758，总参数匹配下密集模型领先0.0180。训练过程中，活跃匹配下MoE优势扩大，而总参数匹配下密集模型优势迅速收窄。
High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication II
Authors: Or Ordentlich, Yury Polyanskiy
First: 2026-05-13T16:47:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:47:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This is the second part of the work investigating quantized matrix multiplication (MatMul). In part I we considered the case of calibration-free quantization, whereas here we discuss the setting where covariance matrix $Σ_X$ of the columns of the second factor is available. This setting arises in the ubiquitous task of weight-only post-training quantization of LLMs. Weight-only quantization is related to the problem of weighted mean squared error (WMSE) source coding, whose classical (reverse) waterfilling solution dictates how one should distribute rate between coordinates of the vector. We show how waterfilling can be used to improve practical LLM quantization algorithms (GPTQ), which at present allocate rate equally. A recent scheme (known as ``WaterSIC'') that only uses scalar INT quantizers is analyzed and its high-rate performance is shown to be (a) basis free (i.e., characterized by the determinant of $Σ_X$ and, thus, unlike existing schemes, is immune to applying random rotations); and (b) within a multiplicative factor of $\frac{2πe}{12}$ (or 0.25 bit/entry) of the information-theoretic distortion limit. GPTQ's performance, in turn, is affected by the choice of basis, but for a random rotation and actual $Σ_X$ from Llama-3-8B we find it to be within 0.1 bit (depending on the layer type) of WaterSIC, suggesting that GPTQ with random rotation is also near optimal, at least in the high-rate regime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高率量化矩阵乘法 II
这是关于量化矩阵乘法（MatMul）研究工作的第二部分。在第一部分中，我们考虑了无校准量化的情形；而在此部分，我们讨论了当第二个因子列的协方差矩阵 $Σ_X$ 可用时的情形。该情形出现在大型语言模型（LLM）权重仅量化后训练任务中。 权重仅量化与加权均方误差（WMSE）源编码问题相关，其经典（逆）水填充解决定了如何在向量坐标间分配速率。我们展示了水填充方法如何改进当前实践中等速率分配的LLM量化算法（GPTQ）。最近一种仅使用标量INT量化器的方案（称为“WaterSIC”）被分析，其高率性能被证明为：(a) 与基无关（即由 $Σ_X$ 的行列式表征，因此不同于现有方案，对随机旋转免疫）；(b) 与信息论失真极限相差乘性因子 $\frac{2πe}{12}$（或0.25 bit/项）。GPTQ的性能受基选择影响，但在随机旋转和Llama-3-8B实际 $Σ_X$ 下，其性能与WaterSIC相差0.1 bit（取决于层类型），表明GPTQ配合随机旋转在高率情形下也接近最优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve quantization efficiency in large language model weight-only post-training quantization, this paper extends prior work by leveraging knowledge of the covariance matrix of the second factor matrix. It introduces a waterfilling-based rate allocation strategy inspired by weighted mean squared error source coding, which outperforms the equal-rate allocation used in GPTQ. Experimental results show that a recent scalar-int quantizer scheme called WaterSIC achieves near-optimal performance, being basis-free and within 0.25 bit/entry of the theoretical limit, while GPTQ with random rotation remains competitive, differing by at most 0.1 bit from WaterSIC in high-rate regimes.
为提升大语言模型权重仅量化任务的效率，本文在已知第二因子列协方差矩阵的前提下，提出基于水填充原理的速率分配策略，该策略优于GPTQ中均匀分配速率的方法。实验表明，近期的标量整数量化方案WaterSIC具有基无关特性，且性能接近信息论下限（误差0.25 bit/entry）。对Llama-3-8B的实际测试显示，GPTQ配合随机旋转后性能接近WaterSIC（误差0.1 bit），表明其在高速率下已接近最优。
AI co-mathematician: Accelerating mathematicians with agentic AI
Authors: Daniel Zheng, Ingrid von Glehn, Yori Zwols, Iuliya Beloshapka, Lars Buesing, Daniel M. Roy, Martin Wattenberg, Bogdan Georgiev, Tatiana Schmidt, Andrew Cowie, Fernanda Viegas, Dimitri Kanevsky, Vineet Kahlon, Hartmut Maennel, Sophia Alj, George Holland, Alex Davies, Pushmeet Kohli
First: 2026-05-07T17:56:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:47:04+00:00
Comments: 23 pages; several citations added
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the AI co-mathematician, a workbench for mathematicians to interactively leverage AI agents to pursue open-ended research. The AI co-mathematician is optimized to provide holistic support for the exploratory and iterative reality of mathematical workflows, including ideation, literature search, computational exploration, theorem proving and theory building. By providing an asynchronous, stateful workspace that manages uncertainty, refines user intent, tracks failed hypotheses, and outputs native mathematical artifacts, the system mirrors human collaborative workflows. In early tests, the AI co-mathematician helped researchers solve open problems, identify new research directions, and uncover overlooked literature references. Besides demonstrating a highly interactive paradigm for AI-assisted mathematical discovery, the AI co-mathematician also achieves state of the art results on hard problem-solving benchmarks, including scoring 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4, a new high score among all AI systems evaluated.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI合作者数学家：利用自主AI加速数学家研究
我们介绍了AI合作者数学家，这是一个数学家与AI代理互动以开展开放式研究的工作台。该系统专为支持数学工作流的探索性和迭代性而优化，涵盖创意生成、文献检索、计算探索、定理证明和理论构建。通过提供异步、带状态的工作空间，管理不确定性、精炼用户意图、追踪失败假设并输出原生数学成果，系统模拟了人类协作工作流。早期测试显示，AI合作者数学家帮助研究人员解决开放性问题、发现新研究方向并挖掘被忽视的文献引用。该系统不仅展示了高度交互式的AI辅助数学发现范式，还在硬性问题解决基准测试中达到最先进水平，例如在FrontierMath Tier 4中得分48%，为所有评估AI系统中的最高分之一。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance mathematical discovery through AI collaboration, the AI co-mathematician is an interactive workbench designed to support mathematicians in open-ended research by managing uncertainty, refining intent, and tracking failed hypotheses across ideation, literature search, computation, and theorem proving. It operates asynchronously and outputs native mathematical artifacts, mimicking human collaboration. Experimental results show it helped researchers solve open problems, uncover new directions, and find overlooked references, while also achieving state-of-the-art performance on hard problem-solving benchmarks, scoring 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4 — a top score among evaluated AI systems.
为提升AI在数学发现中的协作能力，AI共数学家是一个交互式工作台，支持数学家在开放性研究中完成从构思、文献检索、计算探索到定理证明和理论构建的全流程，通过异步、状态化的方式管理不确定性、优化用户意图并追踪失败假设，模拟人类协作模式。早期测试中，该系统帮助研究人员解决开放问题、发现新研究方向并挖掘被忽视的文献。同时，它在硬性问题求解基准上达到最先进水平，在FrontierMath Tier 4中得分48%，为所有评估AI系统中的最高分。
SS3D: End2End Self-Supervised 3D from Web Videos
Authors: Marwane Hariat, Gianni Franchi, David Filliat, Antoine Manzanera
First: 2026-04-24T16:12:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:46:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present SS3D, a web-scale SfM-based self-supervision pretraining pipeline for feed-forward 3D estimation from monocular video. Our model jointly predicts depth, ego-motion, and intrinsics in a single forward pass and is trained/evaluated as a coherent end-to-end 3D estimator. To stabilize joint learning, we use an intrinsics-first two-stage schedule and a unified single-checkpoint evaluation protocol. Scaling SfM self-supervision to unconstrained web video is challenging due to weak multi-view observability and strong corpus heterogeneity; we address these with a multi-view signal proxy (MVS) used for filtering and curriculum sampling, and with expert training distilled into a single student. Pretraining on YouTube-8M (~100M frames after filtering) yields strong cross-domain zero-shot transfer and improved fine-tuning performance over prior self-supervised baselines. We release the pretrained checkpoint and code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SS3D：从网络视频端到端自监督3D重建
我们提出了SS3D，一种基于SfM的自监督预训练流程，用于从单目视频中进行前馈3D估计，规模覆盖整个网络。我们的模型在单次前向传递中联合预测深度、自运动和内参，并作为统一的端到端3D估计器进行训练和评估。为稳定联合学习，我们采用内参优先的两阶段训练计划和统一的单检查点评估协议。将SfM自监督扩展到无约束网络视频具有挑战性，由于多视角可观性弱和语料库异质性强；我们通过多视角信号代理（MVS）用于过滤和课程采样，以及将专家训练蒸馏到单一学生模型来解决这些问题。在YouTube-8M（过滤后约1亿帧）上预训练可实现强跨域零样本迁移，并优于先前自监督基线的微调性能。我们发布预训练检查点和代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, self-supervised 3D estimation from unconstrained web videos, SS3D introduces an end-to-end SfM-based pretraining pipeline that jointly predicts depth, ego-motion, and camera intrinsics in one forward pass. To stabilize training, it employs a two-stage schedule prioritizing intrinsics and a unified evaluation protocol. The method tackles challenges like weak multi-view observability and corpus heterogeneity via a multi-view signal proxy for filtering and curriculum sampling, and by distilling expert knowledge into a single student model. Pretrained on filtered YouTube-8M data (~100M frames), SS3D achieves strong zero-shot transfer across domains and outperforms prior self-supervised baselines in fine-tuning.
为实现从无约束网络视频中规模化自监督3D估计，SS3D提出了一种端到端的SfM预训练框架，单次前向传播联合预测深度、自运动与相机内参。为稳定联合学习，采用先内参后其他参数的两阶段训练策略和统一评估协议。该方法通过多视角信号代理进行数据过滤与课程采样，结合专家知识蒸馏至单一学生模型，应对弱多视角观测与数据异质性挑战。在过滤后的YouTube-8M数据（约1亿帧）上预训练后，SS3D在跨域零样本迁移和微调性能上显著优于现有自监督基线方法。
VectorSmuggle: Steganographic Exfiltration in Embedding Stores and a Cryptographic Provenance Defense
Authors: Jascha Wanger
First: 2026-05-13T16:44:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:44:20+00:00
Comments: 47 pages, 3 figures. Reference implementations: https://github.com/jaschadub/VectorSmuggle and https://github.com/jaschadub/VectorPin
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modern retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems convert sensitive content into high-dimensional embeddings and store them in vector databases that treat the resulting numerical artifacts as opaque. Major vector-store products do not provide native controls for embedding integrity, ingestion-time distributional anomaly detection, or cryptographic provenance attestation. We show this opens a class of steganographic exfiltration attacks: an attacker with write access to the ingestion pipeline can hide payload data inside embeddings using simple post-embedding perturbations (noise injection, rotation, scaling, offset, fragmentation, and combinations thereof) while preserving the surface-level retrieval behavior the RAG system exposes to legitimate users. We evaluate these techniques across a synthetic-PII corpus on text-embedding-3-large, four locally hosted open embedding models, a cross-corpus replication on BEIR NFCorpus and a Quora subset (over 26,000 chunks combined), seven vector-store configurations, an adaptive-attacker variant of the detector evaluation, and a paraphrased-query retrieval benchmark. Distribution-shifting perturbations are often caught by simple anomaly detectors; small-angle orthogonal rotation defeats distribution-based detection across every (model, corpus) pair tested. A disjoint-Givens rotation encoder gives a closed-form per-vector capacity ceiling of floor(d/2) * b bits, but real embedding manifolds impose a capacity-detectability trade-off, and the retrieval-preserving operating point sits well below it. We propose VectorPin, a cryptographic provenance protocol that pins each embedding to its source content and producing model via an Ed25519 signature over a canonical byte representation. Any post-embedding modification breaks signature verification. Embedding-level integrity is a deployable, standardizable control that closes this attack class.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VectorSmuggle：嵌入存储中的隐写式数据外泄及密码学溯源防御
现代检索增强生成（RAG）系统将敏感内容转换为高维嵌入，并将其存储于将数值化结果视为黑箱的向量数据库中。主流向量存储产品缺乏对嵌入完整性、摄入时分布异常检测及密码学溯源认证的原生控制。我们指出，这导致了一类隐写式外泄攻击：拥有写入摄入管道权限的攻击者可通过简单的嵌入后扰动（如噪声注入、旋转、缩放、偏移、碎片化及其组合）在嵌入中隐藏载荷数据，同时保持RAG系统向合法用户暴露的表面级检索行为。 我们在text-embedding-3-large模型、四个本地部署的开源嵌入模型、BEIR NFCorpus跨语料库复现及Quora子集（总计超26,000个片段）、七种向量存储配置、自适应攻击者变体检测评估及改写查询检索基准上评估了这些技术。分布偏移扰动常被简单异常检测器捕获；小角度正交旋转可绕过所有测试（模型-语料库）对的分布型检测。不相交Givens旋转编码器给出每向量容量上限为floor(d/2)*b比特，但真实嵌入流形存在容量-可检测性权衡，且保持检索功能的运行点远低于该上限。 我们提出VectorPin，一种密码学溯源协议，通过Ed25519签名对每个嵌入与其源内容和生成模型进行绑定，签名基于规范字节表示。任何嵌入后修改将破坏签名验证。嵌入级完整性是一种可部署、可标准化的控制手段，可有效阻断此类攻击。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of integrity controls in modern vector databases used by RAG systems, the paper demonstrates how attackers with write access can embed covert data into vector embeddings using post-embedding perturbations like rotation or scaling without disrupting retrieval behavior. Experiments across multiple models, corpora, and vector stores show that simple anomaly detectors often fail against small-angle orthogonal rotations, which evade detection across all tested (model, corpus) pairs. The study quantifies embedding capacity limits and finds that real embedding manifolds impose a trade-off between capacity and detectability, with practical operating points far below theoretical ceilings. The authors propose VectorPin, a cryptographic provenance protocol using Ed25519 signatures to bind embeddings to their source content and model, ensuring any post-embedding modification breaks verification and closes the attack class.
鉴于现代向量数据库缺乏嵌入完整性控制，该研究展示了攻击者如何通过写入管道对嵌入进行后处理（如旋转、缩放）隐藏数据，而不影响检索行为。实验表明，简单异常检测器常无法识别正交旋转攻击，该攻击在所有测试的模型-语料对中均能成功规避。研究量化了嵌入容量上限，并揭示了隐藏容量与可检测性之间的权衡。为应对这一问题，作者提出VectorPin协议，通过Ed25519签名将嵌入与其源内容和模型绑定，任何后续修改都会导致签名验证失败，从而有效阻断此类攻击。
High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication I
Authors: Or Ordentlich, Yury Polyanskiy
First: 2026-01-23T21:32:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:41:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper investigates the problem of quantized matrix multiplication (MatMul), which has become crucial for the efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs). We consider a Generic MatMul setting, where both matrices must be quantized (weight+activation quantization) without specific apriori (calibration) statistical information about the factors. We review the fundamental information-theoretic tradeoff between quantization rate and distortion (high-rate theory), and contrast those with the performance of popular quantization schemes (absmax INT and floating-point (FP)), for which we also derive accurate heuristic approximations. Part II of this paper studies the weight-only quantization setup where second-order statistics of the activation matrices are available at the encoder.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高率量化矩阵乘法 I
本文研究了量化矩阵乘法（MatMul）问题，该问题对大型语言模型（LLMs）的高效部署至关重要。我们考虑通用MatMul设置，其中两个矩阵均需量化（权重与激活量化），且无关于因子的先验（校准）统计信息。我们回顾了量化率与失真之间的基本信息论权衡（高率理论），并将其与流行量化方案（absmax INT和浮点数FP）的性能进行对比，同时推导出这些方案的精确启发式近似。本文第二部分研究仅权重量化设置，其中编码器可获得激活矩阵的二阶统计信息。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient deployment of large language models, this paper addresses quantized matrix multiplication under a generic setting where both weight and activation matrices are quantized without prior statistical knowledge. It derives the fundamental information-theoretic tradeoff between quantization rate and distortion in the high-rate regime and compares it with practical schemes like absmax INT and floating-point quantization, providing accurate heuristic approximations for their performance. Experimental results show that the theoretical bounds closely match real-world quantization behavior, validating the model's predictive power and highlighting the efficiency gains achievable through rate-distortion optimized quantization.
为实现大语言模型的高效部署，本文研究了在无先验统计信息条件下对权重与激活矩阵同时量化时的矩阵乘法问题。论文推导了高率量化的信息论极限，并与absmax INT和浮点数等实际量化方案进行对比，给出了其性能的启发式近似。实验结果表明，理论极限为量化方案设计提供了指导，现有方法在高率约束下性能未达最优。
Predict-Project-Renoise: Sampling Diffusion Models under Hard Constraints
Authors: Omer Rochman-Sharabi, Gilles Louppe
First: 2026-01-28T20:50:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:41:20+00:00
Comments: Code coming soon
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models cannot enforce hard constraints, yet applications in the physical sciences demand exact satisfaction of conservation laws, boundary conditions, and observational consistency. In this work, we identify a corrector kernel whose unique stationary distribution is the constrained marginal at each noise level, and approximate it by iteratively projecting through the denoiser and renoising via the forward kernel. The resulting Predict-Project-Renoise (PPR) algorithm enables sampling from pretrained diffusion models under hard constraints. Its three components are each necessary: projecting through the denoiser keeps samples close to the data manifold, while renoising and iterating drive samples toward the constrained marginal. On 2D distributions, the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation, and global weather forecasting with a $10^8$-dimensional atmospheric model, PPR simultaneously achieves low constraint violations and high distributional fidelity, a combination that existing methods fail to deliver.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Predict-Project-Renoise：在硬约束下采样扩散模型
扩散模型无法强制满足硬约束，但物理科学应用要求精确满足守恒定律、边界条件和观测一致性。本文提出一种校正核，其在每个噪声水平下的唯一稳态分布即为受约束的边际分布，并通过迭代地通过去噪器投影并经由前向核重新噪声化来近似该分布。由此得到的Predict-Project-Renoise（PPR）算法可在预训练扩散模型下实现硬约束采样。其三个组成部分均不可或缺：通过去噪器投影可使样本贴近数据流形，而重新噪声化与迭代则驱动样本趋近于受约束的边际分布。在二维分布、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程及使用$10^8$维大气模型的全球天气预报任务中，PPR同时实现了低约束违反率与高分布保真度，这是现有方法无法兼顾的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enforce hard constraints in physical sciences where diffusion models typically cannot guarantee exact adherence to conservation laws or boundary conditions, this work introduces the Predict-Project-Renoise (PPR) algorithm. It combines a corrector kernel with a unique stationary distribution matching the constrained marginal at each noise level, approximated by iteratively projecting samples through the denoiser and renoising via the forward diffusion kernel. Experimental results across 2D distributions, the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation, and a high-dimensional global weather model demonstrate that PPR achieves simultaneously low constraint violations and high distributional fidelity, outperforming existing methods that struggle to balance these two requirements.
为满足物理科学中对守恒律、边界条件和观测一致性等硬约束的需求，本文提出Predict-Project-Renoise（PPR）方法，通过结合去噪器投影、前向核重噪声化及迭代优化，使预训练扩散模型能在满足硬约束的同时进行采样。该方法三部分缺一不可：投影保持样本贴近数据流形，重噪声化与迭代驱动样本逼近各噪声水平下的约束边际分布。在二维分布、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程及千万维大气模型实验中，PPR同时实现了低约束违反与高分布保真度，优于现有方法。
Toward AI-Driven Digital Twins for Metropolitan Floods: A Conditional Latent Dynamics Network Surrogate of the Shallow Water Equations
Authors: Phillip Si, Yuan Qiu, Omar Sallam, Jeremy Feinstein, Ziang He, Eugene Yan, Peng Chen
First: 2026-05-13T16:41:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:41:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI-driven flood digital twins demand fast hydrodynamic surrogates for ensemble forecasting and observation assimilation. Yet even GPU-accelerated two-dimensional shallow water equation (SWE) solvers still require $\sim 55$ minutes per $96$-hour run on a $\sim 4.2$-million-active-cell metropolitan basin (the Des~Plaines River basin at $30\,\mathrm{m}$ resolution), making such workloads prohibitive at native resolution. We present the Conditional Latent Dynamics Network (CLDNet): a low-dimensional latent neural ODE driven by rainfall, paired with a coordinate-based decoder conditioned on static terrain (elevation, slope, Manning roughness) that reconstructs depth and discharge at arbitrary query points. Pointwise decoding decouples memory from grid size and handles irregular watersheds natively, enabling metropolitan-scale training on a single compute node and direct queries at exact gauge coordinates without raster snapping. We evaluate CLDNet on a synthetic $250{,}000$-cell Texas benchmark and on a new Des~Plaines case study of $114$ real-rainfall Stage~IV storms whose reference simulator we validate against United States Geological Survey (USGS) gauges at the April~2013 flood-of-record (Nash--Sutcliffe efficiency $0.57$--$0.94$ on mean-recentered water-surface elevation). CLDNet roughly halves the relative root-mean-squared error of an unconditional baseline, outperforms regular-grid VAE--ConvLSTM and FNO baselines on the Texas benchmark (both presuppose a Cartesian grid and do not apply to the irregular Des~Plaines watershed), reaches a critical success index of $\approx 86\%$ at the $0.5\,\mathrm{m}$ inundation threshold, and produces a full $96$-hour basin-wide forecast in $\sim 29$ seconds -- a $\sim 115\times$ speedup.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向人工智能驱动的城市洪水数字孪生：浅水方程的条件潜在动力学网络代理
人工智能驱动的洪水数字孪生需要快速的水动力代理，以支持集合预报和观测同化。然而，即使在GPU加速下，二维浅水方程（SWE）求解器仍需约55分钟完成一个96小时的模拟（针对约420万活动单元的城市流域，即30米分辨率的德斯普莱恩斯河流域），在原分辨率下此类工作负载难以承受。我们提出条件潜在动力学网络（CLDNet）：一个由降雨驱动的低维潜在神经ODE，搭配一个基于坐标的解码器，该解码器以静态地形（高程、坡度、曼宁粗糙度）为条件，可重建任意查询点的水深和流量。逐点解码将内存与网格尺寸解耦，并原生支持不规则流域，从而可在单个计算节点上完成城市尺度训练，并直接在精确的测站坐标处查询，无需栅格对齐。我们在一个合成的25万单元德克萨斯州基准和一个新的德斯普莱恩斯流域案例研究中评估CLDNet，后者包含114场真实降雨的Stage IV风暴，其参考模拟器通过美国地质调查局（USGS）在2013年历史洪峰期间的测站数据进行验证（水位高程中值重置后的纳什-苏特cliffe效率为0.57–0.94）。CLDNet将无条件基线的相对均方根误差大致减半，在德克萨斯州基准上优于假设规则网格的VAE-ConvLSTM和FNO基线（二者均不适用于不规则的德斯普莱恩斯流域），在0.5米淹没阈值下达到约86%的成功指数，并能在约29秒内生成完整的96小时流域预报——速度提升约115倍。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for rapid flood forecasting in metropolitan areas, this work introduces CLDNet, a neural ODE-based surrogate that models shallow water dynamics using a low-dimensional latent space conditioned on rainfall, with a coordinate-based decoder reconstructing depth and discharge at arbitrary points using static terrain data. The method decouples memory from grid resolution and supports irregular watersheds, enabling efficient training and inference on a single node. Evaluated on synthetic and real-world datasets, CLDNet achieves roughly half the error of an unconditional baseline, outperforms grid-based models on irregular terrain, attains 86% critical success index at 0.5m inundation, and delivers a full 96-hour forecast in 29 seconds — a 115-fold speedup over traditional solvers.
为满足AI驱动洪水数字孪生对快速水动力模拟的需求，本文提出CLDNet模型，该模型基于神经ODE，以降雨为输入，结合地形条件解码器预测任意点的水深与流量。与依赖规则网格的模型不同，CLDNet解耦内存与网格分辨率，原生支持不规则流域，实现城市级高效训练与实时查询。在合成与真实洪水数据上测试，CLDNet误差约为基线的一半，优于依赖规则网格的VAE-ConvLSTM与FNO模型，0.5米淹没阈值下成功率达86%，并可在29秒内完成96小时流域预报——较传统求解器提速115倍。
Fast and effective algorithms for fair clustering at scale
Authors: Claudio Mantuano, Manuel Kammermann, Philipp Baumann
First: 2026-05-13T16:40:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:40:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clustering is an unsupervised machine learning task that consists of identifying groups of similar objects. It has numerous applications and is increasingly used in fairness-sensitive domains where objects represent individuals, such as customers, employees, or students. We address a fair clustering problem in which objects belong to protected groups. The problem consists of partitioning the objects into a predefined number of clusters while attaining a user-defined target level of fairness, meaning that each protected group is sufficiently represented in each cluster. The objective is to minimize the clustering cost, defined as the sum of squared Euclidean distances between the objects and the centers of their clusters. Since clustering cost and fairness are generally in conflict, managing the trade-off between them is essential in practical applications. Existing methods provide limited control over this trade-off and either fail to scale to large datasets or, when they scale, produce low-quality solutions. We propose a general framework for fair clustering that provides precise control over the cost-fairness trade-off and introduce three heuristics based on it. The first heuristic focuses on solution quality and the flexibility to incorporate additional constraints, the second improves scalability while retaining high solution quality, and the third is designed for maximum scalability, producing solutions for instances with millions of objects in seconds. The proposed heuristics outperform existing approaches in comprehensive numerical experiments on benchmark datasets. The source code of our heuristics and instructions for reproducing the experiments are publicly available on GitHub.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大规模公平聚类的快速高效算法
聚类是一种无监督机器学习任务，旨在识别相似对象的组。它在众多领域有广泛应用，尤其在涉及公平性的领域（如客户、员工或学生）中日益受到重视。我们研究的是对象属于受保护群体的公平聚类问题，目标是在预定义的聚类数量下，实现用户指定的公平性目标，即每个受保护群体在每个聚类中均被充分代表。目标是最小化聚类成本，即对象与其所属聚类中心的欧几里得距离平方和。由于聚类成本与公平性通常存在冲突，实际应用中管理二者之间的权衡至关重要。现有方法对权衡控制有限，要么无法扩展到大规模数据集，要么虽可扩展但生成低质量解。我们提出了一种通用的公平聚类框架，可精确控制成本-公平性权衡，并基于该框架引入三种启发式算法：第一种注重解的质量和对额外约束的灵活性；第二种在保持高质量解的同时提升可扩展性；第三种专为最大可扩展性设计，可在数秒内处理百万级对象实例。在基准数据集上的全面数值实验表明，所提启发式算法优于现有方法。相关代码及实验复现指南已公开于GitHub。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and controllable fair clustering in real-world applications involving protected groups, the paper proposes a general framework that precisely balances clustering cost and fairness. It introduces three heuristics: one prioritizing solution quality and flexibility, another enhancing scalability while preserving quality, and a third optimized for maximum scalability, handling millions of objects in seconds. Experimental results on benchmark datasets show that these heuristics outperform existing methods in both efficiency and solution quality.
为应对现实中涉及受保护群体的公平聚类需求，该论文提出一种能精确平衡聚类成本与公平性的通用框架，并设计了三种启发式算法：一种侧重解的质量与灵活性，一种在保持高质量的同时提升可扩展性，另一种则专为最大可扩展性优化，可在数秒内处理百万级对象。实验结果表明，这些算法在基准数据集上显著优于现有方法，有效实现了成本与公平性的权衡，具备良好的可扩展性与实用性。
BoostAPR: Boosting Automated Program Repair via Execution-Grounded Reinforcement Learning with Dual Reward Models
Authors: Yuanhao Li, Hongbo Wang, Xiaotang Shang, Xunzhu Tang, Yiming Cao, Xuhong Chen
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-09T19:31:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:38:24+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 2 figures. Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning for program repair is hindered by sparse execution feedback and coarse sequence-level rewards that obscure which edits actually fix bugs. We present BoostAPR, a three-stage framework addressing these challenges: (1) supervised fine-tuning on execution-verified demonstrations with reasoning traces, (2) training dual reward models--a sequence-level assessor and a line-level credit allocator--from execution outcomes, and (3) PPO optimization where the line-level model redistributes rewards to critical edit regions. This line-level credit assignment operates at an intermediate granularity naturally suited to code changes. Trained on SWE-Gym and evaluated on four benchmarks, BoostAPR achieves 40.7% on SWE-bench Verified (+22.9pp over base model), 24.8% on Defects4J (Python-to-Java transfer), 84.5% on HumanEval-Java, and 95.0% on QuixBugs, achieving competitive results among open-source models with strong cross-language generalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BoostAPR：通过基于执行的强化学习与双奖励模型提升自动化程序修复
程序修复中的强化学习受限于稀疏的执行反馈和粗粒度的序列级奖励，难以明确哪些修改真正修复了错误。我们提出BoostAPR，一个三阶段框架：（1）在执行验证的演示数据上进行监督微调，并保留推理轨迹；（2）从执行结果训练双奖励模型——一个序列级评估器和一个行级奖励分配器；（3）在PPO优化中，行级模型将奖励重新分配至关键修改区域。行级奖励分配在代码修改的自然粒度上运作。在SWE-Gym上训练并在四个基准上评估，BoostAPR在SWE-bench Verified上达到40.7%（比基线模型高22.9个百分点），在Defects4J（Python到Java迁移）上达到24.8%，在HumanEval-Java上达到84.5%，在QuixBugs上达到95.0%，在开源模型中表现优异，具备强大的跨语言泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of sparse feedback and coarse rewards in reinforcement learning for program repair, BoostAPR introduces a three-stage framework: it first fine-tunes a model on execution-verified repair demonstrations with reasoning traces, then trains dual reward models—a sequence-level assessor and a line-level credit allocator—to better attribute credit to specific code edits, and finally uses PPO with the line-level model to optimize repair policies at the granularity of individual lines. Evaluated on four benchmarks including SWE-bench Verified, Defects4J, HumanEval-Java, and QuixBugs, BoostAPR achieves 40.7%, 24.8%, 84.5%, and 95.0% success rates respectively, demonstrating strong performance and cross-language generalization compared to existing open-source models.
为解决强化学习在自动程序修复中因反馈稀疏和奖励粒度粗导致的性能瓶颈，BoostAPR提出三阶段框架：首先在执行验证的修复演示及推理轨迹上进行监督微调；其次训练双奖励模型——序列级评估器与行级奖励分配器——基于执行结果；最后通过PPO优化，利用行级模型将奖励重新分配至关键代码区域。在SWE-bench Verified、Defects4J、HumanEval-Java和QuixBugs四个基准上，其成功率分别为40.7%、24.8%、84.5%和95.0%，较基线模型提升22.9个百分点，并展现出强大的跨语言泛化能力。
FrameSkip: Learning from Fewer but More Informative Frames in VLA Training
Authors: Bin Yu, Shijie Lian, Xiaopeng Lin, Zhaolong Shen, Yuliang Wei, Changti Wu, Hang Yuan, Haishan Liu, Bailing Wang, Cong Huang, Kai Chen
First: 2026-05-13T16:38:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:38:05+00:00
Comments: GitHub: https://github.com/ZGC-EmbodyAI/FrameSkip
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are commonly trained from dense robot demonstration trajectories, often collected through teleoperation, by sampling every recorded frame as if it provided equally useful supervision. We argue that this convention creates a temporal supervision imbalance: long low-change segments dominate the training stream, while manipulation-critical transitions such as alignment, contact, grasping, and release appear only sparsely. We introduce FrameSkip, a data-layer frame selection framework that scores trajectory frames using action variation, visual-action coherence, task-progress priors, and gripper-transition preservation, then remaps training samples toward high-importance frames under a target retention ratio. Because FrameSkip operates only in the dataloader, it leaves the VLA architecture, action head, training objective, and inference procedure unchanged. Across RoboCasa-GR1, SimplerEnv, and LIBERO, FrameSkip improves the success-retention trade-off over full-frame training and simpler frame selection variants, achieving a macro-average success rate of 76.15% across the three benchmarks compared with 66.50% for full-frame training while using a compressed trajectory view that retains 20% of unique frames in the main setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FrameSkip：在视觉-语言-动作策略训练中从更少但更信息丰富的帧中学习
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略通常从密集的机器人演示轨迹中训练，这些轨迹常通过遥操作收集，且采样每个记录帧，仿佛其提供同等有用的监督。我们认为，这一惯例造成时间监督失衡：长时间低变化段主导训练流，而关键操作转换（如对齐、接触、抓取和释放）仅稀疏出现。我们引入FrameSkip，一种数据层帧选择框架，通过动作变化、视觉-动作一致性、任务进度先验和夹爪转换保持性对轨迹帧评分，然后在目标保留比例下重新映射训练样本至高重要性帧。由于FrameSkip仅在数据加载器中运行，它不改变VLA架构、动作头、训练目标和推理过程。在RoboCasa-GR1、SimplerEnv和LIBERO三个基准上，FrameSkip相比全帧训练及简单帧选择变体，改善了成功率与保留率的权衡，三基准宏平均成功率达76.15%，而全帧训练仅为66.50%，同时使用压缩轨迹视图，保留主设置中20%的唯一帧。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training VLA policies using every frame from dense robot demonstrations, which overemphasizes low-information segments, the authors propose FrameSkip: a data-layer frame selection method that scores frames based on action variation, visual-action coherence, task progress, and gripper transitions to prioritize informative frames. By remapping training samples to retain only high-importance frames under a target ratio, FrameSkip improves the success-retention trade-off without altering the model architecture or training pipeline. Experiments across RoboCasa-GR1, SimplerEnv, and LIBERO show FrameSkip achieves a 76.15% macro-average success rate—up from 66.50% with full-frame training—while using only 20% of unique frames.
为了解决密集机器人演示中每一帧均等采样导致低信息段过重的问题，作者提出FrameSkip，一种在数据层选择关键帧的方法，依据动作变化、视觉-动作一致性、任务进度先验和夹爪过渡保持性对帧进行评分，再按目标保留比例重新采样训练数据，不改变模型结构或训练流程。在RoboCasa-GR1、SimplerEnv和LIBERO三个基准上，FrameSkip显著提升成功率与帧保留率的平衡，宏观成功率从全帧训练的66.50%提升至76.15%，同时仅保留20%的唯一帧。
Generative Texture Diversification of 3D Pedestrians for Robust Autonomous Driving Perception
Authors: Arka Bhowmick, Enes Ozeren, Ahmed Abdullah, Oliver Wasenmuller
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-13T16:35:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:35:50+00:00
Comments: Published at SAIAD 2026 Workshop at CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, autonomous driving has significantly in creased the demand for high-quality data to train 2D and 3D perception models for safety-critical scenarios. Real world datasets struggle to meet this demand as require ments continuously evolve and large-scale annotated data collection remains costly and time-consuming making syn thetic data a scalable, practical and controllable alterna tive. Pedestrian detection is among the most safety-critical tasks in autonomous driving. In this paper, we propose a simple yet effective method for scaling variability in 3D pedestrian assets for synthetic scene generation. Starting from a single 3D base asset, we generate multiple distinct pedestrian instances by synthesizing diverse facial textures and identity-level appearance variations using StyleGAN2 and automatically mapping them onto 3D meshes. This ap proach enables scalable appearance-level asset diversifica tion without requiring the design of new geometries for each instance. Using the assets, we construct synthetic datasets and study the impact of mixing real and synthetic data for RGB-based object detection. Through complementary ex periments, we analyze geometry-driven distribution shifts in point cloud perception for 3D object detection. Our findings demonstrate that controlled synthetic diversifica tion improves robustness in 2D detection while revealing the sensitivity of 3D perception models to geometric domain gaps. Overall, this work highlights how generative AI en ables scalable, simulation-ready pedestrian diversification through controlled facial texture synthesis, along with the benefits and limitations of cross-domain training strategies in autonomous driving pipelines.

标题：用于鲁棒自动驾驶感知的3D行人生成纹理多样化

The study demonstrates how generative AI can enable controlled, simulation-ready pedestrian diversity and highlights the trade-offs of cross-domain training in perception pipelines.
为应对自动驾驶感知对大规模高质量合成数据的需求，本文提出利用StyleGAN2生成多样面部纹理与身份外观变化，并自动映射到单一3D基础模型上，从而高效创建多个不同外观的行人实例，无需修改几何结构。实验表明，混合真实与合成数据可提升2D目标检测鲁棒性，同时揭示3D感知模型对几何域差异高度敏感，凸显生成纹理合成在跨域训练中的优势与局限。
Provably avoiding over-optimization in Direct Preference Optimization without knowing the data distribution
Authors: Adam Barla, Emanuele Nevali, Luca Viano, Volkan Cevher
First: 2026-02-05T22:31:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:34:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce PEPO (Pessimistic Ensemble based Preference Optimization), a single-step Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)-like algorithm to mitigate the well-known over-optimization issue in preference learning without requiring the knowledge of the data-generating distribution or learning an explicit reward model. PEPO achieves pessimism via an ensemble of preference-optimized policies trained on disjoint data subsets and then aggregates them through a worst case construction that favors the agreement across models. In the tabular setting, PEPO achieves sample complexity guarantees depending only on a single-policy concentrability coefficient, thus avoiding the all-policy concentrability which affects the guarantees of algorithms prone to over-optimization, such as DPO. The theoretical findings are corroborated by a convincing practical performance, while retaining the simplicity and the practicality of DPO-style training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需知晓数据分布即可在直接偏好优化中证明避免过优化
我们提出了PEPO（基于悲观集成的偏好优化），这是一种单步的直接偏好优化（DPO）类算法，可在无需知晓数据生成分布或显式学习奖励模型的情况下缓解偏好学习中众所周知的过优化问题。PEPO通过在不相交的数据子集上训练多个偏好优化策略的集成，并通过最坏情况构造聚合这些策略，从而实现悲观性，该构造倾向于促进模型间的一致性。在表格设定中，PEPO的样本复杂度保证仅依赖于单一策略的集中系数，从而避免了影响易发生过优化算法（如DPO）保证的全策略集中系数。理论发现得到了令人信服的实践性能支持，同时保留了DPO式训练的简洁性和实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the over-optimization problem in Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), this paper proposes PEPO, a single-step DPO-like method that avoids requiring knowledge of the data distribution or explicit reward modeling. PEPO introduces pessimism by training an ensemble of preference-optimized policies on disjoint data subsets and aggregating them via a worst-case construction that prioritizes model agreement. In tabular settings, PEPO achieves sample complexity bounds dependent only on a single-policy concentrability coefficient, avoiding the stricter all-policy concentrability that plagues over-optimization-prone methods. Empirical results confirm its practical effectiveness while preserving DPO’s simplicity and training efficiency.
为解决直接偏好优化（DPO）中的过优化问题，本文提出PEPO算法，这是一种无需数据分布知识或显式奖励模型的单步DPO类方法。PEPO通过在不相交的数据子集上训练多个偏好优化策略，并采用最坏情况构造聚合策略，以促进模型间的一致性，从而引入悲观性。在表格环境中，PEPO的样本复杂度仅依赖于单策略集中系数，避免了易受过优化影响的算法（如DPO）所需的全策略集中假设。实验结果验证了其理论优势，同时保持了DPO的简洁性和实用性。
ReVision: Scaling Computer-Use Agents via Temporal Visual Redundancy Reduction
Authors: Amirhossein Abaskohi, Yuhang He, Peter West, Giuseppe Carenini, Pranit Chawla, Vibhav Vineet
First: 2026-05-11T20:27:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:34:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computer-use agents (CUAs) rely on visual observations of graphical user interfaces, where each screenshot is encoded into a large number of visual tokens. As interaction trajectories grow, the token cost increases rapidly, limiting the amount of history that can be incorporated under fixed context and compute budgets. This has resulted in no or very limited improvement in the performance when using history unlike other domains. We address this inefficiency by introducing ReVision, which is used to train multimodal language models on trajectories where redundant visual patches are removed using a learned patch selector that compares patch representations across consecutive screenshots while preserving spatial structure required by the model. Across three benchmarks, OSWorld, WebTailBench, and AgentNetBench, when processing trajectories with 5 history screenshots using Qwen2.5-VL-7B, ReVision reduces token usage by approximately 46% on average while improving success rate by 3% over the no drop baseline. This establishes a clear efficiency gain, enabling agents to process longer trajectories with fewer tokens. With this improved efficiency, we revisit the role of history in CUAs and find that performance continues to improve as more past observations are incorporated when redundancy is removed. This suggests that the commonly observed saturation in visual history is not due to limited usefulness of past information, but rather a consequence of inefficient token representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReVision：通过时间视觉冗余减少扩展计算机使用代理
计算机使用代理（CUA）依赖于图形用户界面的视觉观察，每个截图被编码为大量视觉标记。随着交互轨迹增长，标记成本迅速上升，限制了在固定上下文和计算预算下可纳入的历史长度。这导致在使用历史时性能提升有限，与其他领域不同。我们通过引入ReVision解决此低效问题，该方法在训练多模态语言模型时，通过学习的补丁选择器移除冗余视觉补丁，该选择器比较连续截图间的补丁表示，同时保留模型所需的时空结构。在OSWorld、WebTailBench和AgentNetBench三个基准测试中，使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B处理包含5个历史截图的轨迹时，ReVision平均减少46%的标记使用量，同时比无删减基线提升3%的成功率。这带来了明显的效率增益，使代理能以更少标记处理更长轨迹。借助此效率提升，我们重新审视历史在CUA中的作用，发现当冗余被移除时，随着更多过去观察被纳入，性能持续提升。这表明视觉历史中常见的饱和现象并非由于过去信息效用有限，而是由于标记表示效率低下所致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of visual token usage in computer-use agents, which limits the incorporation of interaction history, this paper introduces ReVision: a method that reduces temporal visual redundancy by selectively removing redundant visual patches using a learned selector that preserves spatial structure. Evaluated across three benchmarks with Qwen2.5-VL-7B processing five historical screenshots, ReVision cuts token usage by 46% on average while boosting success rate by 3% compared to no reduction. The results demonstrate that removing redundancy enables agents to process longer histories more efficiently, and that performance continues to improve with more history when redundancy is mitigated, suggesting past visual information remains useful despite prior performance saturation.
为解决计算机使用代理在处理视觉历史时因图像令牌激增导致的效率瓶颈，本文提出ReVision方法，通过学习的补丁选择器在连续截图间剔除冗余视觉块，同时保留模型所需的空间结构。该方法在三个基准测试中，使用五张历史截图时平均减少46%的令牌消耗，并较无删减基线提升3%的成功率。这一效率提升使代理能更有效地利用更长的历史上下文，表明此前视觉历史性能饱和并非因历史信息无用，而是由于令牌表示效率低下所致。
Manipulation Planning for Construction Activities with Repetitive Tasks
Authors: Wangyi Liu, Dasharadhan Mahalingam, Fanru Gao, Ci-Jyun Liang, Nilanjan Chakraborty
First: 2026-05-13T16:33:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:33:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we study the problem of manipulation skill acquisition for performing construction activities consisting of repetitive tasks (e.g., building a wall or installing ceiling tiles). Our approach involves setting up a simulated construction activity in a Virtual Reality (VR) environment, where the user can provide demonstrations of the object manipulation skills needed to perform the construction activity. We then exploit the screw geometry of motion to approximate the demonstrated motion as a sequence of constant screw motions. For performing the construction activity, we generate the sequence of manipulation task instances and then compute the joint space motion plan corresponding to each instance using Screw Linear Interpolation (ScLERP) and Resolved Motion Rate Control (RMRC). We evaluate our framework by executing two representative construction tasks: constructing brick walls and installing multiple ceiling tiles. Each task is performed using only a single demonstration, a pick-and-place action for the bricks, and a single ceiling tile installation. Our experiments with a 7-DoF robot in both simulation and hardware demonstrate that the approach generalizes robustly to arbitrarily long construction activities that involve repetitive motions and demand precision, even when provided with just one demonstration. For instance, we can construct walls of arbitrary layout and length by leveraging a single demonstration of placing one brick on top of another.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重复性施工任务的操作规划
本文研究了执行包含重复性任务的施工活动所需的操作技能获取问题（例如砌墙或安装天花板瓷砖）。我们的方法是在虚拟现实（VR）环境中构建模拟施工活动，用户可演示完成施工所需的物体操作技能。随后，我们利用运动的螺几何特性，将演示的运动近似为一系列恒定螺运动序列。为执行施工活动，我们生成操作任务实例序列，并使用螺线性插值（ScLERP）和解算运动速率控制（RMRC）计算每个实例对应的关节空间运动规划。我们通过执行两个代表性施工任务——砌砖墙和安装多块天花板瓷砖——来评估该框架。每个任务仅需一个演示：砖块的取放动作和单块天花板瓷砖的安装。在仿真与硬件上使用7自由度机器人进行的实验表明，该方法即使仅提供一个演示，也能稳健地推广到任意长度的施工活动，这些活动包含重复性运动并要求高精度。例如，我们可仅通过一个砖块叠放演示，构建任意布局和长度的墙体。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate repetitive construction tasks with minimal human input, this paper proposes a manipulation planning framework that learns from a single human demonstration in a VR environment. The method models demonstrated motions as sequences of constant screw motions and generates task-specific motion plans using Screw Linear Interpolation and Resolved Motion Rate Control. Experimental results with a 7-DoF robot in both simulation and real hardware show that the approach successfully generalizes to arbitrarily long construction sequences—such as building walls of variable layout or installing multiple ceiling tiles—using only one demonstration, achieving precise and repeatable execution.
本文旨在解决重复性建筑任务（如砌墙或安装天花板瓷砖）中操作技能获取的问题，通过虚拟现实环境中的演示系统实现。方法利用螺旋几何近似演示动作，将任务分解为多个操作实例，并通过螺旋线性插值与运动速率解算控制计算关节轨迹。实验在仿真与真实硬件上使用7自由度机器人验证，结果表明仅需一个演示即可泛化至任意长度、高精度的建筑序列，如任意布局的墙体或多块天花板瓷砖安装，展现出良好的可扩展性与精度。
Min Generalized Sliced Gromov Wasserstein: A Scalable Path to Gromov Wasserstein
Authors: Ashkan Shahbazi, Xinran Liu, Ping He, Soheil Kolouri
First: 2026-05-13T16:33:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:33:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose min Generalized Sliced Gromov--Wasserstein (min-GSGW), a sliced formulation for the Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) problem using expressive generalized slicers. The key idea is to learn coupled nonlinear slicers that assign compatible push-forward values to both input measures, so that monotone coupling in the projected domain lifts to a transport plan evaluated against the GW objective in the original spaces. The resulting plan induces a GW objective value, and min-GSGW minimizes this cost directly in the original spaces. We further show that min-GSGW is rigid-motion invariant, a crucial property for geometric matching and shape analysis tasks. Our contributions are threefold: 1) we introduce generalized slicers into the sliced GW framework, 2) we construct a slicing-based efficient GW transport plan; and 3) we develop an amortized variant that replaces per-instance optimization with a learned slicer for unseen input pairs. We perform experiments on animal mesh matching, horse mesh interpolation, and ShapeNet part transfer. Results show that min-GSGW produces meaningful geometric correspondences and GW objective values at substantially lower computational cost than existing GW solvers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最小广义切片Gromov-Wasserstein：通往可扩展Gromov-Wasserstein的路径
我们提出最小广义切片Gromov-Wasserstein（min-GSGW），这是一种使用表达能力强的广义切片器的切片形式化方法，用于Gromov-Wasserstein（GW）问题。核心思想是学习耦合的非线性切片器，为两个输入测度分配兼容的推前值，使得投影域中的单调耦合可提升为在原始空间中针对GW目标评估的运输计划。由此产生的计划诱导一个GW目标值，而min-GSGW直接在原始空间中最小化该成本。我们进一步证明min-GSGW具有刚体运动不变性，这对几何匹配和形状分析任务至关重要。我们的贡献有三方面：1）将广义切片器引入切片GW框架；2）构建基于切片的高效GW运输计划；3）开发一种摊销变体，用学习到的切片器替代每个实例的优化，适用于未见过的输入对。我们在动物网格匹配、马网格插值和ShapeNet部件迁移任务上进行实验。结果表明，min-GSGW在显著更低的计算成本下生成有意义的几何对应关系和GW目标值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational intractability of the Gromov--Wasserstein problem, this work introduces min Generalized Sliced Gromov--Wasserstein (min-GSGW), which employs expressive generalized slicers to learn coupled nonlinear mappings that project input measures into compatible domains. These slicers enable monotone couplings in the projected space to lift to transport plans evaluated against the GW objective in original spaces, allowing direct minimization of GW cost without full optimization. The method is shown to be rigid-motion invariant, enhancing its suitability for geometric matching tasks. Experiments on animal mesh matching, horse interpolation, and ShapeNet part transfer demonstrate that min-GSGW generates meaningful geometric correspondences while achieving significantly faster computation than existing GW solvers.
为解决Gromov-Wasserstein问题计算复杂度高的问题，本文提出min Generalized Sliced Gromov-Wasserstein（min-GSGW），利用表达能力强的广义切片器学习耦合非线性映射，将输入测度投影到兼容空间。这些切片器使投影空间中的单调耦合可提升为原始空间中基于GW目标的运输计划，从而直接最小化GW成本。该方法具备刚体运动不变性，更适用于几何匹配任务。在动物网格匹配、马网格插值和ShapeNet部件迁移实验中，min-GSGW生成了有意义的几何对应关系，同时计算成本显著低于现有GW求解器。
Learning Responsibility-Attributed Adversarial Scenarios for Testing Autonomous Vehicles
Authors: Yizhuo Xiao, Haotian Yan, Ying Wang, Zhongpan Zhu, Yuxin Zhang, Xintao Yan, Mustafa Suphi Erden, Cheng Wang
First: 2026-05-13T16:33:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:33:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Establishing trustworthy safety assurance for autonomous driving systems (ADSs) requires evidence that failures arise from avoidable system deficiencies rather than unavoidable traffic conflicts. Current adversarial simulation methods can efficiently expose collisions, but generally lack mechanisms to distinguish these fundamentally different failure modes. Here we present CARS (Context-Aware, Responsibility-attributed Scenario generation), a framework that integrates responsibility attribution directly into adversarial scenario generation. CARS combines context-aware adversary selection with a generative adversarial policy optimized in closed-loop simulation to construct collision scenarios that are both physically feasible and diagnostically attributable. Across benchmark datasets spanning heterogeneous national traffic environments, CARS consistently discovers feasible collision scenarios with high attribution rates under multiple regulation-prescribed careful and competent driver models. By coupling adversarial generation with normative responsibility assessment, CARS moves simulation testing beyond collision discovery toward the construction of interpretable, regulation-aligned safety evidence for scalable ADS validation.

标题：学习责任归因的对抗性场景以测试自动驾驶汽车
为建立可信的自动驾驶系统（ADS）安全保证，需证明故障源于可避免的系统缺陷而非不可避免的交通冲突。当前对抗性仿真方法虽能高效暴露碰撞，但普遍缺乏区分这两种根本不同故障模式的机制。本文提出CARS（上下文感知、责任归因场景生成）框架，将责任归因直接整合到对抗性场景生成中。CARS结合上下文感知的对手选择与在闭环仿真中优化的生成对抗策略，构建既物理可行又可诊断归因的碰撞场景。在涵盖异质国家交通环境的基准数据集上，CARS在多种法规规定的谨慎且称职驾驶员模型下，持续发现高归因率的可行碰撞场景。通过将对抗性生成与规范性责任评估结合，CARS使仿真测试从碰撞发现迈向构建可解释、符合法规的安全证据，以支持可扩展的ADS验证。

CARS uses context-aware adversary selection and a generative adversarial policy trained in closed-loop simulation to produce physically feasible collision scenarios that can be clearly attributed to system failures rather than unavoidable traffic conflicts. Experimental results across diverse national traffic datasets show that CARS consistently generates collision scenarios with high attribution rates under multiple regulatory driver models, demonstrating its ability to produce interpretable, regulation-aligned safety evidence for scalable autonomous vehicle validation.
