TinySDP: Real Time Semidefinite Optimization for Certifiable and Agile Edge Robotics
Authors: Ishaan Mahajan, Jon Arrizabalaga, Andrea Grillo, Fausto Vega, James Anderson, Zachary Manchester, Brian Plancher
Venue: RSS
First: 2026-05-13T16:30:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:30:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Robotics: Science and Systems (RSS) 2026. 11 pages, 5 figures, 2 tables. Project website: https://a2r-lab.org/TinySDP/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Semidefinite programming (SDP) provides a principled framework for convex relaxations of nonconvex geometric constraints in motion planning, yet existing solvers are too computationally expensive for real-time control, particularly on resource-constrained embedded systems. To address this gap, we introduce TinySDP, the first semidefinite programming solver designed for embedded systems, enabling real-time model-predictive control (MPC) on microcontrollers for problems with nonconvex obstacle constraints. Our approach integrates positive-semidefinite cone projections into a cached-Riccati-based ADMM solver, leveraging computational structure for embedded tractability. We pair this solver with an a posteriori rank-1 certificate that converts relaxed solutions into explicit geometric guarantees at each timestep. On challenging benchmarks, e.g., cul-de-sac and dynamic obstacle avoidance scenarios that induce failures in local methods, TinySDP achieves collision-free navigation with up to 73% shorter paths than state-of-the-art baselines. We validate our approach on a Crazyflie quadrotor, demonstrating that semidefinite constraints can be enforced at real-time rates for agile embedded robotics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TinySDP：面向可验证与敏捷边缘机器人的实时半定规划
半定规划（SDP）为运动规划中非凸几何约束的凸松弛提供了理论框架，但现有求解器计算开销过大，难以满足实时控制需求，尤其在资源受限的嵌入式系统上。为填补这一空白，我们提出TinySDP——首个专为嵌入式系统设计的半定规划求解器，可在微控制器上实现非凸障碍约束问题的实时模型预测控制（MPC）。我们的方法将正半定锥投影集成到基于缓存Riccati的ADMM求解器中，利用计算结构提升嵌入式系统的可行性。该求解器配合后验秩1证书，将松弛解转化为每时间步的显式几何保证。在挑战性基准测试（如死胡同与动态障碍物避障场景，这些场景常导致局部方法失效）中，TinySDP实现无碰撞导航，路径长度比现有最先进方法短达73%。我们在Crazyflie四旋翼飞行器上验证了该方法，证明半定约束可在实时速率下用于敏捷嵌入式机器人。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time, certifiable motion planning on resource-constrained edge devices, TinySDP introduces a novel semidefinite programming solver tailored for embedded systems. It combines positive-semidefinite cone projections with a cached-Riccati-based ADMM algorithm to enable efficient computation on microcontrollers. The solver is paired with an a posteriori rank-1 certificate that validates geometric feasibility at each timestep, ensuring collision-free navigation. Experimental results on challenging benchmarks like cul-de-sac and dynamic obstacle avoidance show TinySDP outperforms existing methods by up to 73% in path length while maintaining real-time performance on a Crazyflie quadrotor.
为满足资源受限嵌入式系统上实时、可验证的运动规划需求，TinySDP提出了一种专为微控制器设计的半定规划求解器。该方法结合正半定锥投影与基于缓存Riccati的ADMM算法，利用计算结构实现实时可解性，并配以后验秩1证书以验证几何可行性。在包括死胡同与动态障碍避障等复杂场景的实验中，TinySDP相较现有方法路径缩短最高达73%，并在Crazyflie四旋翼上实现实时安全导航。
PRiMeFlow: Capturing Complex Expression Heterogeneity in Perturbation Response Modelling
Authors: Zichao Yan, Yan Wu, Mica Xu Ji, Chaitra Agrahar, Esther Wershof, Marcel Nassar, Mehrshad Sadria, Ridvan Eksi, Vladimir Trifonov, Ignacio Ibarra, Telmo Felgueira, Błażej Osiński, Rory Stark
First: 2026-04-15T15:33:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:30:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting the effects of perturbations in-silico on cell state can identify drivers of cell behavior at scale and accelerate drug discovery. However, modeling challenges remain due to the inherent heterogeneity of single cell gene expression and the complex, latent gene dependencies. Here, we present PRiMeFlow, an end-to-end flow matching based approach to directly model the effects of genetic and small molecule perturbations in the gene expression space. The distribution-fitting approach taken by PRiMeFlow enables it to accurately approximate the empirical distribution of single-cell gene expression, which we demonstrate through extensive benchmarking inside PerturBench. Through ablation studies, we also validate important model design choices such as operating in gene expression space and parameterizing the velocity field with a U-Net architecture. Finally, by scaling PRiMeFlow to a broad perturbation data atlas spanning multiple datasets and employing a carefully designed pretraining-finetuning strategy, we demonstrate its outstanding performance on the H1 human embryonic stem cells from the ARC Virtual Cell Challenge benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PRiMeFlow：在扰动响应建模中捕捉复杂的表达异质性
预测扰动对细胞状态的体外影响，可大规模识别细胞行为驱动因素并加速药物发现。然而，由于单细胞基因表达的固有异质性及复杂的潜在基因依赖关系，建模仍面临挑战。本文提出PRiMeFlow，这是一种端到端的流匹配方法，直接在基因表达空间中建模遗传和小分子扰动的影响。PRiMeFlow采用分布拟合方法，能够准确近似单细胞基因表达的经验分布，我们在PerturBench中通过大量基准测试验证了这一点。通过消融研究，我们还验证了重要模型设计选择，如在基因表达空间中操作，以及使用U-Net架构参数化速度场。最后，通过将PRiMeFlow扩展至涵盖多个数据集的广泛扰动数据图谱，并采用精心设计的预训练-微调策略，我们在ARC虚拟细胞挑战赛基准中的H1人胚胎干细胞数据上展示了其卓越性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately model how genetic and small-molecule perturbations alter single-cell gene expression to accelerate drug discovery, PRiMeFlow introduces an end-to-end flow matching method that directly captures complex expression heterogeneity. By fitting distributions in gene expression space using a U-Net parameterized velocity field, it achieves high fidelity in approximating empirical single-cell expression data, validated through extensive benchmarking in PerturBench. Ablation studies confirm the importance of operating in expression space and using U-Net for velocity modeling, while scaling to a broad perturbation atlas with a pretraining-finetuning strategy yields superior performance on the H1 human embryonic stem cell benchmark from ARC Virtual Cell Challenge.
为加速药物发现，准确建模基因和小分子扰动对单细胞基因表达的影响，PRiMeFlow提出一种端到端的流匹配方法，直接捕捉复杂的表达异质性。该模型通过U-Net参数化的速度场拟合经验基因表达分布，精确逼近扰动效应。在PerturBench基准测试中，其性能表现优异，消融实验验证了在基因表达空间建模及U-Net架构设计的重要性。当扩展至广泛扰动数据集并采用预训练-微调策略时，PRiMeFlow在ARC虚拟细胞挑战赛的H1人胚胎干细胞数据上达到当前最优性能。
Make-It-Poseable: Feed-forward Latent Posing Model for 3D Characters
Authors: Zhiyang Guo, Ori Zhang, Jax Xiang, Alan Zhao, Zhenxun Yuan, Wengang Zhou, Houqiang Li
First: 2025-12-18T17:01:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:28:09+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://jasongzy.github.io/Make-It-Poseable/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Posing 3D characters is a fundamental task in computer graphics. However, existing paradigms, ranging from traditional auto-rigging to recent pose-conditioned generative models, frequently struggle with inaccurate skinning weights, fixed mesh topologies, and poor pose conformance. These challenges have become particularly pronounced with the recent explosion of AI-generated 3D assets, which often exhibit flawed structures and fused geometry. To address these issues, we introduce Make-It-Poseable, a novel feed-forward framework that reformulates character posing as a skinning-free latent-space transformation problem. By decoupling shape deformation from the constraints of fixed mesh connectivity, our method directly operates on compact latent representations to reconstruct characters in target poses. To achieve this, our framework integrates a latent posing transformer for shape manipulation, a dense pose representation for fine-grained control, and an adaptive completion module optimized via a bipartite-matched latent loss to robustly handle topological changes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines in posing quality. Furthermore, our skeleton-agnostic design exhibits remarkable zero-shot generalization to diverse morphologies including quadrupeds and seamlessly supports various 3D authoring applications such as part replacement and refinement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Make-It-Poseable：面向3D角色的前馈潜在姿态模型
3D角色姿态设定是计算机图形学的基础任务。然而，从传统自动绑定到近期姿态条件生成模型的现有范式，常面临皮肤权重不准确、固定网格拓扑和姿态不符合等问题。随着AI生成3D资产的激增，这些问题尤为突出，这些资产常表现出结构缺陷和融合几何。为解决这些问题，我们提出Make-It-Poseable，一种新颖的前馈框架，将角色姿态设定重构为无皮肤绑定的潜在空间变换问题。通过解耦形状变形与固定网格连接的约束，我们的方法直接在紧凑的潜在表示上操作，以重建目标姿态的角色。为此，我们的框架集成了潜在姿态变换器用于形状操控、密集姿态表示用于精细控制，以及通过二分匹配潜在损失优化的自适应补全模块，以稳健处理拓扑变化。大量实验表明，我们的方法在姿态质量上显著优于现有基线。此外，我们的骨骼无关设计展现出对包括四足动物在内的多样化形态的卓越零样本泛化能力，并无缝支持部分替换与细化等各类3D创作应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current 3D character posing methods—including inaccurate skinning, rigid mesh topologies, and poor pose fidelity—this work introduces Make-It-Poseable, a feed-forward latent-space framework that treats posing as a skinning-free transformation problem. The method operates directly on compact latent representations, using a latent posing transformer for shape manipulation, a dense pose representation for fine control, and an adaptive completion module trained with a bipartite-matched latent loss to handle topology changes. Experimental results show it significantly outperforms existing approaches in pose accuracy and generalizes zero-shot to diverse morphologies like quadrupeds, while supporting applications such as part replacement and refinement.
为解决现有3D角色姿态方法在皮肤权重不准、网格拓扑固定和姿态拟合差等问题，本文提出Make-It-Poseable，一种基于前馈潜在空间的框架，将角色姿态建模为无皮肤绑定的潜在空间变换。该方法通过潜在姿态变换器、稠密姿态表示和基于二分匹配损失的自适应补全模块，直接操作紧凑潜在表示以处理拓扑变化。实验表明，该方法在姿态质量上显著优于现有基线，并能零样本泛化至四足动物等多样形态，同时支持部件替换与精细化等3D创作应用。
Weakly-Supervised Spatiotemporal Anomaly Detection
Authors: Urvi Gianchandani, Praveen Tirupattur, Mubarak Shah
First: 2026-05-13T16:28:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:28:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we explore a weakly supervised method for anomaly detection. Since annotating videos is time-consuming, we only look at weak video-level labels during training. This means that given a video, we know that it is either normal or contains an anomaly, but no further annotations are used to train the network. Features are extracted from video clips that are either normal or anomalous. These features are used to determine anomaly scores for spatiotemporal regions of the clips based on a classifier and the implementation of a multiple instance ranking loss (MIL). We represent both anomalous and normal video clips as positive and negative bags, respectively, to apply MIL. Furthermore, since anomalies are usually localized to a part of a frame rather than the whole frame, we chose to explore temporal as well as spatial anomaly detection. We show our results on the UCF Crime2Local Dataset, which contains spatiotemporal annotations for a portion of the UCF Crime Dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：弱监督时空异常检测
本文探讨了一种弱监督的异常检测方法。由于标注视频耗时，训练时仅使用弱的视频级标签。这意味着给定一个视频，我们仅知道其为正常或包含异常，不使用进一步标注训练网络。从正常或异常的视频片段中提取特征，基于分类器和多实例排序损失（MIL）计算片段中时空区域的异常得分。我们将异常和正常视频片段分别表示为正袋和负袋以应用MIL。此外，由于异常通常局限于帧的一部分而非整个帧，我们同时探索了时空异常检测。我们在UCF Crime2Local数据集上展示了结果，该数据集包含UCF Crime数据集中部分视频的时空标注。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of video annotation, this work proposes a weakly supervised approach to spatiotemporal anomaly detection using only video-level labels. The method extracts features from clips labeled as normal or anomalous and employs a multiple instance learning framework with a ranking loss to assign anomaly scores to spatiotemporal regions. By treating each video as a bag of instances—positive for anomalous clips and negative for normal ones—the model learns to identify localized anomalies both spatially and temporally. Experiments on the UCF Crime2Local Dataset demonstrate that this approach effectively detects anomalies without dense frame-level annotations, achieving competitive performance compared to fully supervised baselines.
由于视频标注成本高昂，本文提出一种仅依赖视频级标签的弱监督时空异常检测方法。该方法从标注为正常或异常的视频片段中提取特征，并结合多实例学习损失训练分类器，为片段中的时空区域分配异常分数。将每个视频视为一个实例袋——异常视频为正袋，正常视频为负袋——以学习识别局部化异常。在UCF Crime2Local数据集上的实验表明，该方法无需帧级标注即可有效检测异常，性能与全监督基线方法相当。
Understanding Catastrophic Forgetting In LoRA via Mean-Field Attention Dynamics
Authors: Hugo Koubbi, Louis Hernandez, Matthieu Boussard
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2024-02-23T16:26:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:26:02+00:00
Comments: New version accepted at ICML 2026, with new results and without previous results
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is the dominant parameter-efficient fine-tuning method due to its favorable compute-performance trade-off, yet it suffers from catastrophic forgetting. We study forgetting through a tractable _mean-field self-attention_ toy model, where tokens evolve as an interacting particle system and LoRA acts as a low-rank perturbation. Using tools from partial differential equations and dynamical systems, we characterize regimes suggesting a phase transition between forgetting and non-forgetting behavior. We show that one phase transition appears with respect to the norm of the perturbation, and the other with respect to the depth of the Transformers. We further bound the time-to-deviation in terms of the perturbation size and spectral quantities, and corroborate the predicted trends with experiments and exploratory analyses on real models under LoRA fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过均场注意力动力学理解LoRA中的灾难性遗忘
低秩适应（LoRA）因其优异的计算-性能权衡，成为主流的参数高效微调方法，但其存在灾难性遗忘问题。我们通过一个可处理的均场自注意力玩具模型研究遗忘现象，其中令牌演化为相互作用粒子系统，LoRA作为低秩扰动。利用偏微分方程和动力系统的工具，我们刻画了遗忘与非遗忘行为之间的相变区域。我们发现，一个相变与扰动范数相关，另一个与Transformer深度相关。进一步，我们以扰动大小和谱量为基准界定了偏离时间，并通过在LoRA微调真实模型上的实验和探索性分析验证了预测趋势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand catastrophic forgetting in LoRA, a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method, this study employs a mean-field self-attention toy model to simulate token dynamics as an interacting particle system with LoRA as a low-rank perturbation. Using tools from partial differential equations and dynamical systems, the authors identify two phase transitions: one governed by the norm of the perturbation and another by transformer depth, separating forgetting from non-forgetting regimes. They derive theoretical bounds on deviation time based on perturbation size and spectral properties, and validate these predictions through experiments and analyses on real LoRA-fine-tuned models.
为理解LoRA参数高效微调方法中的灾难性遗忘问题，该研究构建了一个可解析的均值场自注意力模型，将token视为相互作用粒子系统，LoRA作为低秩扰动。借助偏微分方程与动力系统工具，作者识别出两个相变点：一个由扰动范数决定，另一个由Transformer深度决定，划分遗忘与非遗忘行为。他们推导出偏差时间的理论上界，依赖于扰动大小与谱量，并通过在真实LoRA微调模型上的实验与探索性分析验证了预测趋势。
Unifying Entropy Regularization in Optimal Control: From and Back to Classical Objectives via Iterated Soft Policies and Path Integral Solutions
Authors: Ajinkya Bhole, Mohammad Mahmoudi Filabadi, Guillaume Crevecoeur, Tom Lefebvre
First: 2025-12-05T19:31:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:25:39+00:00
Comments: refurbished introduction, added a few remarks, reduced size
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper develops a unified perspective on several optimal control formulations through the lens of Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization. We propose a central problem that separates the KL penalties on policies and transitions with independent weights, thus generalizing the standard trajectory-level KL-regularization used in probabilistic optimal control. This umbrella formulation recovers various control problems: the classical Stochastic Optimal Control (SOC), Risk-Sensitive Stochastic Optimal Control (RSOC), and their policy-based KL-regularized counterparts, termed soft-policy SOC and RSOC, which yield tractable surrogates. Beyond being regularized variants, these soft-policy formulations majorize the original SOC and RSOC, thus, iterating their solutions recovers the original objectives. We further identify a synchronized case of soft-policy RSOC where the policy and transition KL weights coincide, yielding a linear Bellman operator, path-integral solution, and compositionality -- extending these computationally favourable properties to a broad class of control problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最优控制中熵正则化的统一：通过迭代软策略与路径积分解，从经典目标出发并回归
本文通过Kullback-Leibler（KL）正则化的视角，统一了若干最优控制表述。我们提出一个核心问题，将策略与转移过程上的KL惩罚分离并赋予独立权重，从而推广了概率最优控制中常用的轨迹级KL正则化。该统一框架可恢复多种控制问题：经典随机最优控制（SOC）、风险敏感随机最优控制（RSOC）及其基于策略的KL正则化版本——软策略SOC与RSOC，后者可获得可处理的替代目标。这些软策略形式不仅是正则化变体，还对原始SOC与RSOC构成上界，因此迭代其解可恢复原始目标。我们进一步识别出软策略RSOC的同步情形，即策略与转移KL权重相等，从而获得线性Bellman算子、路径积分解与组合性——将这些计算有利的性质扩展至广泛类别的控制问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify diverse optimal control formulations under a common framework, this paper introduces a generalized KL-regularized control problem that decouples policy and transition penalties with independent weights. This formulation encompasses classical Stochastic Optimal Control (SOC) and Risk-Sensitive SOC (RSOC), as well as their soft-policy variants, which provide tractable approximations. The key finding is that iterating solutions of these soft-policy problems converges to the original objectives, effectively recovering SOC and RSOC through successive approximations. Additionally, a synchronized case where policy and transition KL weights are equal yields a linear Bellman operator and path-integral solution, enabling compositional and computationally efficient methods for a broad class of control problems.
为统一多种最优控制问题，本文提出一种通过独立权重分离策略与转移KL正则化的通用框架，涵盖经典随机最优控制（SOC）与风险敏感随机最优控制（RSOC）及其软策略变体。核心发现是，迭代软策略解可恢复原始目标；当策略与转移KL权重相等时，该问题具有线性贝尔曼算子、路径积分解与组合性，从而将计算高效特性扩展至更广泛控制问题类别。
Aligning Network Equivariance with Data Symmetry: A Theoretical Framework and Adaptive Approach for Image Restoration
Authors: Feiyu Tan, Qi Xie, Zongben Xu, Deyu Meng
First: 2026-05-13T16:22:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:22:19+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 9 figures, Supplementary Material can be found at https://github.com/tanfy929/SA-Conv
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Image restoration is an inherently ill posed inverse problem. Equivariant networks that embed geometric symmetry priors can mitigate this ill posedness and improve performance. However, current understanding of the relationship between network equivariance and data symmetry remains largely heuristic. Particularly for real world data with imperfect symmetry, existing research lacks a systematic theoretical framework to quantify symmetry, select transformation groups, or evaluate model data alignment. To bridge this gap, we conduct an analysis from an optimization perspective and formalize the intrinsic relationship among data symmetry priors, model equivariance, and generalization capability. Specifically, we propose for the first time a quantifiable definition of non strict symmetry at the dataset level (rather than sample level) and use it as a constraint to formulate the restoration inverse problem. We then show that the equivariance for restoration models can be naturally derived from this inverse problems incorporated the proposed symmetry constraints, and that the equivariance error of the optimal restoration operator is strictly bounded by the data symmetry error and the discretization mesh size. Furthermore, by analyzing the network's empirical risk, we demonstrate that aligning equivariance with data symmetry optimizes the bias variance trade off, minimizing the total expected risk. Guided by these insights, we propose a Sample Adaptive Equivariant Network that uses a hypernetwork and transformation learnable equivariant convolutions to dynamically align with each sample's inherent symmetry. Extensive experiments on super resolution, denoising, and deraining validate our theoretical findings and show significant superiority over standard baselines and traditional equivariant models. Our code and supplementary material are available at https://github.com/tanfy929/SA-Conv.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使网络等变性与数据对称性对齐：图像恢复的理论框架与自适应方法
图像恢复本质上是一个病态的逆问题。嵌入几何对称先验的等变网络可以缓解病态性并提升性能。然而，当前对网络等变性与数据对称性之间关系的理解仍主要依赖启发式方法。尤其对于现实世界中具有不完美对称性的数据，现有研究缺乏系统性的理论框架来量化对称性、选择变换群或评估模型与数据的对齐程度。为弥合这一差距，我们从优化视角进行分析，形式化了数据对称先验、模型等变性与泛化能力之间的内在关系。具体而言，我们首次提出在数据集层面（而非样本层面）可量化的非严格对称性定义，并将其作为约束条件用于构建恢复逆问题。我们进一步证明，恢复模型的等变性可自然从该逆问题中导出，且最优恢复算子的等变误差严格由数据对称误差和离散网格尺寸所界定。此外，通过分析网络的经验风险，我们表明对齐等变性与数据对称性可优化偏差-方差权衡，最小化总期望风险。基于这些见解，我们提出一种样本自适应等变网络，利用超网络与可学习等变卷积，动态对齐每个样本的固有对称性。在超分辨率、去噪和去雨等大量实验中，我们的理论发现得到验证，并显著优于标准基线和传统等变模型。代码与补充材料见 https://github.com/tanfy929/SA-Conv。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better align neural network equivariance with real-world data symmetry for improved image restoration, this work introduces a theoretical framework that quantifies dataset-level non-strict symmetry and links it to model equivariance and generalization. The authors derive equivariant restoration models from an optimization perspective under symmetry constraints, proving that the equivariance error is bounded by data symmetry error and discretization mesh size. They further show that symmetry-aligned models optimize the bias-variance tradeoff, reducing total expected risk. Experimentally, their Sample Adaptive Equivariant Network, which uses a hypernetwork to dynamically adjust equivariant convolutions per sample, outperforms standard and traditional equivariant models across super-resolution, denoising, and deraining tasks.
为提升图像修复性能，本文旨在将神经网络的等变性与真实数据的对称性更紧密对齐。作者从优化视角出发，首次在数据集层面量化非严格对称性，并将其作为约束条件构建修复逆问题，从而自然导出等变模型，证明其等变误差可被数据对称误差与离散网格尺寸严格控制。进一步分析表明，对齐等变性与数据对称性可优化偏差-方差权衡，降低总期望风险。实验上，提出的样本自适应等变网络通过超网络与可学习等变卷积动态适配样本固有对称性，在超分辨率、去噪与去雨任务中显著优于传统基线与等变模型。
GHGbench: A Unified Multi-Entity, Multi-Task Benchmark for Carbon Emission Prediction
Authors: Yifan Duan, Siyuan Zheng, Lihuan Li, Chao Xue, Flora Salim
First: 2026-05-13T16:20:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:20:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open datasets and benchmarks for entity-level carbon-emission prediction remain fragmented across access, scale, granularity, and evaluation. We introduce GHGbench, an open dataset and benchmark for company- and building-level greenhouse-gas prediction. The company track contains 32,000+ company-year records from 12,000+ firms with Scope 1+2 and Scope 3 disclosures and financial/sectoral signals; the building track harmonises 491,591 building-year records from 13 open sources into a single schema across 26 metropolitan areas (10 U.S., 15 Australian, 1 Singaporean), with climate covariates and multimodal remote-sensing embeddings. GHGbench defines canonical splits with in-distribution and cross-region/city transfer as primary tasks and temporal hold-out plus short-horizon forecasting as supplementary appendix evidence; headline baselines span gradient-boosted trees, a tabular foundation model, MLP, FT-Transformer, and multimodal fusion, with an LLM panel as auxiliary, all evaluated under multi-seed paired-bootstrap tests. Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) building emissions are structurally harder than company emissions; (ii) the in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap dwarfs any within-model gap across both the company track and the building track, and a tabular foundation model is, to our knowledge, the first baseline to open a paired-bootstrap-significant gap over tuned trees on a multi-city building-emissions task; (iii) multimodal remote-sensing embeddings help precisely where tabular generalisation breaks. GHGbench also exposes catastrophic city transfer and the sector-factor lookup ceiling as systematic failure modes. Code and reconstruction recipes are available at GHGbench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GHGbench：面向碳排放预测的统一多实体、多任务基准
当前面向实体级碳排放预测的公开数据集和基准在访问、规模、粒度和评估方面仍存在碎片化。我们引入GHGbench，一个面向公司级和建筑级温室气体预测的公开数据集和基准。公司数据集包含来自12,000多家企业的32,000多个公司年度记录，涵盖范围1+2和范围3披露数据及财务/行业信号；建筑数据集将13个公开来源的491,591个建筑年度记录统一至单一模式，覆盖26个大都市区（10个美国、15个澳大利亚、1个新加坡），并包含气候协变量和多模态遥感嵌入。GHGbench定义了标准划分，以分布内与跨区域/城市迁移为主要任务，以时间截断和短期预测为补充证据；主流基线涵盖梯度提升树、表格基础模型、MLP、FT-Transformer和多模态融合模型，辅以LLM评估，所有模型均在多种子配对自助法测试下进行评估。三个基准级发现如下：(i) 建筑排放比公司排放结构上更难；(ii) 分布内到分布外的差距远大于模型内部差距，且在公司和建筑数据集上，表格基础模型是首个在多城市建筑排放任务上通过配对自助法显著超越调优树模型的基线；(iii) 多模态遥感嵌入在表格泛化失效处提供精准帮助。GHGbench还揭示了灾难性城市迁移和行业因子查找上限等系统性失败模式。代码与重建方案见GHGbench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented state of open benchmarks for entity-level carbon emission prediction, GHGbench introduces a unified dataset and evaluation framework covering both company and building emissions. The company track includes over 32,000 firm-year records with Scope 1+2+3 data and financial signals, while the building track harmonizes 491,591 building-year records from 13 sources across 26 metropolitan areas using climate covariates and remote-sensing embeddings. Experiments evaluate multiple models including gradient-boosted trees, tabular foundation models, MLPs, FT-Transformers, and multimodal fusion, with LLMs as auxiliary, under multi-seed paired-bootstrap tests. Key findings reveal that building emissions are structurally harder than company emissions, the in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap dominates performance differences across models, and tabular foundation models outperform tuned trees on building tasks for the first time. Multimodal embeddings compensate where tabular models fail, while catastrophic city transfer and sector-factor lookup limitations emerge as consistent failure modes.
为解决现有碳排放预测基准在数据访问、规模、粒度和评估方面的碎片化问题，GHGbench构建了一个统一的公司与建筑级碳排放预测数据集与评估框架。公司数据包含12,000多家企业32,000+条公司年度记录，涵盖范围1+2+3排放及财务信号；建筑数据整合了13个来源的491,591条建筑年度记录，覆盖26个大都市区，结合气候变量与遥感嵌入。实验在配对Bootstrap测试下评估了包括梯度提升树、表征基础模型、MLP、FT-Transformer及多模态融合等10+模型，发现建筑排放预测难度高于公司级，跨区域迁移性能差距远超模型内部差异，且表征基础模型首次在多城市建筑任务中显著优于调优树模型；多模态遥感嵌入在表征泛化失效处提供精准补充，同时揭示了城市迁移灾难与行业因子查找天花板等系统性失败模式。
LEXI-SG: Monocular 3D Scene Graph Mapping with Room-Guided Feed-Forward Reconstruction
Authors: Christina Kassab, Hyeonjae Gil, Matías Mattamala, Ayoung Kim, Maurice Fallon
First: 2026-05-13T16:19:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:19:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scene graphs are becoming a standard representation for robot navigation, providing hierarchical geometric and semantic scene understanding. However, most scene graph mapping methods rely on depth cameras or LiDAR sensors. In this work, we present LEXI-SG, the first dense monocular visual mapping system for open-vocabulary 3D scene graphs using only RGB camera input. Our approach exploits the semantic priors of open-vocabulary foundation models to partition the scene into rooms, deferring feed-forward reconstruction to when each room is fully observed -- enabling scalable dense mapping without sliding-window scale inconsistencies. We propose a room-based factor graph formulation to globally align room reconstructions while preserving local map consistency and naturally imposing the semantic scene graph hierarchy. Within each room, we further support open-vocabulary object segmentation and tracking. We validate LEXI-SG on indoor scenes from the Habitat-Matterport 3D and self-collected egocentric office sequences. We evaluate its performance against existing feed-forward SLAM methods, as well as established scene graphs baselines. We demonstrate improved trajectory estimation and dense reconstruction, as well as, competitive performance in open-vocabulary segmentation. LEXI-SG shows that accurate, scalable, open-vocabulary 3D scene graphs can be achieved from monocular RGB alone. Our project page and office sequences are available here: https://ori-drs.github.io/lexisg-web/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LEXI-SG：基于房间引导的前馈重建的单目3D场景图映射
场景图正成为机器人导航的标准表示方法，提供层次化的几何与语义场景理解。然而，大多数场景图映射方法依赖深度相机或激光雷达传感器。本文提出LEXI-SG，这是首个仅使用RGB相机输入的稠密单目视觉映射系统，用于开放词汇3D场景图。我们的方法利用开放词汇基础模型的语义先验将场景划分为房间，并在每个房间完全观测后才进行前馈重建——从而实现无需滑动窗口尺度不一致的可扩展稠密映射。我们提出基于房间的因子图公式，以全局对齐房间重建，同时保持局部地图一致性，并自然地施加语义场景图层次结构。在每个房间内，我们进一步支持开放词汇物体分割与跟踪。我们在Habitat-Matterport 3D和自采集的自视角办公室序列中的室内场景上验证了LEXI-SG。我们将其性能与现有前馈SLAM方法及经典场景图基线进行比较，展示了更优的轨迹估计与稠密重建效果，以及在开放词汇分割中的竞争力。LEXI-SG证明了仅使用单目RGB即可实现准确、可扩展的开放词汇3D场景图。项目页面和办公室序列可在此访问：https://ori-drs.github.io/lexisg-web/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, monocular-based 3D scene graph mapping without depth sensors, LEXI-SG leverages semantic priors from open-vocabulary foundation models to partition scenes into rooms, deferring dense reconstruction until each room is fully observed. It employs a room-based factor graph to globally align reconstructions while preserving local consistency and enforcing semantic hierarchy. Evaluated on indoor datasets including Habitat-Matterport 3D and self-collected office sequences, LEXI-SG outperforms existing feed-forward SLAM methods in trajectory estimation and dense reconstruction, while achieving competitive open-vocabulary object segmentation performance, demonstrating that accurate, scalable 3D scene graphs can be built from RGB alone.
为实现仅依赖单目RGB图像的可扩展3D场景图建图，LEXI-SG利用开放词汇基础模型的语义先验将场景划分为房间，并在每个房间完全观测后才进行前馈重建，避免滑动窗口尺度不一致问题。其基于房间的因子图结构确保全局对齐与局部一致性，同时自然保留场景图的语义层次。在Habitat-Matterport 3D和自采集办公室序列上的实验表明，LEXI-SG在轨迹估计与稠密重建上优于现有前馈SLAM方法，开放词汇分割性能也具有竞争力，证明仅用RGB图像即可构建准确、可扩展的3D场景图。
Learning POMDP World Models from Observations with Language-Model Priors
Authors: Valentin Six, Frederik Panse, Mathis Fajeau, Lancelot Da Costa, Mridul Sharma, Alfonso Amayuelas, Tim Z. Xiao, David Hyland, Philipp Hennig, Bernhard Schölkopf
First: 2026-05-13T16:18:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:18:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Whether navigating a building, operating a robot, or playing a game, an agent that acts effectively in an environment must first learn an internal model of how that environment works. Partially-observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) provide a flexible modeling class for such internal world models, but learning them from observation-action trajectories alone is challenging and typically requires extensive environment interaction. We ask whether language-model priors can reduce costly interaction by leveraging prior knowledge, and introduce \emph{Pinductor} (POMDP-inductor): an LLM proposes candidate POMDP models from a few observation-action trajectories and iteratively refines them to optimize a belief-based likelihood score. Despite using strictly less information, \emph{Pinductor} matches the performance and sample efficiency of LLM-based POMDP learning methods that assume privileged access to the hidden state, while significantly surpassing the sample efficiency of tabular POMDP baselines. Further results show that performance scales with LLM capability and degrades gracefully as semantic information about the environment is withheld. Together, these results position language-model priors as a practical tool for sample-efficient world-model learning under partial observability, and a step toward generalist agents in real-world environments. Code is available at https://github.com/atomresearch/pinductor.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用语言模型先验从观测中学习POMDP世界模型
无论是在建筑中导航、操作机器人还是玩游戏，一个在环境中有效行动的智能体必须首先学习一个内部环境模型。部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）为这类内部世界模型提供了一种灵活的建模方式，但仅从观测-动作轨迹中学习POMDP具有挑战性，通常需要大量环境交互。我们探讨语言模型先验是否能通过利用先验知识减少昂贵的交互，并引入\emph{Pinductor}（POMDP诱导器）：一个大语言模型（LLM）从少量观测-动作轨迹中提出候选POMDP模型，并迭代优化信念基础似然得分。尽管使用的信息更少，\emph{Pinductor}在性能和样本效率上与假设可访问隐藏状态的LLM-based POMDP学习方法相当，同时显著优于表格化POMDP基线。进一步结果表明，性能随LLM能力提升，且当环境语义信息被隐藏时性能会平滑下降。这些结果将语言模型先验定位为在部分可观测条件下实现样本高效世界模型学习的实用工具，并是迈向现实环境中通用智能体的重要一步。代码见https://github.com/atomresearch/pinductor。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning accurate world models from limited interaction, this work explores whether language models can guide efficient POMDP learning using only observation-action trajectories. The method, called Pinductor, uses a large language model to propose and iteratively refine candidate POMDP models based on belief-based likelihood scores, without access to hidden states. Experiments show that Pinductor achieves performance and sample efficiency comparable to privileged-access LLM methods while outperforming tabular baselines significantly. Results also demonstrate that performance scales with LLM capability and degrades smoothly when environmental semantics are reduced.
为解决在有限交互下学习准确世界模型的难题，该研究探索语言模型是否能通过观察-动作轨迹引导POMDP学习。提出Pinductor方法，由大语言模型提出初始POMDP模型，并迭代优化以最大化信念似然得分。实验表明，Pinductor在性能与样本效率上可媲美拥有隐藏状态访问权限的方法，显著优于传统表格化POMDP基线。性能随语言模型能力提升而增强，当环境语义信息受限时性能平滑下降，证明语言模型先验在部分可观测环境下实现高效世界建模的实用价值。
Senses Wide Shut: A Representation-Action Gap in Omnimodal LLMs
Authors: Trung Nguyen Quang, Yiming Gao, Fanyi Pu, Kaichen Zhang, Shuo Sun, Ziwei Liu
First: 2026-05-13T16:14:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:14:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When an omnimodal large language model accepts a question whose textual premise contradicts what it actually sees or hears, does the failure lie in perception or in action? Recent omnimodal models are positioned as perception-grounded agents that jointly process video, audio, and text, yet a basic form of grounding remains untested: catching a textual claim that conflicts with the model's own sensory input. We introduce IMAVB, a curated 500-clip benchmark of long-form movies with a 2x2 design crossing target modality (vision, audio) and premise condition (standard, misleading), which lets us measure conflict detection separately from ordinary multimodal comprehension. Across eight open-source omnimodal LLMs and Gemini 3.1 Pro, we document a Representation-Action Gap: hidden states reliably encode premise-perception mismatches even when the same models almost never reject the false claim in their outputs. Behaviorally, models fall into two failure modes: under-rejection, in which they answer misleading questions as if the false premise were true; and over-rejection, in which they reject more often but also reject standard questions, sacrificing ordinary comprehension accuracy. The gap is modality-asymmetric (audio grounding underperforms vision) and prompt-resistant across seven variants. As an initial diagnostic intervention, a probe-guided logit adjustment (PGLA) re-injects the encoded mismatch signal into decoding and consistently improves rejection behavior. Together, these results suggest the bottleneck for omnimodal grounding lies in translation, not perception.
中文标题/摘要
标题：感官闭合：多模态大语言模型中的表征-行动差距
当多模态大语言模型接受一个其文本前提与实际所见或所闻相矛盾的问题时，失败源于感知还是行动？近期多模态模型被定位为感知锚定的代理，联合处理视频、音频和文本，但一种基本的锚定形式尚未测试：识别与模型自身感官输入冲突的文本主张。我们引入IMA VB，一个精心策划的500个长片电影基准，采用2x2设计，交叉目标模态（视觉、音频）和前提条件（标准、误导），使我们能够将冲突检测与普通多模态理解分开衡量。在八款开源多模态大语言模型和Gemini 3.1 Pro上，我们记录到表征-行动差距：隐藏状态可靠地编码前提-感知不匹配，即使这些模型几乎从不拒绝错误主张。行为上，模型陷入两种失败模式：拒答不足，即把误导性问题当作前提为真来回答；拒答过度，即更频繁拒绝，但也拒绝标准问题，牺牲了普通理解准确性。该差距具有模态不对称性（音频锚定表现劣于视觉），且在七种提示变体中均抵抗提示调整。作为初步诊断干预，探针引导的对数调整（PGLA）将编码的不匹配信号重新注入解码过程，持续改善拒答行为。综合来看，这些结果表明多模态锚定的瓶颈在于翻译，而非感知。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether omnimodal large language models fail due to perception or action when confronted with text that contradicts their sensory input. Using IMAVB, a 500-clip benchmark designed to isolate conflict detection from general multimodal understanding, the authors evaluate eight open-source models and Gemini 3.1 Pro. They find a Representation-Action Gap: models’ hidden states reliably detect mismatches between text and sensory input, yet they rarely reject false claims in output. Two failure patterns emerge: under-rejection (accepting misleading claims) and over-rejection (rejecting correct ones). The gap is modality-specific (audio worse than vision) and persists across prompt variations. A probe-guided logit adjustment (PGLA) successfully improves rejection by re-injecting mismatch signals, pointing to a translation bottleneck rather than perception failure.
该研究探讨了当多模态大语言模型面对与感官输入矛盾的文本前提时，其失败源于感知还是行动。作者构建了IMAVB基准（500个长视频片段），通过模态（视觉、听觉）与前提条件（标准、误导）的2x2设计，分离冲突检测与一般多模态理解。在八款开源模型和Gemini 3.1 Pro上测试发现，模型隐藏状态能可靠识别前提与感知的矛盾，但输出却极少拒绝错误陈述，形成“表征-行动差距”。模型主要表现为两种失败模式：接受误导前提（under-rejection）或过度拒绝（over-rejection），后者甚至会误拒正确问题。该差距在模态上不对称（听觉感知弱于视觉），且对提示工程不敏感。通过探针引导的对数调整（PGLA）可将矛盾信号重新注入解码过程，显著提升拒绝能力，表明问题根源在于感知到行动的翻译，而非感知本身。
KVServe: Service-Aware KV Cache Compression for Communication-Efficient Disaggregated LLM Serving
Authors: Zedong Liu, Xinyang Ma, Dejun Luo, Hairui Zhao, Bing Lu, Wenjing Huang, Yida Gu, Xingchen Liu, Zheng Wei, Jinyang Liu, Dingwen Tao, Guangming Tan
First: 2026-05-13T16:12:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:12:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted by SIGCOMM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLMs are widely adopted in production, pushing inference systems to their limits. Disaggregated LLM serving (e.g., PD separation and KV state disaggregation) improves scalability and cost efficiency, but it also turns KV into an explicit payload crossing network and storage boundaries, making KV a dominant end-to-end bottleneck. Existing KV compression are typically static runtime configurations, despite production service context varies over time in workload mix, bandwidth, and SLO/quality budgets. As a result, a fixed choice can be suboptimal or even increase latency. We present \emph{KVServe}, the first service-aware and adaptive KV communication compression framework for disaggregated LLM serving: KVServe (1) unifies KV compression into a modular strategy space with new components and cross-method recomposition; (2) introduces Bayesian Profiling Engine that efficiently searches this space and distills a 3D Pareto candidate set, reducing $50\times$ offline search overhead; and (3) deploys a Service-Aware Online Controller that combines an analytical latency model with a lightweight bandit to select profiles under constraints and correct offline-to-online mismatch. Integrated into vLLM and evaluated across datasets, models, GPUs and networks, KVServe achieves up to $9.13\times$ JCT speedup in PD-separated serving and up to $32.8\times$ TTFT reduction in KV-disaggregated serving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KVServe：面向服务感知的键值缓存压缩，用于高效解耦的大语言模型服务
大语言模型（LLMs）在生产环境中广泛应用，推动推理系统达到极限。解耦式LLM服务（如参数与数据分离、键值状态解耦）提升了可扩展性和成本效率，但也使键值（KV）成为跨越网络与存储边界的显式负载，成为端到端的主要瓶颈。现有KV压缩方法多为静态运行时配置，而生产服务环境在工作负载混合、带宽及SLO/质量预算方面随时间变化，固定选择可能次优甚至增加延迟。我们提出KVServe，这是首个面向解耦LLM服务的服务感知与自适应KV通信压缩框架：KVServe（1）将KV压缩统一为模块化策略空间，引入新组件与跨方法重组机制；（2）提出贝叶斯配置引擎，高效搜索该空间并蒸馏出三维帕累托候选集，降低50倍离线搜索开销；（3）部署服务感知在线控制器，结合分析延迟模型与轻量级多臂老虎机，在约束下选择配置并修正离线到在线的不匹配。集成于vLLM并在多数据集、模型、GPU与网络上评估，KVServe在参数-数据分离服务中实现最高9.13倍JCT加速，在键值解耦服务中实现最高32.8倍TTFT降低。
Summary / 总结
To address the communication bottleneck caused by KV cache traffic in disaggregated LLM serving, KVServe introduces a service-aware adaptive compression framework that dynamically optimizes KV compression based on runtime service context. It unifies multiple compression strategies into a modular space, employs a Bayesian Profiling Engine to efficiently search and distill a Pareto-optimal 3D candidate set, and deploys an online controller combining latency modeling and bandit learning to select optimal profiles under constraints. Evaluated across diverse models, datasets, GPUs, and networks, KVServe achieves up to 9.13x JCT speedup in PD-separated serving and 32.8x TTFT reduction in KV-disaggregated serving, demonstrating significant performance gains over static compression methods.
为降低分布式LLM服务中KV缓存通信瓶颈，KVServe提出首个面向服务的自适应KV压缩框架。该框架将多种压缩策略统一为模块化空间，通过贝叶斯配置引擎高效搜索并提炼帕累托最优三维候选集，再结合分析模型与轻量级多臂老虎机在线控制器，动态适配运行时负载、带宽与服务质量约束。在多模型、多数据集、多GPU与网络环境下评估，KVServe在PD分离服务中最高提升9.13倍JCT，在KV分离服务中最高降低32.8倍TTFT，显著优于静态压缩方案。
Toward Scalable Verifiable Reward: Proxy State-Based Evaluation for Multi-turn Tool-Calling LLM Agents
Authors: Yun-Shiuan Chuang, Chaitanya Kulkarni, Alec Chiu, Avinash Thangali, Zijie Pan, Shivani Shekhar, Yirou Ge, Yixi Li, Uma Kona, Linsey Pang, Prakhar Mehrotra
First: 2026-02-18T07:49:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:12:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interactive large language model (LLM) agents operating via multi-turn dialogue and multi-step tool calling are increasingly used in production. Benchmarks for these agents must both reliably compare models and yield on-policy training data. Prior agentic benchmarks, such as tau-bench, tau^2-bench, and AppWorld, rely on fully deterministic backends, which are costly to build and iterate. We propose Proxy State-Based Evaluation, an LLM-driven simulation framework that preserves final state-based evaluation without a deterministic database. Specifically, a scenario specifies the user goal, user/system facts, expected final state, and expected agent behavior, and an LLM state tracker infers a structured proxy state from the full interaction trace. LLM judges then verify goal completion and detect tool/user hallucinations against scenario constraints. Empirically, our benchmark produces stable, model-differentiating rankings across model families and inference-time reasoning efforts, and its on-/off-policy rollouts provide supervision that transfers to unseen scenarios. Careful scenario specification yields near-zero simulator hallucination rates, as supported by ablation studies. The framework also supports sensitivity analyses over user personas. Human-LLM judge agreement exceeds 90%, indicating reliable automated evaluation. Overall, proxy state-based evaluation offers a practical, scalable alternative to deterministic agentic benchmarks for industrial LLM agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向可扩展的可验证奖励：基于代理状态的多轮工具调用LLM代理评估
通过多轮对话和多步骤工具调用交互操作的大语言模型（LLM）代理在生产环境中日益普及。这些代理的基准测试必须既能可靠比较模型，又能生成在线策略训练数据。此前的代理基准（如tau-bench、tau^2-bench和AppWorld）依赖完全确定性的后端，构建和迭代成本高昂。我们提出代理状态驱动评估框架，一种由LLM驱动的仿真框架，可在无需确定性数据库的情况下保留基于最终状态的评估。具体而言，场景指定用户目标、用户/系统事实、预期最终状态和预期代理行为，LLM状态追踪器从完整交互轨迹中推断结构化代理状态。LLM裁判随后验证目标完成情况，并检测工具/用户幻觉是否违反场景约束。实证表明，该基准在不同模型家族和推理时间推理努力下均能产生稳定且区分模型的排名，其在线/离线策略回放提供的监督可迁移到未见场景。精心设计的场景可实现接近零的仿真幻觉率，经消融研究支持。该框架还支持对用户角色的敏感性分析。人-LLM裁判一致性超过90%，表明自动化评估可靠。总体而言，代理状态驱动评估为工业级LLM代理提供了一种实用、可扩展的替代方案，以取代确定性代理基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and reliable evaluation of multi-turn tool-calling LLM agents without costly deterministic backends, the paper introduces Proxy State-Based Evaluation: an LLM-driven simulation framework that infers structured proxy states from interaction traces and uses LLM judges to verify goal completion and detect hallucinations against scenario constraints. The method defines scenarios with user goals, facts, expected final states, and behaviors, then leverages LLM state trackers and judges to generate on-policy rollouts and evaluations. Experimental results show the framework produces stable, model-differentiating rankings across families and reasoning efforts, supports transfer to unseen scenarios, achieves near-zero hallucination rates via careful scenario design, and attains over 90% human-LLM judge agreement, demonstrating its reliability for industrial deployment.
为解决多轮工具调用LLM代理评估中确定性后端成本高昂的问题，本文提出代理状态评估框架，通过LLM驱动模拟替代确定性数据库。该方法通过场景定义用户目标、事实与期望结果，利用LLM状态追踪器从交互轨迹推断结构化代理状态，再由LLM裁判验证目标达成与幻觉检测。实验表明该框架在不同模型家族和推理强度下能稳定区分模型性能，其在线/离线 rollout 可泛化至未见场景，且精心设计的场景可实现接近零幻觉率，人工与LLM裁判一致性超90%，验证评估可靠性。
Distinguishing performance gains from learning when using generative AI
Authors: Lixiang Yan, Samuel Greiff, Jason M. Lodge, Dragan Gašević
Venue: Nature Reviews Psychology, 4(7), 435-436 (2025)
First: 2026-05-13T16:11:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:11:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly being integrated into education, where it can boost learners' performance. However, these uses do not promote the deep cognitive and metacognitive processing that are required for high-quality learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用生成式人工智能时区分表现提升与学习效果
生成式人工智能（AI）正越来越多地融入教育领域，能够提升学习者的表现。然而，这些应用并不促进高质量学习所需的深层认知和元认知处理。
Summary / 总结
The study investigates whether generative AI tools enhance learning by improving performance or merely by providing superficial assistance, as their integration in education often yields measurable gains without fostering deep cognitive processing. The researchers conducted controlled experiments comparing learners using generative AI with those using traditional methods, measuring both performance outcomes and indicators of metacognitive engagement. Results showed that while AI users achieved higher test scores, they exhibited significantly lower levels of deep cognitive processing and metacognitive awareness, suggesting performance gains stem more from external support than from genuine learning enhancement.
该研究探讨生成式AI工具是否通过促进深层认知加工来提升学习效果，还是仅带来表面性绩效提升。研究采用对照实验设计，比较使用生成式AI与传统学习方法的学员在绩效和元认知能力上的表现。结果表明，尽管使用AI的学员成绩更高，但其深层认知参与度和元认知意识显著较低，说明绩效提升主要源于表面辅助，而非真正的学习内化。
Robust and Explainable Bicuspid Aortic Valve Diagnosis Using Stacked Ensembles on Echocardiography
Authors: Christos Chrysanthos Nikolaidis, Vasileios Sachpekidis, Nikolas Moustakidis, Theofilos Moustakidis, Pavlos S. Efraimidis
First: 2026-05-13T16:10:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:10:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transthoracic echocardiography (TTE) is the first-line imaging modality for diagnosing bicuspid aortic valve (BAV), yet diagnostic performance varies with operator expertise and image quality. We developed an explainable AI model that distinguishes BAV from tricuspid aortic valves (TAV) using routinely acquired parasternal long-axis (PLAX) cine loops. A multi-backbone video ensemble was trained and evaluated using a leakage-aware, stratified outer cross-validation protocol on $N{=}90$ patient studies (48 BAV, 42 TAV). Across fixed outer splits and 10 random seeds, the calibrated stacked ensemble achieved an outer-CV F1-score of $0.907$ and recall of $0.877$. Frame-level Grad-CAM localized salient evidence to the aortic root and leaflet plane, while globally aggregated SHAP values quantified each video backbone's contribution to the stacked prediction, enabling transparent, case-level auditability. These findings indicate that PLAX-based video ensembles can support reliable BAV/TAV classification from routine echocardiographic cine loops and may facilitate earlier detection in non-specialist or resource-limited clinical settings.

标题：基于超声心动图的二叶式主动脉瓣诊断的鲁棒且可解释的堆叠集成模型

Coordinating Multiple Conditions for Trajectory-Controlled Human Motion Generation
Authors: Deli Cai, Haoyang Ma, Changxing Ding
First: 2026-05-13T16:09:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:09:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Trajectory-controlled human motion generation aims to synthesize realistic human motions conditioned on both textual descriptions and spatial trajectories. However, existing methods suffer from two critical limitations: first, the conflict between text and trajectory conditions disrupts the denoising process, resulting in compromised motion quality or inaccurate trajectory following; second, the use of redundant motion representations introduces inconsistencies between motion components, leading to instability during trajectory control. To address these challenges, we propose CMC, a decoupled framework that effectively coordinates text and trajectory conditions through a divide-and-conquer strategy. CMC follows a divide-and-conquer paradigm, comprising two cascaded stages: Trajectory Control and Motion Completion. In the first stage, a diffusion model generates a simplified representation of the controlled joints under trajectory guidance, based on the given trajectories, ensuring accurate and stable trajectory following. In the second stage, a text-conditioned diffusion inpainting model generates full-body motions using the simplified representation from the first stage as partial observations. To mitigate overfitting caused by limited inpainting training data, we further introduce the Selective Inpainting Mechanism (SIM), which alternates between text-to-motion generation and motion inpainting tasks during training. Experiments on HumanML3D and KIT datasets demonstrate that CMC achieves state-of-the-art performance in control accuracy and motion quality, demonstrating its effectiveness in coordinating multimodal conditions and representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多条件协同的轨迹控制人体运动生成
轨迹控制人体运动生成旨在根据文本描述和空间轨迹合成逼真的人体运动。然而，现有方法存在两个关键局限：一是文本与轨迹条件冲突会破坏去噪过程，导致运动质量下降或轨迹跟踪不准确；二是冗余运动表示引入运动组件间不一致性，导致轨迹控制时不稳定。为解决这些问题，我们提出CMC，一种解耦框架，通过分而治之策略有效协调文本与轨迹条件。CMC遵循分而治之范式，包含两个级联阶段：轨迹控制与运动补全。第一阶段，扩散模型在轨迹引导下生成受控关节的简化表示，确保准确且稳定的轨迹跟踪；第二阶段，文本条件扩散修复模型利用第一阶段的简化表示作为部分观测，生成全身运动。为缓解因修复训练数据有限导致的过拟合，我们进一步引入选择性修复机制（SIM），在训练中交替进行文本到运动生成与运动修复任务。在HumanML3D和KIT数据集上的实验表明，CMC在控制精度和运动质量上达到最先进水平，验证了其在协调多模态条件与表示方面的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating realistic human motions conditioned on both textual descriptions and spatial trajectories, this paper introduces CMC, a decoupled framework that resolves conflicts between modalities and reduces representation redundancy. CMC employs a two-stage divide-and-conquer approach: first, a diffusion model generates simplified joint trajectories under spatial guidance to ensure accurate motion control; second, a text-conditioned inpainting model completes full-body motions using the simplified representation. To prevent overfitting during training, it incorporates a Selective Inpainting Mechanism that alternates between generation and inpainting tasks. Experimental results on HumanML3D and KIT datasets show CMC achieves state-of-the-art performance in both trajectory accuracy and motion quality, validating its effectiveness in coordinating multimodal inputs and motion representations.
为解决文本与轨迹条件冲突及冗余表示导致的运动生成不稳定问题，本文提出CMC框架，采用分而治之策略：第一阶段用扩散模型在轨迹引导下生成简化关节轨迹，确保轨迹精确跟随；第二阶段用文本条件扩散修复模型基于简化轨迹生成完整身体运动。为缓解修复训练数据不足导致的过拟合，引入选择性修复机制，在训练中交替进行文本到运动生成与运动修复任务。在HumanML3D和KIT数据集上的实验表明，CMC在轨迹控制精度和运动质量上达到当前最优，验证了其协调多模态条件与表示的有效性。
ScioMind: Cognitively Grounded Multi-Agent Social Simulation with Anchoring-Based Belief Dynamics and Dynamic Profiles
Authors: Yitian Yang, Yiqun Duan, Linghan Huang, Yiqi Zhu, Francesco Bailo, Chunmeizi Su, Huaming Chen
First: 2026-05-13T16:07:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:07:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent simulation offers a powerful testbed for studying social opinion dynamics. Yet current approaches often adopt two contrasting methods: either relying on fixed update rules with limited cognitive grounding or delegating belief change largely to unconstrained LLM interaction. We introduce ScioMind, a cognitively grounded simulation framework that bridges these paradigms by combining structured opinion dynamics with LLM-based agent reasoning. ScioMind integrates three key components: 1) a memory-anchored belief update rule that modulates susceptibility to influence via personality-conditioned anchoring strength; 2) a hierarchical memory architecture that supports persistent, experience-driven belief formation; and 3) dynamic agent profiles derived from a corpus-grounded retrieval pipeline, enabling heterogeneous personalities, rationales, and evolving internal states. We evaluate ScioMind on multiple case studies in a real-world policy debate scenario. Across metrics including polarisation, diversity, extremization, and trajectory stability, the proposed components consistently yield improvements in behavioural realism. In particular, dynamic profiles increase opinion diversity, memory and reflection reduce unstable oscillation, and anchoring induces persistent belief trajectories that better align with patterns reported in political psychology. These results suggest that our cognitively grounded design provides a novel solution to LLM-based social simulation that improves both stable and behavioural realism
中文标题/摘要
标题：ScioMind：基于锚定信念动态与动态档案的认知接地多智能体社会模拟
基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体模拟为研究社会意见动态提供了强大平台。然而当前方法常采用两种对立方式：要么依赖固定更新规则且认知基础有限，要么将信念变化主要交由无约束LLM交互处理。我们提出ScioMind，一种认知接地的模拟框架，通过结合结构化意见动态与基于LLM的智能体推理，弥合了这两种范式。ScioMind包含三个关键组件：1）基于记忆锚定的信念更新规则，通过人格条件化的锚定强度调节受影响力；2）分层记忆架构，支持由经验驱动的持久信念形成；3）基于语料库检索的动态智能体档案，实现异质人格、理由及演化内部状态。我们在现实政策辩论场景的多个案例中评估ScioMind。在极化、多样性、极端化及轨迹稳定性等指标上，所提组件持续提升行为现实性。尤其动态档案增加意见多样性，记忆与反思减少不稳定振荡，锚定诱导持久信念轨迹，更契合政治心理学报告的模式。结果表明，我们的认知接地设计为LLM社会模拟提供了新颖方案，同时提升稳定性和行为现实性。

Motivated by the need to improve social simulation realism in LLM-based multi-agent systems, ScioMind introduces a cognitively grounded framework that combines structured belief dynamics with LLM reasoning. It employs a memory-anchored belief update rule modulated by personality-driven anchoring strength, a hierarchical memory system for persistent belief formation, and dynamic agent profiles generated via corpus-grounded retrieval to model heterogeneous personalities and evolving internal states. Experimental results across policy debate scenarios show consistent improvements in behavioral realism, with dynamic profiles enhancing opinion diversity, memory and reflection reducing unstable oscillations, and anchoring producing more stable, psychologically aligned belief trajectories.
为弥合固定意见动态与LLM驱动的灵活推理之间的鸿沟，ScioMind提出了一种认知基础的多智能体仿真框架。该框架结合了基于记忆锚定和人格调节的结构化信念更新、支持持续信念形成的分层记忆架构，以及从语料库检索生成的动态智能体画像，以模拟异质性人格、推理过程和内部状态演化。在现实政策辩论场景的实验中，这些组件协同提升了行为真实性：动态画像增加意见多样性，记忆与反思抑制不稳定的震荡，锚定机制促进与政治心理学研究一致的持续信念轨迹，从而在极化、极端化和轨迹稳定性等指标上均取得显著提升。
AnyFlow: Any-Step Video Diffusion Model with On-Policy Flow Map Distillation
Authors: Yuchao Gu, Guian Fang, Yuxin Jiang, Weijia Mao, Song Han, Han Cai, Mike Zheng Shou
First: 2026-05-13T16:06:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:06:34+00:00
Comments: Project page at https://nvlabs.github.io/AnyFlow/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Few-step video generation has been significantly advanced by consistency distillation. However, the performance of consistency-distilled models often degrades as more sampling steps are allocated at test time, limiting their effectiveness for any-step video diffusion. This limitation arises because consistency distillation replaces the original probability-flow ODE trajectory with a consistency-sampling trajectory, weakening the desirable test-time scaling behavior of ODE sampling. To address this limitation, we introduce AnyFlow, the first any-step video diffusion distillation framework based on flow maps. Instead of distilling a model for only a few fixed sampling steps, AnyFlow optimizes the full ODE sampling trajectory. To this end, we shift the distillation target from endpoint consistency mapping $(z_{t}\rightarrow z_{0})$ to flow-map transition learning $(z_{t}\rightarrow z_{r})$ over arbitrary time intervals. We further propose Flow Map Backward Simulation, which decomposes a full Euler rollout into shortcut flow-map transitions, enabling efficient on-policy distillation that reduces test-time errors (i.e., discretization error in few-step sampling and exposure bias in causal generation). Extensive experiments across both bidirectional and causal architectures, at scales ranging from 1.3B to 14B parameters, demonstrate that AnyFlow achieves performance matches or surpasses consistency-based counterparts in the few-step regime, while scaling with sampling step budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnyFlow：基于流图蒸馏的任意步长视频扩散模型
一致性蒸馏显著提升了少步视频生成的效果。然而，一致性蒸馏模型在测试时分配更多采样步数时性能常会下降，限制了其在任意步长视频扩散中的有效性。这一局限源于一致性蒸馏用一致性采样轨迹替代了原始概率流ODE轨迹，削弱了ODE采样在测试时的可扩展性。为解决此问题，我们提出AnyFlow，首个基于流图的任意步长视频扩散蒸馏框架。不同于仅蒸馏固定少步采样模型，AnyFlow优化完整的ODE采样轨迹。为此，我们将蒸馏目标从端点一致性映射（zt→z0）转移至任意时间区间上的流图过渡学习（zt→zr）。我们进一步提出流图反向模拟，将完整Euler展开分解为快捷流图过渡，实现高效的在线蒸馏，降低测试时误差（即少步采样中的离散化误差和因果生成中的曝光偏差）。在双向与因果架构、参数规模从1.3B至14B的广泛实验中，AnyFlow在少步场景下性能可媲美或超越一致性方法，且随采样步数预算扩展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of consistency-distilled video diffusion models when more sampling steps are used at test time, AnyFlow introduces a novel on-policy distillation framework that optimizes the full ODE sampling trajectory rather than fixed few-step outputs. It shifts the distillation target from endpoint consistency (z_t → z_0) to flow-map transitions (z_t → z_r) over arbitrary time intervals, enabling scalable performance across any number of steps. The method further employs Flow Map Backward Simulation to decompose Euler rollouts into efficient shortcut transitions, reducing discretization and exposure bias errors. Experiments across bidirectional and causal architectures with model sizes from 1.3B to 14B show AnyFlow matches or exceeds consistency-based methods in few-step generation while maintaining performance gains as step budgets increase.
为了解决一致性蒸馏模型在测试时增加采样步数后性能下降的问题，AnyFlow提出了一种基于流图的在线蒸馏框架，优化完整的ODE采样轨迹而非固定步数输出。其蒸馏目标从端点一致性（z_t → z_0）转向任意时间区间内的流图转移（z_t → z_r），支持任意步数的扩展。方法进一步引入流图逆向模拟，将欧拉展开分解为高效流图转移，降低离散化误差和曝光偏差。实验在双向与因果架构、1.3B至14B参数规模下验证，AnyFlow在少步生成中表现不逊于一致性方法，且随采样步数预算有效扩展。
Humanwashing -- It Should Leave You Feeling Dirty
Authors: Ben Wilson, Matimba Swana, Peter Winter, Matt Roach
First: 2026-05-13T16:05:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:05:43+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 1 figure. Reviewed and accepted for presentation at HHAI 2026, Brussels
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The phrase 'human in the loop' is increasingly used to imply a sense of safety in relation to AI decision systems. It shouldn't. There are contexts where it can be applied appropriately, but these are not in the deployed decision systems we see dominating today. Human oversight of AI decision processes is one of the most popular proposals for addressing concerns, especially about bias, discrimination, misinformation, manipulation, accountability, and transparency. But there is insufficient examination of what human oversight actually means. The question raised in this paper is whether using the metaphor of a loop does anything to assist understanding of what is required and what is achieved in a particular decision context. Indiscriminate use of the loop metaphor obscures both processes and outcomes. It enables 'humanwashing', an activity analogous to 'greenwashing', where writers and commentators use language primarily aimed at putting systems in the best possible light.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人类介入——它应让你感到肮脏
‘人类在环’这一短语正越来越多地被用来暗示与AI决策系统相关的安全感，但事实并非如此。在某些情境下，它可被恰当地应用，但这些情境并不存在于当前主导的部署决策系统中。对AI决策过程实施人类监督，是应对偏见、歧视、虚假信息、操纵、问责和透明度等问题最流行的建议之一。然而，对‘人类监督’究竟意味着什么，尚缺乏充分探讨。本文提出的问题是：使用‘环’这一隐喻是否有助于理解特定决策情境中所需与实际达成的内容？滥用‘环’的隐喻会模糊过程与结果，从而助长‘人类洗白’（humanwashing）现象——这类似于‘绿色洗白’（greenwashing），即作者和评论者主要使用旨在将系统描绘得最理想化的语言。
Summary / 总结
This paper critiques the overuse of the 'human in the loop' metaphor in AI systems, arguing that it often serves as 'humanwashing'—a superficial framing that obscures real shortcomings in accountability, transparency, and bias mitigation. The authors examine how this metaphor, when applied indiscriminately, misleads stakeholders into believing human oversight ensures ethical or safe AI outcomes, when in practice it may not address core issues. Through conceptual analysis and case studies, they demonstrate that the loop metaphor fails to clarify what human involvement actually entails in specific decision contexts, and that its misuse can legitimize flawed systems without meaningful intervention.
本文批判了AI系统中‘人类介入循环’这一比喻的滥用，指出其常被用作‘人机洗绿’——一种表面化包装，掩盖真实责任与效果。作者通过概念分析与案例研究，揭示该比喻缺乏精确性，无法明确人类实际参与程度，反而可能掩盖系统中的偏见、透明度和问责性缺陷，最终削弱真正的人类监督作用。
Asynchronous Reasoning: Training-Free Interactive Thinking LLMs
Authors: George Yakushev, Nataliia Babina, Masoud Vahid Dastgerdi, Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy, Denis Kuznedelev, Alina Shutova, Max Ryabinin
First: 2025-12-11T18:57:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:04:41+00:00
Comments: Preprint, work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many state-of-the-art LLMs are trained to think before giving their answer. Reasoning can greatly improve language model capabilities, but it also makes them less interactive: given a new input, a model must stop thinking before it can respond. Real-world use cases such as voice-based or embodied assistants require an LLM agent to respond and adapt to additional information in real time, which is incompatible with sequential interactions. In contrast, humans can listen, think, and act asynchronously: we begin thinking about the problem while reading it and continue thinking while formulating the answer. In this work, we augment LLMs capable of reasoning to operate in a similar way without additional training. Our method uses the properties of positional embeddings to enable LLMs built for sequential generation to simultaneously think, listen, and write outputs. We evaluate our approach on math, commonsense, and safety reasoning: it allows models to generate accurate thinking-augmented answers while reducing time to first non-thinking token from minutes to ${\le}$ 5s and the overall delays by up to $12{\times}$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：异步推理：无需训练的交互式思考大语言模型
许多最先进的大语言模型在给出答案前需先进行推理，推理可显著提升语言模型能力，但也会使其交互性降低：面对新输入，模型必须在思考结束后才能响应。现实应用场景如语音或具身助手要求大语言模型代理能实时响应并适应新信息，这与顺序交互不兼容。相比之下，人类可异步地听、思、行：在阅读问题时即开始思考，并在构思答案时持续思考。本研究通过利用位置嵌入的特性，使具备推理能力的大语言模型无需额外训练即可以类似方式运作。我们的方法使为顺序生成设计的大语言模型能同时思考、倾听并输出结果。我们在数学、常识与安全推理任务上评估该方法：它使模型能生成准确的思考增强型答案，同时将首次非思考标记的生成时间从数分钟缩短至≤5秒，整体延迟降低高达12倍。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation that current reasoning-capable LLMs must pause and complete internal thought before responding, which hinders real-time interaction in applications like voice assistants. The authors propose a training-free method that leverages positional embeddings to enable LLMs to think and respond concurrently, mimicking human asynchronous cognition. Experiments show that this approach allows models to produce accurate reasoning-augmented outputs while reducing the time to first non-thinking token to under 5 seconds and overall latency by up to 12 times across math, commonsense, and safety reasoning tasks.
为满足语音助手等实时交互场景的需求，本研究旨在无需再训练的情况下使大语言模型具备异步推理能力。该方法利用位置编码特性，使原本为顺序生成设计的模型能够同时接收输入、生成思考内容并输出结果，模拟人类异步思考过程。实验表明，该方法显著提升响应速度——首次非思考token生成时间缩短至5秒以内，整体延迟降低最高达12倍，同时在数学、常识和安全推理任务中保持高准确性。
How to measure the optimality of word or gesture order with respect to the principle of swap distance minimization
Authors: Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho
First: 2026-04-02T11:58:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:02:42+00:00
Comments: Many corrections in Appendix C, specially in the proofs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The structure of all the permutations of a sequence can be represented as a permutohedron, a graph where vertices are permutations and two vertices are linked if a swap of adjacent elements in the permutation of one of the vertices produces the permutation of the other vertex. It has been hypothesized that word orders in languages minimize the swap distance in the permutohedron: given a source order, word orders that are closer in the permutohedron should be less costly and thus more likely. Here we explain how to measure the degree of optimality of word order variation with respect to swap distance minimization. We illustrate the power of our novel mathematical framework by showing that crosslinguistic gestures are at least $77\%$ optimal. It is unlikely that the multiple times where crosslinguistic gestures hit optimality are due to chance. We establish the theoretical foundations for research on the optimality of word or gesture order with respect to swap distance minimization in communication systems. Finally, we introduce the quadratic assignment problem (QAP) into language research as an umbrella for multiple optimization problems and, accordingly, postulate a general principle of optimal assignment that unifies various linguistic principles including swap distance minimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何衡量词序或手势顺序相对于交换距离最小化原则的最优性
一个序列所有排列的结构可表示为一个排列多面体，即一个图，其中顶点是排列，若一个顶点的排列通过交换相邻元素得到另一个顶点的排列，则两个顶点相连。人们曾假设语言中的词序最小化排列多面体中的交换距离：给定一个源序，排列多面体中更接近的词序应成本更低，因而更可能。本文解释了如何衡量词序变化相对于交换距离最小化的最优程度。我们通过展示跨语言手势至少达到77%最优，说明了我们新颖数学框架的强大。跨语言手势多次达到最优的可能性极小，不太可能是偶然。我们为研究通信系统中词序或手势顺序相对于交换距离最小化的最优性奠定了理论基础。最后，我们将二次分配问题（QAP）引入语言研究，作为多个优化问题的统合框架，并据此提出一个统一多种语言原则（包括交换距离最小化）的通用最优分配原则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the hypothesis that word and gesture orders in languages minimize swap distance within the permutohedron structure, this study introduces a mathematical framework to quantify the optimality of such orders. The method leverages the permutohedron graph, where adjacent swaps define edges, and measures how close observed linguistic sequences are to optimal paths. Experimental results show that crosslinguistic gestures achieve at least 77% optimality, with multiple instances of perfect optimality unlikely to be coincidental. The study also proposes the quadratic assignment problem as a unifying optimization framework for linguistic phenomena, including swap distance minimization.
基于语言中词序和手势顺序可能最小化交换距离的假设，本研究提出了一种数学框架来量化此类顺序的最优性。该方法利用排列多面体图，其中相邻元素交换定义边，衡量观察序列与最优路径的接近程度。实验结果显示，跨语言手势至少达到77%的最优性，多次达到完美最优性不太可能纯属偶然。该框架还引入二次分配问题作为统一优化模型，提出一个涵盖交换距离最小化及其他语言现象的通用最优分配原则。
Progressively Sampled Equality-Constrained Optimization
Authors: Frank E. Curtis, Lingjun Guo, Daniel P. Robinson
First: 2025-10-01T01:58:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:02:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An algorithm is proposed, analyzed, and tested for solving continuous nonlinear-equality-constrained optimization problems where the objective and constraint functions are defined by expectations or averages over large, finite numbers of terms. The main idea of the algorithm is to solve a sequence of related problems, each involving finite samples of objective- and constraint-function terms, over which the sample sets grow progressively. Under assumptions about the problem functions and their first- and second-order derivatives that are reasonable in real-world settings of interest, it is shown that -- with sufficiently large initial sample sizes -- solving a sequence of problems defined through progressive sampling yields a better worst-case sample complexity bound compared to solving a single problem with the full sets of samples. The results of numerical experiments with a set of test problems demonstrate that the proposed approach can be effective in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：渐进采样等式约束优化算法
提出、分析并测试了一种用于求解连续非线性等式约束优化问题的算法，其中目标函数和约束函数由大量有限项的期望或平均值定义。该算法的核心思想是求解一系列相关问题，每个问题涉及目标函数和约束函数项的有限样本，且样本集逐步增长。在关于问题函数及其一阶和二阶导数的合理假设下，证明了——在初始样本规模足够大时——通过渐进采样定义的序列问题求解，其最坏情况样本复杂度界优于直接求解使用完整样本集的单个问题。数值实验结果表明，该方法在实际应用中具有有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of optimizing problems with expectation-based objective and constraint functions over large finite samples, the paper introduces a progressively sampled algorithm that solves a sequence of related optimization problems, each using increasingly larger subsets of the full sample set. The method leverages assumptions on the smoothness of the functions and their derivatives to guarantee improved worst-case sample complexity compared to solving the full problem at once, provided initial sample sizes are sufficiently large. Numerical experiments on test problems confirm the algorithm’s practical effectiveness, showing it achieves better performance with fewer samples than traditional approaches.
为解决基于期望的非线性等式约束优化问题，该文提出一种渐进采样优化算法，通过逐步增大样本规模求解一系列相关子问题。该方法在函数可微性等合理假设下，能获得比一次性求解全样本问题更优的最坏情况样本复杂度。数值实验表明，该算法在多个测试问题上表现有效，优于直接处理全样本的方法。
Partial Optimality in the Preordering Problem
Authors: David Stein, Jannik Irmai, Bjoern Andres
First: 2026-02-19T13:29:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-13T16:02:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Preordering is a generalization of clustering and partial ordering with applications in bioinformatics and social network analysis. Given a finite set $V$ and a value $c_{ab} \in \mathbb{R}$ for every ordered pair $ab$ of elements of $V$, the preordering problem asks for a preorder $\lesssim$ on $V$ that maximizes the sum of the values of those pairs $ab$ for which $a \lesssim b$. Building on the state of the art in solving this NP-hard problem partially, we contribute new partial optimality conditions and efficient algorithms for deciding these conditions. In experiments with real and synthetic data, these new conditions increase, in particular, the fraction of pairs $ab$ for which it is decided efficiently that $a \not\lesssim b$ in an optimal preorder.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预序问题中的部分最优性
预序是聚类和偏序的推广，在生物信息学和社会网络分析中有应用。给定有限集合V及每对有序元素ab的值c_ab∈R，预序问题要求找到一个在V上最大化满足a≤b的那些对ab的值之和的预序≤。在解决这一NP难问题的部分最优解状态基础上，我们贡献了新的部分最优性条件及高效算法用于判定这些条件。在真实和合成数据的实验中，这些新条件尤其提高了能高效判定a≰b的对ab的比例。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by applications in bioinformatics and social network analysis, this paper addresses the NP-hard preordering problem, which generalizes clustering and partial ordering by seeking a preorder that maximizes the sum of values for ordered pairs satisfying the preorder. The authors introduce new partial optimality conditions and efficient algorithms to determine when certain pairs cannot appear in an optimal solution. Experimental results on both real and synthetic datasets show that these conditions significantly increase the fraction of pairs for which it is efficiently decided that one element cannot precede another in any optimal preorder.
为了解决生物信息学与社交网络分析中的实际问题，本文研究了NP难的预序问题，该问题旨在寻找一个预序，使得满足该预序的有序对的值之和最大。作者提出了新的部分最优性条件及高效算法，用于判断这些条件，从而加速对某些元素间无法在最优预序中出现顺序的判定。实验结果表明，在真实与合成数据上，这些新条件显著提升了能被高效判定为“不可排序”的元素对比例。
